### [79] [Blind Adaptive Equalization in Additive Impulsive Noise Using the Logarithmic Product Fractional-Moment (LP-FM) Criterion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18478)
*Shafayat Abrar*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对数乘积分数阶矩统计量（LP-FMS）的盲均衡算法（NBEA-SAS），用于在对称α稳定分布（SαS）脉冲噪声环境下抑制码间干扰，在收敛速度和稳态性能上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在加性白脉冲噪声（由对称α稳定分布建模）环境中，传统的盲均衡算法性能退化严重，亟需一种鲁棒的盲均衡准则和算法来有效抑制码间干扰。

Method: 提出对数乘积分数阶矩统计量（LP-FMS）准则，将互补分数阶矩统计量与对数归一化相结合，构建约束优化框架；在此基础上，利用随机梯度上升法、递归分数阶矩估计和Bussgang一致性约束自适应，推导出对称α稳定噪声下的归一化盲均衡算法（NBEA-SAS）。

Result: 在64-APSK调制、分数间隔多径微波信道上的仿真表明，在中等和强脉冲性SαS噪声条件下，NBEA-SAS算法比FLOS-CMA、RAW-CMA和NBEA-GG收敛更快，且稳态残余码间干扰性能相当。

Conclusion: 所提出的LP-FMS准则为脉冲噪声环境下的盲自适应均衡提供了一种鲁棒框架，NBEA-SAS算法在收敛速度与稳态性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Original Abstract: The blind mitigation of inter-symbol interference in additive white impulsive noise modeled by a symmetric $α$-stable (S$α$S) distribution is investigated. A novel logarithmic product fractional-moment statistics (LP-FMS) criterion is proposed by combining complementary fractional-moment statistics with logarithmic normalization in a constrained optimization framework. Based on this criterion, a normalized blind equalization algorithm for symmetric alpha-stable noise (NBEA-SAS) is derived using stochastic gradient ascent with recursive fractional-moment estimation and Bussgang-consistent constrained adaptation. Simulation results for $64$-APSK signaling over fractionally spaced multipath microwave channels show that the proposed algorithm converges faster than FLOS-CMA, RAW-CMA, and NBEA-GG while achieving a comparable steady-state residual intersymbol interference floor under both moderately and highly impulsive S$α$S noise conditions. The results demonstrate that the proposed LP-FMS criterion provides a robust framework for blind adaptive equalization in impulsive noise environments.

中文摘要翻译: 研究了加性白脉冲噪声（由对称α稳定分布（SαS）建模）中码间干扰的盲抑制问题。通过将互补分数阶矩统计量与对数归一化相结合，在约束优化框架下提出了一种新颖的对数乘积分数阶矩统计量（LP-FMS）准则。基于该准则，利用随机梯度上升、递归分数阶矩估计以及Bussgang一致性约束自适应，推导出一种适用于对称α稳定噪声的归一化盲均衡算法（NBEA-SAS）。在分数间隔多径微波信道上进行64-APSK信号传输的仿真结果表明，在中度和高度脉冲性的SαS噪声条件下，所提算法比FLOS-CMA、RAW-CMA和NBEA-GG收敛更快，同时达到相当的稳态残余码间干扰水平。实验结果证明，所提出的LP-FMS准则为脉冲噪声环境下的盲自适应均衡提供了鲁棒框架。

</details>


### [80] [Long-Horizon Wireless Link Scheduling with State-Augmented Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18480)
*Romina Garcia Camargo,Zhiyang Wang,Navid NaderiAlizadeh,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模无线网络中的最优链路调度问题，提出了一种基于图神经网络（GNN）和状态增强技术的迭代算法，通过拉格朗日对偶性求解受约束的优化问题，生成了随时间变化的最优调度策略，在满足最低速率需求的同时最大化总速率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统原始-对偶方法导致时间不变的调度策略，而实际无线网络中的链路调度需要在避免干扰的同时保证每个链路按比例传输，这要求策略随时间动态变化。现有方法无法有效生成这种时变策略。

Method: 1. 将链路调度问题建模为带约束的优化问题，利用拉格朗日对偶性求解；2. 提出迭代算法采样最优调度序列和对偶变量；3. 使用图神经网络（GNN）参数化调度决策；4. 引入状态增强技术，将对偶变量作为策略的动态输入，使GNN能够随时间自适应调整调度决策。

Result: 通过大量数值仿真，在多种约束水平下与多个基线方法进行对比，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 状态增强技术使得GNN能够根据对偶变量的动态变化自适应调整调度决策，在满足约束条件和最大化性能之间取得了良好平衡，为大规模无线网络中的时变最优调度提供了有效解决方案。

Original Abstract: We address optimal link scheduling in large-scale wireless networks. The goal is to schedule transmissions over a time horizon so that to maximize sum rate while ensuring that average rates of each customer attain a minimum rate requirement. To this end, we formulate a constrained optimization problem and solve it using Lagrangian duality. Common primal-dual approaches lead to time invariant policies. Our constraint requires all links transmit a fraction of the time while avoiding interference, which calls for time-varying policies across time slots. We propose an iterative algorithm to sample optimal sequences of schedules and dual variables. The scheduling decisions are parameterized using a Graph Neural Network. We incorporate state-augmentation techniques to learn said parameterization, introducing dual variables as dynamic inputs to the policy. This augmentation enables the GNN to adapt scheduling decisions over time, balancing constraint satisfaction with performance maximization. We validate our approach through extensive numerical simulations, benchmarking against several baselines and considering varying constraint levels.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究大规模无线网络中的最优链路调度问题。目标是在一个时间范围内调度传输，以最大化总速率，同时确保每个用户的平均速率达到最低速率要求。为此，我们构建了一个带约束的优化问题，并利用拉格朗日对偶性进行求解。常见的原始-对偶方法会导致时间不变的策略。而我们的约束要求所有链路在避免干扰的同时按一定比例传输，这需要跨时隙采用时变策略。我们提出了一种迭代算法来采样最优的调度序列和对偶变量。调度决策使用图神经网络（GNN）进行参数化。我们引入状态增强技术来学习该参数化，将对偶变量作为策略的动态输入。这种增强使得GNN能够随时间自适应调整调度决策，在约束满足与性能最大化之间取得平衡。我们通过大量数值仿真验证了所提方法，在多种约束水平下与多个基线方法进行了对比。

</details>


### [81] [Semantic-Aware Data-Aided Channel Estimation with Large Language Models for MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18640)
*Sojeong Park,Jaehyun Choi,Hyun Jong Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的语义感知信道估计框架，用于MIMO系统。通过两层机制筛选和修正可靠符号，显著优于传统数据辅助方案，性能接近理想估计器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统数据辅助信道估计仅依赖物理层统计信息选择可靠符号，但误检测符号会污染信道估计。传输载荷中蕴含的语义结构可以帮助解决检测误差，现有方法未充分利用这一信息。

Method: 提出语义感知信道估计框架，利用微调的大语言模型（LLM）进行符号可靠性和修正。包含两层机制：第一层通过语义验证选择可靠译码符号；第二层通过将LLM修正符号与接收信号进行物理层交叉验证，选择准确的修正符号。

Result: 理论证明修正符号比初始正确译码符号带来严格更大的期望估计误差缩减。仿真表明，所提框架在归一化均方误差和误码率上显著优于传统数据辅助方案，性能接近理想估计器。

Conclusion: 语义信息与物理层信息的融合能有效提升MIMO系统信道估计性能，LLM辅助的符号选择与修正机制是实现这一目标的有效途径。

Original Abstract: Data-aided channel estimation enhances spectral efficiency by reusing detected symbols as virtual pilots. In this process, selecting only reliable symbols is crucial to prevent misdetected symbols from corrupting the channel estimate. However, conventional methods rely exclusively on physical-layer statistics. Beyond physical-layer information, transmitted payloads possess inherent semantic structures that can be exploited to resolve detection errors. In this paper, we propose a novel semantic-aware channel estimation framework for multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems that utilizes a fine-tuned large language model (LLM) to perform reliable symbol selection and correction based on semantic information. The framework employs a two-layer mechanism: one layer selects reliable decoded symbols through semantic verification, while the other selects accurately LLM-corrected symbols by cross-validating them against the received signal using physical-layer information. We prove that corrected symbols yield a strictly larger expected reduction in estimation error than initially correctly decoded symbols. Extensive simulations demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly outperforms conventional data-aided schemes in both normalized mean squared error and bit error rate, closely approaching the performance of an oracle estimator.

中文摘要翻译: 数据辅助信道估计通过将检测到的符号作为虚拟导频重用来提升频谱效率。在此过程中，仅选择可靠符号至关重要，以防止误检测符号污染信道估计。然而，传统方法仅依赖物理层统计信息。除了物理层信息之外，传输载荷本身具有固有的语义结构，可以利用这些结构来解决检测误差。本文针对多输入多输出（MIMO）系统提出了一种新颖的语义感知信道估计框架，该框架利用微调的大语言模型（LLM）基于语义信息执行可靠符号选择与修正。该框架采用两层机制：一层通过语义验证选择可靠译码符号，另一层通过将LLM修正符号与接收信号进行物理层交叉验证，选择准确的LLM修正符号。我们证明了修正符号比初始正确译码符号能带来严格更大的期望估计误差缩减。大量仿真表明，所提框架在归一化均方误差和误码率上均显著优于传统数据辅助方案，性能接近理想估计器。

</details>


### [82] [Joint Synchronization and Sensing in Networked ISAC via Structured Canonical Polyadic Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18680)
*Lin Chen,Yifan Liang,Hongbin Li*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出结构化规范多元分解（SCPD）算法，用于解决网络化集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中的同步问题，通过分离感知信道多径分量实现联合网络级同步与多目标参数估计，并在此基础上设计多目标跟踪与自适应波束成形方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 网络化ISAC需要基站间时频同步，但现有同步方法受感知信道压缩引起的路径间干扰影响，导致同步精度下降。

Method: 提出结构化规范多元分解（SCPD）算法分离感知信道多径分量；基于不同基站对估计的参数设计多目标跟踪算法；利用前一时刻跟踪结果开发自适应波束成形方案以提升下一时刻跟踪性能。

Result: 理论分析表明SCPD渐近达到克拉美罗界；仿真结果显示所提算法在同步精度和感知性能上均优于传统方法，具有出色的异常值鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SCPD算法有效解决了网络化ISAC中的同步干扰问题，实现了高精度联合同步与感知，且所提跟踪与波束成形方案适用于系统连续运行。

Original Abstract: Networked integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) offers significant potential for next-generation wireless systems. By exploiting spatial diversity through the cooperation of multiple base stations (BSs), this architecture expands coverage and achieves enhanced sensing performance. However, accurate sensing in networked ISAC requires time-frequency synchronization among BSs. Existing synchronization methods for networked ISAC suffer from inter-path interference caused by sensing channel compression. To address this problem, this paper proposes a structured canonical polyadic decomposition (SCPD) algorithm that effectively separates the multipath components of the sensing channel. Benefiting from this separation, SCPD achieves joint network-level synchronization and multi-target parameter estimation. We establish theoretical identifiability conditions for SCPD and show that it asymptotically achieves the Cramér-Rao bound. Furthermore, by incorporating parameters estimated from different BS pairs, we propose a multi-target tracking algorithm designed for the continuous operation of the system. The proposed algorithm tracks both the trajectories and velocities of moving targets by leveraging geometric diversity. Utilizing tracking results from the previous snapshot, an adaptive beamforming scheme is also developed to improve tracking performance in the next snapshot. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed algorithms achieve superior accuracy and outlier robustness for both synchronization and sensing in networked ISAC, outperforming traditional approaches.

中文摘要翻译: 网络化集成感知与通信（ISAC）为下一代无线系统提供了巨大潜力。通过多个基站（BS）协作利用空间分集，该架构扩展了覆盖范围并实现了增强的感知性能。然而，网络化ISAC中的精确感知需要基站间的时频同步。现有网络化ISAC的同步方法受到感知信道压缩引起的路径间干扰的影响。为解决该问题，本文提出一种结构化规范多元分解（SCPD）算法，该算法能有效分离感知信道的多径分量。得益于这种分离，SCPD实现了联合网络级同步与多目标参数估计。我们建立了SCPD的理论可辨识性条件，并证明其渐近达到克拉美罗界。此外，通过融合不同基站对估计的参数，我们提出一种专为系统连续运行设计的多目标跟踪算法。该算法利用几何分集跟踪运动目标的轨迹和速度。利用前一时刻的跟踪结果，还开发了一种自适应波束成形方案以提升下一时刻的跟踪性能。仿真结果表明，所提算法在网络化ISAC的同步和感知中均实现了优于传统方法的卓越精度和异常值鲁棒性。

</details>


### [83] [Cyclic-Prefix-Free OFDM With Tail-Reuse Reconstruction for Distributed Acoustic Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18687)
*Ziang Chen,Zhiyang Xue,Zhongxing Tian,Zeyu Feng,Dongdong Zou,Yi Cai,Huan Huang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于分布式声学传感（DAS）的无显式循环前缀OFDM波形，通过复用前一有用块的尾部作为虚拟循环扩展，在保持空间分辨率的同时延长了无混叠振动带宽，并通过仿真和5.2-km实验验证了该方法的有效性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统相干DAS中，OFDM所需的显式循环前缀（CP）会延长探测周期，降低每个距离单元的慢时间奈奎斯特极限，从而限制了可无混叠测量的振动频率上限。本文旨在去除显式CP以提升可测振动带宽。

Method: 提出一种重复的无循环前缀OFDM波形，将前一有用块的尾部作为当前块的虚拟循环扩展（尾重用）；推导了尾重用重建的有限记忆范围条件，并识别了有用周期短于信道记忆时的圆形折叠效应。

Result: 在5.2-km数值仿真中，75 MSa/s处理速率和4096样本有用块实现54.61 μs探测周期和9.16 kHz慢时间奈奎斯特极限，恢复800 Hz至8800 Hz的100个振动事件。实验中以111.984 MHz OFDM带宽在5.2-km链路上盲定位了500 Hz和3 kHz的PZT振动，并恢复了波形和频谱。

Conclusion: 尾重用信道重建是可行的，显式CP的去除可以有效扩展无混叠振动带宽，且不改变占用带宽受限的空间分辨率。

Original Abstract: Orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) enables frequency-domain reconstruction of the distributed Rayleigh backscatter channel in coherent distributed acoustic sensing (DAS), but an explicitly transmitted cyclic prefix (CP) lengthens the probing period and reduces the slow-time Nyquist limit of each range cell. We investigate a repeated cyclic-prefix-free OFDM waveform for DAS, in which the tail of the preceding useful block serves as a virtual cyclic extension. A finite-memory range condition for tail-reuse reconstruction is derived, and circular folding is identified when the useful period is shorter than the channel memory. For fixed useful-block length and fiber memory, removing the explicit CP increases the period-limited highest unaliased vibration frequency without changing the occupied-bandwidth-limited spatial resolution. In a 5.2-km numerical configuration, a 75-MSa/s processing rate and a 4096-sample useful block give a 54.61-us probing period and a 9.16-kHz slow-time Nyquist limit. Simulations verify tail reuse, the predicted folding boundary, and recovery of 100 vibration events from 800 Hz to 8800 Hz. Bandwidth-scaling simulations further show that joint processing of fine spatial observations improves differential-phase reliability and reconstruction SNR at a fixed reporting interval. Experiments on a 5.2-km coherent DAS link with 111.984-MHz occupied OFDM bandwidth blindly localize 500-Hz and 3-kHz PZT-induced vibrations at 5063.7 m and 5070.1 m, respectively, and recover their waveforms and spectra. The results demonstrate feasible tail-reuse channel reconstruction and quantify the extension of the unaliased vibration bandwidth.

中文摘要翻译: 正交频分复用（OFDM）能够在相干分布式声学传感（DAS）中实现分布式瑞利背向散射信道的频域重构，但显式传输的循环前缀（CP）会延长探测周期并降低每个距离单元的慢时间奈奎斯特极限。本文研究了一种用于DAS的重复无循环前缀OFDM波形，其中前一有用块的尾部作为虚拟循环扩展。推导了尾重用重建的有限记忆范围条件，并识别了有用周期短于信道记忆时的圆形折叠效应。对于固定的有用块长度和光纤记忆，去除显式CP可在不改变占用带宽受限空间分辨率的情况下，提高周期受限的最高无混叠振动频率。在5.2-km数值配置中，75 MSa/s处理速率和4096样本有用块实现了54.61 μs探测周期和9.16 kHz慢时间奈奎斯特极限。仿真验证了尾重用、预测的折叠边界以及从800 Hz到8800 Hz的100个振动事件的恢复。带宽缩放仿真进一步表明，在固定报告间隔下，联合处理精细空间观测可改善差分相位可靠性和重建信噪比。在5.2-km相干DAS链路上进行的实验，以111.984 MHz占用OFDM带宽，分别盲定位了5063.7 m处500 Hz和5070.1 m处3 kHz的PZT感应振动，并恢复了其波形和频谱。结果证明了可行的尾重用信道重建，并量化了无混叠振动带宽的扩展。

</details>


### [84] [Non-Square UPA-Enabled XL-MIMO Systems: Anisotropic Near-Field Characterization, Fundamental Limits, and Channel Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18736)
*Yilong Liu,Xi Yang,Jing Xu,Jun Zhang,Shi jin*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了非方形均匀平面阵列（UPA）在超大规模MIMO系统中的各向异性近场特性，揭示了非方形几何带来的性能影响，并提出了低复杂度的三维各向异性近场码本用于信道估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 实际部署中非方形UPA由于孔径差异会改变波前特性，产生沿不同轴向上的各向异性波束聚焦能力，需充分揭示这种非方形几何对系统性能的影响，以释放非方形UPA的潜力。

Method: 1) 推导长短轴有效波束聚焦距离；2) 划分辐射空间为三个区域（全近场、各向异性近场、远场）；3) 推导渐近有效自由度；4) 推导距离估计的闭式CRB和三维位置误差界；5) 设计三维各向异性近场码本用于低复杂度信道估计。

Result: 1) 各向异性区域随阵列纵横比增大而渐近主导整个近场空间；2) 距离域复用由大纵横比下长轴孔径主导；3) 确定了使三维PEB最优的阵列纵横比；4) 所提码本在降低复杂度的同时达到与三维极域码本相当的精度。

Conclusion: 非方形UPA的各向异性近场特性可被有效利用，通过优化阵列纵横比可实现三维定位性能折衷，且所提各向异性近场码本实现了精度与复杂度的良好平衡。

Original Abstract: Extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) is crucial for next-generation communication systems. In practice, the deployment of non-square uniform planar arrays (UPAs) fundamentally alters wavefront characteristics and induces anisotropic beamfocusing capability along different axes due to the aperture disparity originating from the non-square array geometry. To fully uncover the performance impact of such non-square geometry and thus unleash the potential of the non-square UPAs, we investigate the anisotropic near-field characteristics, fundamental limits, and channel estimation for non-square UPA-enabled XL-MIMO systems. First, we derive the effective beamfocusing distances for the long and short axes of the array. Interestingly, the radiation space of a non-square UPA can be partitioned into three regions, i.e., the fully near-field, the anisotropic near-field, and the far-field regions, and the anisotropic region asymptotically dominates the overall near-field space as the array aspect ratio increases. Then, the asymptotic effective degree of freedom for non-square UPA-enabled XL-MIMO systems is provided, which reveals that distance-domain multiplexing is governed by the long-axis aperture in the large array aspect ratio regime. Furthermore, the closed-form Cramer-Rao bound for distance estimation and the three-dimensional (3D) position error bound (PEB) are derived to reveal the geometry-induced performance trade-offs among distance, azimuth, and elevation estimation, based on which the optimal array aspect ratio that minimizes the 3D PEB is determined. Finally, by exploiting the anisotropic wavefront properties, we design a 3D anisotropic near-field codebook to facilitate low-complexity channel estimation for non-square UPAs. Numerical results validate that the proposed codebook achieves comparable accuracy to the 3D polar-domain codebook at reduced complexity.

中文摘要翻译: 超大规模多输入多输出（XL-MIMO）是下一代通信系统的关键技术。在实际部署中，非方形均匀平面阵列（UPA）由于非方形阵列几何带来的孔径差异，从根本上改变了波前特性，并在不同轴向上产生各向异性的波束聚焦能力。为充分揭示这种非方形几何对性能的影响，进而释放非方形UPA的潜力，我们研究了非方形UPA支持的XL-MIMO系统的各向异性近场特性、基本极限及信道估计问题。首先，我们推导了阵列长轴和短轴的有效波束聚焦距离。有趣的是，非方形UPA的辐射空间可划分为三个区域：全近场区、各向异性近场区和远场区，且各向异性区域随着阵列纵横比的增加渐近主导整个近场空间。接着，给出了非方形UPA支持的XL-MIMO系统的渐近有效自由度，揭示了在大阵列纵横比条件下距离域复用由长轴孔径主导。此外，推导了距离估计的闭式克拉美-罗界以及三维位置误差界，揭示了距离、方位角和仰角估计之间由几何因素引起的性能折衷，并据此确定了使三维位置误差界最小的最优阵列纵横比。最后，利用各向异性波前特性，我们设计了一种三维各向异性近场码本，以促进非方形UPA的低复杂度信道估计。数值结果验证了所提码本在降低复杂度的同时能够达到与三维极域码本相当的精度。

</details>


### [85] [Outage-Constrained Environment Division Multiple Access (EDMA) for Pinching-Antenna Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18738)
*Weihao Mao,Yang Lu,Yanqing Xu,Zhiguo Ding*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于可移动天线的环境分多址（EDMA）框架，通过联合优化天线部署和功率分配来最大化总信息速率，并提出了两种低复杂度算法（PGD和SCA），在保证接近最优性能的同时显著优于传统多址方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 克服传统多址接入中的用户间干扰问题，利用可移动天线对环境传播条件的自适应能力，同时考虑实际信道中的概率性视距阻塞、随机非视距散射及波导衰减等非理想因素。

Method: 针对两用户两天线场景推导中断概率闭式解，提出基于投影梯度下降（PGD）的算法；扩展到多用户多天线场景，利用Chernoff界技术近似中断概率，提出基于逐次凸近似（SCA）的算法。

Result: 两种算法均达到接近穷举搜索的最优性能；所提出的PA-EDMA方案相比PA使能的TDMA方案和传统TDMA方案均取得显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 基于可移动天线的EDMA框架能够有效缓解多用户干扰，在考虑实际传播环境因素下仍能实现优异性能，是一类有前景的多址接入方案。

Original Abstract: Environment division multiple access (EDMA) has emerged as a promising multiple access paradigm, which mitigates inter-user interference by dynamically adjusting pinching antenna (PA) positions to the underlying propagation environment. This paper investigates a multi-user PA-enabled EDMA framework that accounts for probabilistic line-of-sight (LoS) blockages, random non-LoS (NLoS) scattering, and practical inwaveguide attenuation. With the aim of maximizing the total information rate, we formulate a joint PA deployment and power allocation problem subject to statistical rate outage constraints, the transmit power budget, and the feasible deployment region of PAs. We first consider a canonical two-user two-PA scenario and derive closed-form expressions for the outage probabilities, followed by a low-complexity projected gradient descent (PGD)-based algorithm to address the reformulated problem. Then, we extend our design to the general multi-user multi-PA scenario and derive tractable approximations for the outage probabilities by assuming interfering links to be NLoS and applying the Chernoff bounding technique, where a successive convex approximation (SCA)-based algorithm is proposed to handle the resulting nonconvex problem. Simulations validate the superiority of the proposed PA-enabled EDMA design and the effectiveness of the proposed algorithms. Specifically, both the PGD-based algorithm and the SCA-based algorithm achieve near-optimal performance in comparison with the exhaustive search. Furthermore, the PA-enabled EDMA design yields significant performance gains over both PA-enabled and conventional time division multiple access designs.

中文摘要翻译: 环境分多址（EDMA）已成为一种有前景的多址接入范式，它通过动态调整可移动天线（PA）的位置以适应底层传播环境，从而减轻用户间干扰。本文研究了一个多用户PA使能的EDMA框架，该框架考虑了概率性视距（LoS）阻塞、随机非视距（NLoS）散射以及实际的波导衰减。以最大化总信息速率为目标，我们提出了一个联合PA部署与功率分配问题，并受到统计速率中断约束、发射功率预算以及PA可行部署区域的限制。我们首先考虑一个典型的两用户两PA场景，推导了中断概率的闭式表达式，随后提出了一种基于低复杂度投影梯度下降（PGD）的算法来求解重构后的问题。接着，我们将设计推广到通用的多用户多PA场景，通过假设干扰链路为NLoS并应用Chernoff界技术，推导了中断概率的可处理近似，并提出了一种基于逐次凸近似（SCA）的算法来处理由此产生的非凸问题。仿真验证了所提出的PA使能EDMA设计的优越性以及所提算法的有效性。具体而言，与穷举搜索相比，基于PGD的算法和基于SCA的算法均实现了接近最优的性能。此外，PA使能的EDMA设计相比PA使能的时分多址设计和传统时分多址设计均取得了显著的性能增益。

</details>


### [86] [Low-Complexity Channel Estimation Framework for Non-Square UPA-Assisted XL-MIMO Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18744)
*Yilong Liu,Xi Yang,Binggui Zhou,Yu Han,Ting Liu,Shaodan Ma*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对XL-MIMO系统中非方形均匀平面阵列在混合场环境下仰角分辨率受限和信道稀疏性恶化导致的高复杂度与低精度问题，提出了一种低复杂度信道估计框架，通过天线域与相关域外推方案及解耦参数估计方法实现高效信道获取。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 极大规模MIMO系统中，非方形均匀平面阵列在实际混合场部署中面临仰角到达角分辨率有限和信道稀疏性恶化的问题，导致计算复杂度过高且估计精度下降，亟需低复杂度的信道状态信息获取方案。

Method: 提出一种低复杂度信道估计框架：1) 天线域外推方案利用相邻阵元空间相关性合成虚拟垂直孔径，突破仰角分辨率限制；2) 将二维联合搜索解耦为两个顺序一维搜索，即沿虚拟垂直ULA采用外推增强的DFT-NOMP算法提取仰角AoA，沿水平ULA采用离散分数阶傅里叶变换-NOMP算法获取方位角AoA、距离和增益；3) 子空间拟合驱动的路径匹配算法配对解耦参数；4) 进一步开发相关域外推方案，利用空间相关矩阵结构特性解耦近场二次项和方位相位分量，构建噪声抑制虚拟阵列。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提框架的有效性，表明该方法在降低计算复杂度的同时提升了信道估计精度。

Conclusion: 所提出的低复杂度信道估计框架通过天线域和相关域外推方案以及参数解耦策略，有效解决了非方形UPA在混合场环境下的精度和复杂度挑战，为XL-MIMO系统的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Low-complexity channel state information acquisition is crucial for extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) systems. However, practical deployments of non-square uniform planar arrays (UPAs) in hybrid-field environments face prohibitive computational complexity and degraded estimation accuracy due to limited elevation angle-of-arrival (AoA) resolution and deteriorated channel sparsity. To tackle these challenges, we propose a low-complexity channel estimation framework. First, an antenna-domain extrapolation scheme synthesizes a virtually enlarged vertical aperture via the spatial correlation among adjacent elements, breaking the elevation resolution limit. The framework then disentangles the parameter coupling by transforming the two-dimensional joint search into two sequential one-dimensional searches. Specifically, elevation AoAs are extracted via an extrapolation-enhanced discrete Fourier transform-Newtonized orthogonal matching pursuit (NOMP) algorithm along the virtually enlarged vertical uniform linear array (ULA), while azimuth AoAs, ranges, and gains are acquired utilizing a discrete fractional Fourier transform-NOMP algorithm along a horizontal ULA. A subspace fitting-driven path matching algorithm pairs these decoupled parameters. To overcome the accuracy bottleneck of the antenna-domain scheme, a correlation-domain extrapolation scheme is further developed by exploiting the structural properties of the spatial correlation matrix to decouple the near-field quadratic and azimuth phase components, yielding a noise-suppressed virtual array. Numerical results validate the effectiveness of the proposed framework.

中文摘要翻译: 低复杂度的信道状态信息获取对于极大规模多输入多输出系统至关重要。然而，非方形均匀平面阵列在混合场环境中的实际部署面临着严峻的计算复杂度和估计精度下降问题，这是由于仰角到达角分辨率有限以及信道稀疏性恶化所致。为应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种低复杂度信道估计框架。首先，天线域外推方案通过利用相邻阵元间的空间相关性合成虚拟扩大的垂直孔径，突破了仰角分辨率限制。该框架随后通过将二维联合搜索转化为两个顺序一维搜索来解耦参数耦合。具体地，沿虚拟扩大的垂直均匀线性阵列采用外推增强的离散傅里叶变换-牛顿化正交匹配追踪算法提取仰角到达角，同时沿水平均匀线性阵列采用离散分数阶傅里叶变换-牛顿化正交匹配追踪算法获取方位角到达角、距离和增益。子空间拟合驱动的路径匹配算法对这些解耦参数进行配对。为克服天线域方案的精度瓶颈，进一步开发了相关域外推方案，利用空间相关矩阵的结构特性解耦近场二次项和方位相位分量，构建噪声抑制虚拟阵列。数值结果验证了所提框架的有效性。

</details>


### [87] [Low-Altitude UAV-Assisted Bistatic ISAC: Closed-form 3D CRLB and Coverage Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18766)
*Haoyu Jiang,Hengyou Kong,Xiaoli Xu,Yong Zeng*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文研究了无人机辅助集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中三维定位的基本性能极限，推导了三维克拉美-罗下界（CRLB）的闭式表达式，并提出了CRLB约束的感知覆盖概念，揭示了无人机位移、高度和CRLB阈值对可靠感知区域几何形状的影响。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在无人机辅助的ISAC系统中，三维定位精度受到多种因素影响，目前缺乏一个统一的性能极限分析框架，无法准确量化定位精度与无人机位置、目标位置之间的数学关系，需要建立理论下界以指导系统设计与优化。

Method: 推导了三维CRLB的闭式表达式，该表达式由信号传播延迟、角度测量及其耦合效应三个自然分量组成；通过基于OFDM的ISAC系统进行最大似然估计验证；引入CRLB约束的感知覆盖概念，通过局部边界近似和覆盖范围评估分析可靠感知区域。

Result: 推导的CRLB闭式表达式能准确预测不同几何配置和无人机移动模式下最大似然估计的性能；揭示了无人机位移、高度和CRLB阈值共同决定可靠感知区域的几何形状与范围。

Conclusion: 本文建立的三维CRLB理论框架为无人机辅助ISAC系统的定位性能评估提供了有效工具，CRLB约束的感知覆盖概念可用于指导无人机部署和系统参数设计，以实现期望的定位精度保障。

Original Abstract: This paper investigates the fundamental performance limits of three-dimensional (3D) localization in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-assisted integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. Specifically, a base station (BS) estimates the 3D position of a sensing target with the aid of a UAV acting as a flexible aerial anchor node. We derive a closed-form expression for the 3D Cramer-Rao lower bound (CRLB), which explicitly quantifies the achievable localization accuracy as a function of both the UAV's location and the target's position. The CRLB is shown to decompose naturally into three distinct components, arising from signal propagation delay, angular measurements, and their coupling effect, respectively. To validate the analytical results, we consider a representative orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM)-based ISAC system and demonstrate that the derived CRLB closely predicts the performance of maximum-likelihood estimation across diverse geometric configurations and UAV mobility patterns. Furthermore, we introduce the notion of CRLB-constrained sensing coverage to characterize the spatial region within which a prescribed localization accuracy can be guaranteed. Through local boundary approximations and coverage-size evaluations, we reveal how UAV displacement, altitude, and the CRLB threshold jointly shape the extent and geometry of the reliable sensing region.

中文摘要翻译: 本文研究了无人机辅助集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中三维定位的基本性能极限。具体而言，基站（BS）借助充当灵活空中锚节点的无人机来估计感知目标的三维位置。我们推导了三维克拉美-罗下界（CRLB）的闭式表达式，该表达式明确地将可实现的定位精度量化为无人机位置和目标位置的函数。该CRLB可自然地分解为三个独立分量，分别源自信号传播延迟、角度测量及其耦合效应。为验证分析结果，我们考虑了一个代表性的基于正交频分复用（OFDM）的ISAC系统，并证明所推导的CRLB在不同几何配置和无人机移动模式下能够紧密预测最大似然估计的性能。此外，我们引入了CRLB约束的感知覆盖概念，以刻画能够保证规定定位精度的空间区域。通过局部边界近似和覆盖范围评估，我们揭示了无人机位移、高度和CRLB阈值如何共同塑造可靠感知区域的范围与几何形状。

</details>


### [88] [Data-aided Channel Estimation and Sensing With Sparse Bayesian Learning for AFDM-ISAC System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18881)
*Yirui Luo,Yong Liang Guan,Yao Ge,Yonghong Jiang,Lingsheng Meng,David González G*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据辅助的网格演化稀疏贝叶斯学习（D-GESBL）方案，用于AFDM波形下的集成感知与通信系统，通过将信道估计建模为多测量向量离网格稀疏恢复问题，有效解决了分数时延和多普勒偏移下的频谱效率与参数精度难以兼得的问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在双弥散信道中，分数时延和分数多普勒偏移导致传统方法难以在提高频谱效率的同时获得精确的信道和感知相关参数，亟需一种能够同时应对离网格失配和高精度估计的方案。

Method: 将信道估计任务建模为多测量向量（MMV）离网格稀疏恢复问题，提出数据辅助的网格演化稀疏贝叶斯学习（D-GESBL）方案。具体包括：在叠加导频框架下设计数据辅助迭代接收机，将解码可靠的数据符号作为伪导频反馈辅助估计；开发网格演化过程，根据估计的离网格分量迭代调整离散仿射傅里叶域（DAF）中的虚拟网格以缓解离网格失配；并结合广义近似消息传递（GAMP）算法提出低复杂度D-GAMP-GESBL算法。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提方案的有效性，并证明了其相对于现有最先进方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的D-GESBL及其低复杂度变体D-GAMP-GESBL方案能有效提升双弥散信道下AFDM-ISAC系统的信道估计与感知性能，优于现有方法。

Original Abstract: Affine frequency division multiplexing (AFDM) has emerged as a promising waveform for next-generation integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems. However, it becomes challenging to improve spectral efficiency while simultaneously obtaining accurate channel and sensing-related parameters, particularly in doubly-dispersive channels with fractional delays and fractional Doppler shifts. To tackle this challenge, by formulating the channel estimation task as a multiple measurement vector (MMV) off-grid sparse recovery problem, we propose a data-aided grid-evolution sparse Bayesian learning (D-GESBL) scheme for channel estimation and sensing under a superimposed pilot framework. Specifically, we develop an efficient data-aided iterative receiver, in which reliably decoded data symbols are fed back as additional pseudo-pilot information to assist channel estimation and sensing. To mitigate off-grid mismatch and improve the overall estimation accuracy, we develop a grid evolution procedure that iteratively adjusts the virtual grids in the discrete affine Fourier (DAF) domain according to the estimated off-grid components. Furthermore, by integrating the generalized approximate message passing (GAMP) algorithm into the proposed SBL framework, we also develop a low-complexity data-aided GAMP-based grid-evolution SBL (D-GAMP-GESBL) algorithm. Finally, the numerical results validate the effectiveness of our proposed schemes and demonstrate their superiority over existing state-of-the-art methods.

中文摘要翻译: 仿射频分复用（AFDM）已成为下一代集成感知与通信（ISAC）系统中一种有前景的波形。然而，在具有分数时延和分数多普勒偏移的双弥散信道中，提高频谱效率同时获得精确的信道和感知相关参数变得具有挑战性。为应对这一挑战，通过将信道估计任务建模为多测量向量（MMV）离网格稀疏恢复问题，我们提出了一种在叠加导频框架下的数据辅助网格演化稀疏贝叶斯学习（D-GESBL）方案，用于信道估计与感知。具体而言，我们开发了一种高效的数据辅助迭代接收机，其中可靠解码的数据符号被反馈作为额外的伪导频信息以辅助信道估计与感知。为缓解离网格失配并提升整体估计精度，我们开发了一种网格演化过程，根据估计的离网格分量迭代调整离散仿射傅里叶（DAF）域中的虚拟网格。此外，通过将广义近似消息传递（GAMP）算法集成到所提出的SBL框架中，我们还开发了一种低复杂度的数据辅助GAMP网格演化SBL（D-GAMP-GESBL）算法。最终，数值结果验证了所提方案的有效性，并证明了其相对于现有最先进方法的优越性。

</details>


### [89] [Convex Optimization-Based Procedures for Non-Convex Quadratic Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18933)
*Mahmoud Zaher,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文综述了数学优化在信号处理和无线通信中的基础作用，重点讨论了二次约束二次规划（QCQP）问题——包括凸与非凸情况，指出非凸QCQP（如不定二次型和秩约束）通常导致NP-hard问题，并强调为下一代系统开发先进优化理论与算法的重要性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 信号处理和通信系统中的许多设计问题可自然建模为优化问题，但随着应用发展，优化问题的结构与复杂性显著变化，需要发展适配下一代系统需求的高级优化理论与算法。

Method: 本文是一篇综述性论文，系统梳理了数学优化在信号处理和通信中的应用，重点分析了QCQP问题的分类（凸与非凸）、难点（非凸QCQP的NP-hard性质）以及各类实际应用场景。

Result: 凸QCQP可用多项式时间算法高效求解，但一般非凸QCQP（含不定二次型和秩约束）是计算上NP-hard的，此类问题广泛存在于信号处理、通信、控制、机器学习及网络优化等领域。

Conclusion: 针对下一代系统需求，亟需发展专门化的高阶优化理论与算法来应对非凸QCQP带来的计算挑战。

Original Abstract: Mathematical optimization plays a fundamental role in signal processing and wireless communications, serving as an essential framework for the systematic design of modern systems. Many design challenges in these fields, as well as in many others, can naturally be formulated as optimization problems. Over the years, the advancements in signal processing applications have significantly changed the structure and complexity of these optimization problems, creating new challenges in their analysis, understanding, and solution \cite{liu2024survey}. Consequently, the rapid development of sophisticated optimization theories and algorithms tailored to the demands of next-generation systems is crucial.
  Quadratic optimization problems constitute one of the most important classes of optimization problems in modern engineering systems. In signal processing and communications, quadratic forms naturally emerge when modeling power, energy, covariance matrices, and Euclidean distances, to name a few examples. Consequently, a broad family of practical design problems can be represented using quadratically constrained quadratic programs (QCQPs), where both the objective function and the constraints are quadratic functions of the optimization variables. While convex QCQPs can be solved efficiently using polynomial-time algorithms, the general non-convex QCQP remains computationally challenging. Specifically, indefinite quadratic forms and rank constraints often induce NP-hardness. Non-convex QCQP problems arise in a broad range of signal processing, communications, control, machine learning, and network optimization applications.

中文摘要翻译: 数学优化在信号处理和无线通信中发挥着基础性作用，是现代系统系统化设计的重要框架。这些领域以及许多其他领域中的大量设计问题都可以自然地表述为优化问题。多年来，信号处理应用的进步显著改变了这些优化问题的结构和复杂性，为其分析、理解和求解带来了新的挑战。因此，针对下一代系统需求，快速发展先进且专门化的优化理论和算法至关重要。二次优化问题是现代工程系统中最重要的一类优化问题。在信号处理和通信中，二次形式自然出现在建模功率、能量、协方差矩阵及欧氏距离等场景中。因此，大量实际设计问题可以用二次约束二次规划（QCQP）来表示，其中目标函数和约束条件都是优化变量的二次函数。虽然凸QCQP可以通过多项式时间算法高效求解，但一般的非凸QCQP在计算上仍然具有挑战性。具体而言，不定二次型和秩约束往往导致NP-hard问题。非凸QCQP问题广泛存在于信号处理、通信、控制、机器学习和网络优化等应用中。

</details>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [90] [FSDBN: Foreground-Aware EEG--Visual Alignment via Dynamic Brain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18344)
*Yiheng Liu,Chuhang Zheng,Peiliang Gong,Jingtao Liu,Daoqiang Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出FSDBN统一框架，通过语义一致性显著性对齐和动态门控融合解决EEG视觉解码中前景背景不对称和时空动态性问题，在零样本脑-图像检索任务上达到SOTA性能（Top-1 69.0%，Top-5 92.2%）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EEG视觉解码方法忽略了复杂场景中前景与背景的感知不对称性，导致背景干扰和语义错配；同时EEG信号具有快速时间动态和非平稳空间模式，难以捕捉与聚焦视觉注意相关的时变脑连接。

Method: 提出FSDBN统一框架：(1) 语义一致性显著性对齐模块，在联合显著性和语义约束下分离语义相关的前景区域与背景噪声；(2) 语义先验动态门控前景融合模块，自适应调节前景和背景特征的贡献；(3) EEG信号建模为自适应时空脑网络，功能连接动态重组以捕获对显著前景的神经响应。

Result: 在零样本脑-图像检索任务上，FSDBN达到69.0%的Top-1准确率和92.2%的Top-5准确率，优于此前所有最优方法。

Conclusion: FSDBN通过显式建模视觉前景-背景不对称性和EEG时空动态性，有效实现鲁棒的EEG视觉解码，代码已开源。

Original Abstract: EEG-based visual decoding provides a non-invasive pathway for interpreting visual semantics. However, existing methods often overlook the perceptual asymmetry between foreground and background in complex scenes, leading to background interference and semantic misalignment. EEG signals also exhibit rapid temporal dynamics and nonstationary spatial patterns, making it difficult to capture the time-varying brain connectivity associated with focal visual attention. To address these limitations, we propose FSDBN, a unified framework for robust EEG-visual decoding. FSDBN introduces Semantic-Consistent Saliency Alignment to separate semantically relevant foreground regions from background noise under joint saliency and semantic constraints. It further employs Semantic-Prior Dynamic Gating Foreground Fusion to adaptively regulate the contributions of foreground and background features. In parallel, EEG signals are modeled as adaptive spatiotemporal brain networks whose functional connectivity dynamically reorganizes to capture neural responses to salient foregrounds. Experiments on zero-shot brain-to-image retrieval demonstrate that FSDBN achieves 69.0 percent top-1 accuracy and 92.2 percent top-5 accuracy, outperforming previous state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at https://github.com/LiuYiheng1/FSDBN-EEG.

中文摘要翻译: 基于EEG的视觉解码提供了一条用于解读视觉语义的非侵入式通路。然而，现有方法往往忽略了复杂场景中前景与背景之间的感知不对称性，导致背景干扰和语义错配。EEG信号还表现出快速的时间动态性和非平稳的空间模式，使得捕获与聚焦视觉注意相关的时变脑连接变得困难。为解决这些局限性，我们提出FSDBN，一个用于鲁棒EEG-视觉解码的统一框架。FSDBN引入了语义一致性显著性对齐，在联合显著性和语义约束下将语义相关的前景区域与背景噪声分离开来。它进一步采用语义先验动态门控前景融合，自适应调节前景和背景特征的贡献。同时，EEG信号被建模为自适应时空脑网络，其功能连接动态重组以捕获对显著前景的神经响应。在零样本脑-图像检索上的实验表明，FSDBN达到了69.0%的Top-1准确率和92.2%的Top-5准确率，优于此前的最优方法。代码已在https://github.com/LiuYiheng1/FSDBN-EEG开源。

</details>


### [91] [Thinking Fast, Thinking Slow: Adaptive Multimodal Transformer-based Sensor Fusion for Depth Estimation on Ultra-low-power MCUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18897)
*Luca Crupi,Lorenzo Lamberti,Giovanni Badaracco,Daniele Allegri,Alessandro Giusti,Daniele Palossi*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应多模态传感器融合方法，结合轻量级循环Transformer架构（68.8万参数），在超低功耗嵌入式平台上实现深度图估计，仅牺牲4.8%的δ1精度即可节省90%能耗（2.44 mJ/帧）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在超低功耗（<100 mW）嵌入式平台上，增加传感器数量会提升能耗，需要在预测精度与能量消耗之间取得平衡。现有方法难以在资源极度受限的条件下实现高效的多模态融合。

Method: 提出自适应AI方法，融合相机、超声和ToF三种传感器；采用基于循环Transformer的轻量级架构（68.8万参数），通过跨迭代的token传播机制保持时间一致性；设计置信度门控机制，在每次迭代中动态决定是否新增功耗更高的传感器，实现增量式传感器调度。

Result: 在NYUv2数据集上，自适应系统相比使用全部传感器和迭代的基线，仅损失4.8%的δ1精度，但节省90%能耗（2.44 mJ/帧）；相比MobileDepth，参数减少9倍，δ1精度仅低5.6%；相比同样运行在GAP9上的SOTA模型，δ1精度提升31.8%，且功耗预算相同（~400 mW）。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可在极端资源约束下实现高效多模态传感器融合的自适应AI方法，通过置信度门控和token传播机制，在精度与能耗之间取得了优异的平衡，适合部署在超低功耗嵌入式平台。

Original Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI)-based multimodal sensor fusion is a relevant topic gaining ever more traction across ultra-low-power (ULP) embedded and cyber-physical systems, as it improves reliability, accuracy, and robustness under real-world constraints. However, adding more and more sensors to ultra-constrained sub-100 mW platforms requires balancing energy consumption against prediction accuracy. To achieve this ambitious goal, we present a novel adaptive AI methodology that combines multimodal sensor fusion (camera, ultrasound, and Time-of-Flight sensors) with a lightweight recurrent Transformer-based architecture (688 k parameters). We address the depth map estimation task with a mechanism that combines token propagation across iterations with incremental sensor utilization. At each iteration, a confidence-based gating mechanism dynamically decides whether to continue the computation by adding progressively richer but more power-demanding sensors as input. Token propagation ensures temporal consistency by forwarding context features across time. To deploy our algorithm and test a first real-world prototype, we design a novel printed circuit board featuring all three sensors, coupled with an ULP GWT GAP9 multicore System-on-Chip. When comparing our adaptive system against the same pipeline using all sensors and iterations on the NYUv2 dataset, we lose only 4.8% of the δ1 accuracy in exchange for 90% energy saving (2.44 mJ/frame). Finally, our adaptive method marks only 5.6% lower δ1 accuracy than MobileDepth despite using 9x fewer parameters. Compared with a state-of-the-art model also running on GAP9, our method improves δ1 accuracy by 31.8% thanks to our adaptive sensor fusion while operating within the same average power budget (~400 mW).

中文摘要翻译: 基于人工智能的多模态传感器融合是一个在超低功耗嵌入式系统和信息物理系统中日益受到关注的重要课题，因为它能在真实场景约束下提升可靠性、准确性和鲁棒性。然而，在功耗低于100 mW的极端受限平台上增加传感器的数量需要在能耗与预测精度之间进行权衡。为实现这一目标，我们提出了一种新颖的自适应AI方法，将多模态传感器融合（相机、超声和飞行时间传感器）与轻量级循环Transformer架构（68.8万参数）相结合。我们通过一种在迭代间结合token传播与增量式传感器调用的机制来解决深度图估计任务。在每次迭代中，基于置信度的门控机制动态决定是否通过逐步添加更丰富但功耗更高的传感器输入来继续计算。token传播通过跨时间步传递上下文特征来确保时间一致性。为部署算法并测试首个真实世界原型，我们设计了一款新型印刷电路板，集成了三种传感器和超低功耗GWT GAP9多核片上系统。在NYUv2数据集上，将我们的自适应系统与使用全部传感器和迭代的同一流水线相比，我们仅损失4.8%的δ1精度，同时节省90%的能耗（2.44 mJ/帧）。此外，我们的自适应方法虽然参数减少9倍，但δ1精度仅比MobileDepth低5.6%。与同样运行在GAP9上的现有最优模型相比，我们的方法在相同的平均功耗预算（约400 mW）下，凭借自适应传感器融合将δ1精度提升了31.8%。

</details>


### [92] [Wavefront Parallelization for Efficient Learned Image Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19082)
*Shimon Murai,Fangzheng Lin,Kasidis Arunruangsirilert,Jiro Katto*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需重新训练的自回归图像压缩加速算法，通过波前并行重排推理顺序，实现超过13倍加速且不损失率失真性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 自回归上下文模型在 learned image compression 中推理速度极慢（串行），现有加速方法（如 checkerboard context）需修改架构并重新训练，无法直接应用于预训练模型，因此需要一种训练无关的推理加速方案。

Method: 借鉴视频编码标准中的波前并行（wavefront parallelism）思想，将自回归推理重新组织为最优的“交错”波前顺序，在保持严格自回归依赖关系的前提下，最小化串行步骤数，实现训练无关的推理加速。

Result: 在预训练自回归模型（如 Cheng et al. 的方法）上实现超过13倍加速，且保持原始率失真性能不变；此外，通过权衡上下文依赖关系的精确性，还可获得更快的解码速度。

Conclusion: 所提方法是一种完全无需训练、可直接应用于预训练模型的推理加速算法，能大幅提升自回归图像压缩模型的解码效率，同时保持原有压缩性能。

Original Abstract: Autoregressive context models are foundational for learned image compression,but they suffer from slow serial inference. Existing acceleration methods such as checkerboard context require architectural changes and retraining, thus are inapplicable to pre-trained models. We propose a completely training-free inference-time acceleration algorithm inspired by wavefront parallelism in video coding standards. Our method reorganizes inference into an optimal ``staggered'' wavefront order, minimizing sequential steps while maintaining exact autoregressive dependencies. Experimental results show our approach accelerates pre-trained autoregressive models (e.g., Cheng et al.) by more than $13\times$ while preserving the original rate-distortion performance. We also demonstrate that faster decoding is possible by trading off precise context dependencies. Source code will be available at https://github.com/tokkiwa/compressai-wavefront.

中文摘要翻译: 自回归上下文模型是学习型图像压缩的基础，但其串行推理速度极慢。现有的加速方法（如棋盘格上下文）需要修改网络架构并重新训练，因此无法应用于预训练模型。我们提出了一种完全无需训练的推理加速算法，该算法受视频编码标准中波前并行思想的启发。我们的方法将推理过程重新组织为最优的“交错”波前顺序，在保持严格自回归依赖关系的同时，最小化串行步骤数。实验结果表明，我们的方法在预训练自回归模型（例如Cheng等人的模型）上实现了超过13倍的加速，同时保持了原有的率失真性能。我们还证明，通过权衡上下文依赖关系的精确性，可以实现更快的解码速度。源代码将在 https://github.com/tokkiwa/compressai-wavefront 提供。

</details>


### [93] [Fast reconstruction of tensor tomographic X-ray scattering data for real-time applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19124)
*André M. Antunes,Daniël M. Pelt,K. Joost Batenburg*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种直接重建算法的扩展方法，通过代数滤波器将多次迭代更新近似为单次滤波和反投影步骤，将X射线散射张量层析成像的重建速度提升一个数量级以上，在商用硬件上1秒内完成53×53×53×28张量体积重建，为实时散射成像奠定基础。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的X射线散射张量层析成像依赖于缓慢的迭代重建，无法满足实时应用需求。

Method: 提出一种直接重建方法的扩展，针对平行束几何计算代数滤波器，将多次迭代更新近似为单次滤波和反投影步骤。通过显式分离层析投影算子与视角相关的混合算子，使方法能够泛化到不同张量表示和（小角）X射线散射测量模式。

Result: 模拟和实验结果表明，该方法在逼近迭代重建结果的同时，计算时间减少一个数量级以上，在商用硬件上1秒内实现了53×53×53×28张量体积的重建。

Conclusion: 该方法的速度和稳定性可实现高通量分析，为基于散射的实时成像开辟了道路。

Original Abstract: X-ray scattering tensor tomography reveals nanoscale structural orientation in 3D, but its reliance on slow iterative reconstruction limits real-time use. We introduce an extension of a direct reconstruction approach for parallel-beam geometries that computes algebraic filters approximating multiple iterative updates with a single filtering and back-projection step. By explicitly separating the tomographic projector from a view-dependent mixing operator, the method generalizes across tensor representations and modes of (small-angle) X-ray scattering measurement. Simulated and experimental results show that the approach approximates iterative reconstructions while reducing computation time by over an order of magnitude, realizing a reconstruction of a 53x53x53x28 tensor volume in 1 second on commercially-available hardware. The resulting speed and stability enable high-throughput analysis and open the door to real-time scattering-based imaging.

中文摘要翻译: X射线散射张量层析成像能够揭示三维空间中的纳米级结构取向，但其依赖缓慢的迭代重建限制了实时应用。本文提出一种针对平行束几何的直接重建方法的扩展，通过计算代数滤波器，将多次迭代更新近似为单次滤波和反投影步骤。通过显式分离层析投影算子与视角相关的混合算子，该方法能够泛化到不同的张量表示和（小角）X射线散射测量模式。模拟和实验结果表明，该方法在逼近迭代重建结果的同时，计算时间减少了一个数量级以上，在商用硬件上1秒内实现了53×53×53×28张量体积的重建。由此带来的速度和稳定性支持高通量分析，并为基于散射的实时成像打开了大门。

</details>


### [94] [MIRAGE: Multi-scale Lesion-Informed Representation with Auxiliary Guidance for MRI Contrast Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19137)
*Andrea Borghesi,Xin Wang,Jonas Teuwen,George Yiasemis*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出MIRAGE，一种结合全局重建损失、感知损失和三种病灶感知监督的残差2D U-Net，用于从增强前MRI单切片推断对比增强图像，在病灶定位上优于多种生成模型，并揭示了保真度与实用性的权衡。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从单个增强前乳腺MRI切片推断对比增强图像是一个欠定问题：增强后图像包含的生理信息无法唯一地从基线解剖结构编码。仅优化逐像素保真度会抑制不确定的病灶增强，而对抗性或随机生成目标虽能生成逼真的增强后外观，但无法保证患者特异的病灶保真度。现有方法存在保真度与病灶特异性之间的根本矛盾。

Method: 提出MIRAGE，一种残差2D U-Net，结合了全局重建损失和感知损失，并在训练过程中引入三种病灶感知监督：（1）对遗漏肿瘤增强的不对称惩罚；（2）多尺度辅助肿瘤分割；（3）通过冻结的增强后肿瘤分割nnU-Net提供指导。

Result: 在MAMA-SYNTH多中心数据的301个病例上，使用8种互补指标评估，MIRAGE在6项指标上排名第一，在病灶定位上显著优于调优的pix2pix、条件扩散和潜在桥接匹配基线。消融实验表明各损失函数在病灶定位上部分冗余，但对外观、影像组学和边界精度有不同影响。

Conclusion: 任务感知的合成方法能有效提升病灶定位性能，但其表面上的最优性是条件性的，依赖于用于定义实用性的下游模型和评估指标。生成式替代方案在LPIPS或对比分类上仍有优势，揭示了保真度与实用性之间明确的权衡。

Original Abstract: Inferring contrast enhancement from one pre-contrast breast MRI slice is underdetermined: post-contrast appearance contains physiological information that is not uniquely encoded in baseline anatomy. Optimizing only paired pixel fidelity can suppress uncertain lesion enhancement, whereas adversarial or stochastic generative objectives can favor realistic post-contrast appearance without guaranteeing patient-specific lesion fidelity. We introduce MIRAGE, a residual 2D U-Net that combines global reconstruction and perceptual losses with three forms of lesion-aware supervision available only during training: an asymmetric penalty for missed tumor enhancement, multi-scale auxiliary tumor segmentation, and guidance through a frozen post-contrast tumor segmentation nnU-Net. We evaluate the method on 301 cases from the multi-centre MAMA-SYNTH data using eight complementary image-, region-, radiomics-, and segmentation-based metrics. MIRAGE ranks first on six metrics and markedly improves downstream lesion localization over tuned pix2pix, conditional diffusion, and latent bridge-matching baselines. The generative alternatives retain advantages in LPIPS or contrast classification, revealing a clear fidelity-utility trade-off. Leave-one-in and leave-one-out ablations show that the losses are partly redundant for lesion localization but exert distinct effects on appearance, radiomics, and boundary accuracy. These results support task-aware synthesis while also showing that its apparent optimality is conditional on the downstream models and metrics used to define utility.

中文摘要翻译: 从单个增强前乳腺MRI切片推断对比增强图像是一个欠定问题：增强后图像包含的生理信息无法唯一地从基线解剖结构中编码。仅优化逐像素保真度会抑制不确定的病灶增强，而对抗性或随机生成目标虽能生成逼真的增强后外观，但无法保证患者特异的病灶保真度。我们提出MIRAGE，一种残差2D U-Net，结合了全局重建损失和感知损失，并引入仅在训练期间可用的三种形式的病灶感知监督：对遗漏肿瘤增强的不对称惩罚、多尺度辅助肿瘤分割，以及通过冻结的增强后肿瘤分割nnU-Net提供指导。我们在多中心MAMA-SYNTH数据的301个病例上，使用八种互补的基于图像、区域、影像组学和分割的指标进行了评估。MIRAGE在六项指标上排名第一，并在下游病灶定位上显著优于调优的pix2pix、条件扩散和潜在桥接匹配基线。生成式替代方案在LPIPS或对比分类上仍有优势，揭示了保真度与实用性之间明确的权衡。留一纳入和留一剔除消融实验表明，各损失函数在病灶定位上部分冗余，但对外观、影像组学和边界精度产生不同的影响。这些结果支持任务感知的合成方法，同时也表明其表面上的最优性取决于用于定义实用性的下游模型和指标。

</details>

### [95] [Analysis of inter-spike interval statistics in neuronal networks with depolarizing and hyperpolarizing threshold potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18428)
*Oliver Gambrell,Abhyudai Singh*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有固定和自适应阈值的整合-放电神经元模型在兴奋-抑制回路中的锋电位间隔（ISI）统计特性，发现自适应阈值模型相比固定阈值模型具有更大的ISI噪声，并揭示了超极化自适应阈值模型可在仅受抑制输入驱动时产生动作电位。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解神经元通信中动作电位生成的ISI统计特性，特别是自适应阈值机制对ISI噪声的影响，以及兴奋性和抑制性输入在不同频率下如何调控神经元的发放模式。

Method: 采用经典整合-放电神经元模型，分别对固定阈值、去极化自适应阈值和超极化自适应阈值三种情况进行建模，通过随机分析和数值仿真研究ISI统计量（如变异系数）随模型参数的变化规律。

Result: 1) 在相同平均ISI下，去极化自适应阈值模型的ISI噪声（变异系数）大于固定阈值模型；2) ISI噪声可表现为亚指数或超指数分布，取决于兴奋/抑制输入频率；3) 超极化自适应阈值模型中，仅抑制性输入即可驱动突触后神经元产生动作电位。

Conclusion: 该工作为自适应阈值模型在动作电位生成中的随机分析提供了系统性方法，揭示了自适应阈值机制在神经元间信息处理中的重要作用。

Original Abstract: Neuronal communication is mediated in part by changes in neuronal firing rates. The time interval between successive neuronal firings is referred to as the inter-spike interval (ISI), and quantifying its statistics is important for understanding neuronal communication. This paper studies the ISI statistics of a postsynaptic neuron receiving independent excitatory and inhibitory presynaptic action potentials (EI circuit). This circuit is modeled as a classical integrate-and-fire neuron, and the ISI statistics are investigated for both fixed and adaptive threshold potentials. First, a depolarizing adaptive threshold model is studied, where the threshold potential increases with the postsynaptic membrane potential. Our analysis shows that the ISI noise, quantified as the coefficient of variation, is larger in the adaptive threshold model compared to the fixed threshold model for the same mean ISI. Additionally, simulations reveal that the ISI noise can be either hypo- or hyper-exponential (defined as ISI noise smaller or larger than one, respectively) depending on the frequencies of excitatory and inhibitory inputs. Next, a hyperpolarizing adaptive threshold potential is studied, where the threshold decreases as the membrane potential hyperpolarizes. Interestingly, this model shows that the postsynaptic neuron can generate action potentials (APs) when driven solely by inhibitory inputs. Furthermore, mean and noise signatures are characterized across model parameters for both excitatory and inhibitory inputs. In summary, this work provides a systematic stochastic analysis of adaptive threshold models for AP generation to understand their role in interneuronal information processing.

中文摘要翻译: 神经元通信部分地通过神经元发放率的变化来介导。连续两次神经元发放之间的时间间隔称为锋电位间隔（ISI），量化其统计特性对于理解神经元通信至关重要。本文研究了接受独立兴奋性和抑制性突触前动作电位的突触后神经元（EI回路）的ISI统计特性。该回路被建模为经典的整合-放电神经元，并在固定阈值和自适应阈值电位下研究ISI统计特性。首先，研究了一种去极化自适应阈值模型，其中阈值电位随突触后膜电位升高而增加。我们的分析表明，在相同平均ISI下，自适应阈值模型的ISI噪声（以变异系数衡量）大于固定阈值模型。此外，仿真揭示，ISI噪声可以是亚指数或超指数分布（分别定义为ISI噪声小于或大于1），具体取决于兴奋性和抑制性输入的频率。其次，研究了一种超极化自适应阈值电位，其中阈值随膜电位超极化而降低。有趣的是，该模型表明，突触后神经元可以在仅由抑制性输入驱动时产生动作电位（AP）。此外，还针对兴奋性和抑制性输入，表征了跨模型参数的平均值和噪声特征。总之，本工作对用于AP生成的自适应阈值模型进行了系统的随机分析，以理解它们在神经元间信息处理中的作用。

</details>


### [96] [Competitive and Complementary Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18460)
*David C. Krakauer*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过将人类、工具和任务建模为一个共同演化的动力系统，发现人机协作中存在双稳态：当工具可用性超过临界阈值时，用户能力会崩溃至低依赖水平；降低可用性无法逆转崩溃，需降至更低阈值。两个拥有相同工具访问权限的用户可能处于截然相反的稳定状态（胜任 vs. 依赖），取决于他们先建立的是哪种状态。崩溃阈值取决于用户的初始能力和工具的透明性。当目标不确定时，工具可能导致人类丧失自主性，不可逆地将决策权转移给工具。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 理解人类在借助工具（尤其是大语言模型）完成任务时，用户的胜任能力（competence）和对外部工具的依赖（reliance）如何共同演化，以及这种演化是否会导致不可逆的能力下降。

Method: 将用户、工具和任务建模为一个共同演化的动力系统（dynamical system），通过数学分析识别系统的稳态和相变条件，并用GPS/地图使用、算术技能、语言模型等多个独立数据集进行验证。

Result: (1) 系统存在双稳态：胜任状态和依赖状态；(2) 工具可用性超过临界阈值时胜任状态被摧毁，能力崩溃至低水平；(3) 降低可用性无法逆转崩溃，需降至更低阈值，即存在滞后效应；(4) 崩溃阈值取决于用户的初始胜任能力和工具的透明性（用户能重建多少工具运作机制）；(5) 当目标不确定时，工具会导致不可逆的"能动性转移"（agency transfer）。

Conclusion: 工具的构建方式、AI的部署策略以及教育体系都需要重新审视：应设计"工具抵抗型"教育，防止用户因过度依赖工具而导致核心能力不可逆地丧失。

Original Abstract: Humans have always externalized thought onto tools, from the tally and the abacus to the map and, now, large language models. I model the agent, the tool, and the task as one dynamical system in which competence (what the user retains) and reliance (what the user outsources) co-evolve, and find that the outcome is bistable. Above a critical tool availability the competent state is destroyed and competence collapses toward a low dependent floor as the user outsources completely. Lowering availability does not reverse the collapse until a far lower threshold, so history of practice rather than the current tool fixes the state. Two users with the same present access can therefore occupy opposite and lasting states, one competent and one dependent, decided only by which they built first. The collapse threshold depends jointly on the competence a user brings to a task and on the tool's transparency, the fraction of its working a user can reconstruct. In the case where an agent faces an uncertain goal, a tool can cause agency itself to transfer to the tool and the human-agent becomes an agentic-instrument, irreversibly, because the tool's model is too large to internalize. The model is tested against several independent data sets, including GPS and map use, arithmetic expertise, and language models. These results reframe how tools should be built, how artificial intelligence is deployed, and what a tool-resistant education might require.

中文摘要翻译: 人类一直将思维外化到工具上——从计算筹码和算盘，到地图，再到如今的大语言模型。本文将用户（agent）、工具（tool）和任务（task）建模为一个动力系统，其中用户的胜任能力（competence，即用户保留的部分）和依赖程度（reliance，即用户外包的部分）共同演化，并发现该系统具有双稳态（bistable）。当工具可用性超过某一临界阈值时，胜任状态被摧毁，用户能力崩溃至一个低依赖水平，用户完全外包任务。降低工具可用性无法逆转这一崩溃，直到达到一个更低阈值；因此，决定系统状态的并非当前工具，而是用户的历史实践。两个当前具有相同工具访问权限的用户可能处于截然相反且持久的稳定状态——一个胜任、一个依赖——完全取决于他们先建立了哪种状态。崩溃阈值取决于用户面对任务时的初始胜任能力以及工具的透明性（transparency），即用户能够重建工具运作机制的比例。当用户面临不确定目标时，工具可能导致能动性（agency）本身转移至工具，人类用户变成"能动性工具"（agentic-instrument），且这一过程不可逆，因为工具的模型过于庞大而无法被人类内化。该模型在多个独立数据集上进行了验证，包括GPS与地图使用、算术技能以及语言模型。这些结果重新定义了工具应如何构建、人工智能应如何部署，以及"抗工具型"教育可能需要什么样的方案。

</details>


### [97] [How the fly holds a single goal: normalization, not selection, in Drosophila FC2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18969)
*Gioele Nanni,Christopher Lee*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文通过分析果蝇全脑连接组（FlyWire），发现扇形体FC2神经元的目标定向抑制主要来自FB5A细胞的全局性抑制，而非局部竞争机制，因此FC2网络不具备赢家通吃的环吸引子特性，而是执行目标归一化功能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究果蝇扇形体FC2神经元之间的抑制性连接回路是如何产生的，以及这种抑制是否允许FC2在多个竞争目标中主动选择（赢家通吃），还是仅仅维持外部设定的单一目标。

Method: 基于FlyWire单脑连接组数据，对FC2神经元的抑制性突触连接进行全脑追踪，计算FB5A、hDelta中间神经元和直接抑制分量的贡献比例；使用环吸引子模型、赢家通吃网络模型和脉冲神经网络等多种动力学模型进行数值仿真验证。

Result: 抑制几乎完全是全局性的：FB5A细胞对所有FC2神经元施加近乎均等的抑制，hDelta提供较小的距离依赖性贡献，直接抑制分量可忽略；FC2回路缺乏局部循环兴奋，无法形成环吸引子赢家通吃网络；在所有动力学模型中，连接组参考耦合强度下均无法锁定赢家；上游目标设定候选为hDelta网络，排除了原先假设的候选神经元（其输入占FC2总输入不足0.2%）。

Conclusion: FC2是对外部设定的目标进行归一化处理，而非选择目标；FB5A很可能充当全局归一化器（类似蘑菇体中的APL神经元）；同时明确指出两个未解决问题：hDelta提供的相互抑制在极强耦合下理论上可能实现选择功能，以及FB5A的抑制性身份尚需实验验证。

Original Abstract: A walking fly steers toward a goal direction, held as a bump of activity across the FC2 neurons of the fan-shaped body. These neurons also inhibit one another over distance, more strongly the farther apart they are, a feedback proposed to keep the fly on a single goal. We asked, from the connectome, what circuit produces this inhibition, and whether it lets FC2 actively choose one goal among competitors (a winner-take-all) or simply keeps a goal set elsewhere as one clean bump. Tracing the wiring in a single FlyWire brain, we find the inhibition is almost entirely global: four FB5A cells inhibit every FC2 neuron roughly equally, with a smaller, distance-dependent contribution from hDelta interneurons and a negligible direct component. A ring-attractor winner-take-all (the kind the compass uses) requires local recurrent excitation that the FC2 wiring lacks, so this geometry cannot build one; and across a range of dynamical models, including a spiking network, no version of the circuit locks onto a winner at the connectome-scaled reference coupling. FC2 therefore normalizes an externally set goal rather than selecting it, with FB5A likely acting as the global normalizer, much as the APL neuron does in the mushroom body. We are explicit about two open points: a different mechanism, mutual inhibition between two competing goals (which hDelta supplies), could in principle select at very strong coupling, and we bound rather than exclude it; and FB5A's inhibitory identity is a low-confidence prediction of the connectome's transmitter classifier, not yet measured, and likely not GABAergic. We then ask where the goal is actually set: the connectome nominates an upstream hDelta network and rules out the leading proposed alternative, whose neurons supply under 0.2% of FC2's input. Finally, we propose a direct experiment, silencing FB5A while imaging FC2, that would test the account.

中文摘要翻译: 一只行走的果蝇会朝着目标方向导航，该目标方向在扇形体FC2神经元中以活动凸块的形式保持。这些神经元还会相互抑制——相隔越远，抑制越强，这种反馈被认为能使果蝇保持单一目标。我们从连接组出发提出疑问：是什么回路产生了这种抑制，以及这种抑制是否让FC2在多个竞争目标中主动选择（赢家通吃），还是仅仅维持外部设定的目标形成一个干净的凸块。通过对单个FlyWire脑中的布线进行追踪，我们发现这种抑制几乎是完全全局性的：四个FB5A细胞对所有FC2神经元施加大致相等的抑制，hDelta中间神经元贡献了较小的距离依赖性分量，而直接抑制分量可忽略不计。环吸引子赢家通吃（指南针系统所用的类型）需要FC2布线所缺乏的局部循环兴奋，因此这种几何结构无法构建赢家通吃网络；在包括脉冲神经网络在内的一系列动力学模型中，没有一种版本的回路能在连接组尺度参考耦合强度下锁定赢家。因此，FC2是对外部设定的目标进行归一化，而非选择目标，FB5A很可能充当全局归一化器，类似于蘑菇体中的APL神经元。我们明确指出了两个未解决的问题：一种不同的机制——两个竞争目标之间的相互抑制（由hDelta提供）——在极强耦合条件下理论上可以做出选择，我们只是设定了边界而非完全排除这种可能性；FB5A的抑制性身份是连接组递质分类器的低置信度预测，尚未通过实验测量，且很可能不是GABA能的。随后，我们探究目标究竟在哪里设定：连接组数据提示上游存在一个hDelta网络，并排除了之前的主流候选方案——该方案所涉及的神经元仅提供FC2输入的不到0.2%。最后，我们提出了一项直接验证该假说的实验方案：在沉默FB5A的同时对FC2进行成像。

</details>


### [98] [Eccentricity-Constrained CNN Training Reveals Adaptive Information Coding Around the Visual Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19316)
*Dylan M. Diaz,Margaret M. Henderson*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究利用自我中心视频和眼动追踪数据，通过对比学习训练ResNet-18模型，验证了自然经验中不同偏心度视觉信息（中央凹vs周边）可以驱动视觉皮层产生功能分化，并发现周边模型在场景选择性皮层（PPA、RSC）中具有解释优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探索灵长类视觉系统中存在的"偏心度偏差"（中央视野偏好支持高分辨率细粒度任务如人脸识别，周边视野偏好支持场景理解）是否可以从自然经验中涌现，即验证生态化经验是否自适应性约束了皮层信息处理的组织方式。

Method: 使用Visual Experience Dataset（VEDB）中的自我中心视频和眼动追踪数据，通过SimCLR对比学习训练ResNet-18模型。将训练帧处理为三种条件：凝视依赖的中央凹裁剪、周边裁剪、以及应用NeuroFovea变换的周边裁剪。通过下游任务性能（VEDB帧分类、Places365场景分类、VGGFace2人脸识别）和与人类fMRI数据（NSD）的编码模型对齐度进行评估。

Result: 1) 中央凹与周边模型在不同类别上表现出系统性差异，表明不同偏心度在不同任务中的信息量不同；2) VEDB预训练模型在下游场景分类中比人脸识别泛化更好，且中央凹模型在两者上均更强；3) VEDB预训练模型的皮层神经预测性可与中等规模非自我中心数据集（ImageNet-100）训练的模型相当；4) 在场景选择性皮层（PPA、RSC）中，周边模型在解释方差上略优于中央凹模型。

Conclusion: 自我中心经验可能自适应地约束了皮层信息处理的组织方式，支持了偏心度依赖编码可以从自然经验中涌现的假说。

Original Abstract: In the primate visual system, center-preferring cortical populations have higher spatial resolution and overlap face- and word-selective regions while periphery-preferring populations have lower spatial resolution and overlap scene-selective regions. This "eccentricity bias" may reflect differential task-relevance: central vision may better support fine-grained tasks like face recognition and reading, while peripheral vision may better support scene understanding. To test whether eccentricity-dependent coding can emerge from natural experience, we used egocentric video and eye-tracking data from the Visual Experience Dataset (VEDB). We trained ResNet-18 models using contrastive learning (SimCLR) on frames modified to isolate different eccentricities (gaze-contingent fovea-only crops, periphery-only crops, and periphery-only crops with a NeuroFovea transform applied). We evaluated downstream task performance and model alignment with human fMRI data (Natural Scenes Dataset; encoding models). In-domain VEDB frame classification showed systematic differences between fovea- and periphery-only models across categories, indicating differential informativeness across tasks. On downstream classification, VEDB-pretrained models generalized better to scene categorization (Places365) than face recognition (VGGFace2), with fovea-only models stronger on both. Across visual cortex, VEDB-pretrained models matched neural predictivity of models trained on mid-sized non-egocentric datasets (ImageNet-100), suggesting egocentric data supports emergence of cortically-aligned representations. In scene-selective cortex (PPA, RSC), periphery-only models held a small but consistent advantage in explained variance over fovea-only models, suggesting these regions are aligned with peripheral statistics. Together, these results suggest egocentric experience may adaptively constrain cortical information processing.

中文摘要翻译: 在灵长类视觉系统中，偏好中央视野的皮层种群具有更高的空间分辨率，并与面孔和文字选择性区域重叠，而偏好周边视野的种群空间分辨率较低，并与场景选择性区域重叠。这种"偏心度偏差"可能反映了不同的任务相关性：中央视觉更有利于支持细粒度任务（如人脸识别和阅读），而周边视觉更有利于支持场景理解。为检验偏心度依赖的编码能否从自然经验中涌现，我们使用了视觉经验数据集（VEDB）中的自我中心视频和眼动追踪数据。我们采用对比学习（SimCLR）对经过不同偏心度处理（凝视依赖的中央凹裁剪、周边裁剪、以及应用NeuroFovea变换的周边裁剪）的视频帧训练ResNet-18模型。我们评估了模型的下游任务性能及其与人类fMRI数据（自然场景数据集；编码模型）的对齐程度。在域内VEDB帧分类中，中央凹与周边模型在不同类别间表现出系统性差异，表明不同任务的信息量存在差异。在下游分类任务上，VEDB预训练模型在场景分类（Places365）上的泛化能力优于人脸识别（VGGFace2），且中央凹模型在两者上均表现更强。在整个视觉皮层的预测性上，VEDB预训练模型与在中等规模非自我中心数据集（ImageNet-100）上训练的模型相当，表明自我中心数据能够支持皮层对齐表征的涌现。在场景选择性皮层（PPA、RSC）中，周边模型在解释方差上比中央凹模型具有微弱但一致的优势，提示这些区域与周边统计特性相对齐。综合来看，这些结果表明自我中心经验可能自适应地约束了皮层信息处理的组织方式。

</details>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [FALCON-Discover: Discovering Concentrated False-Confidence Regions for Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18278)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FALCON-Discover框架，通过置信度、局部支持度、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性等多维差异信号来发现模型预测中的局部过度自信错误（错误置信集中现象）。实验表明，该方法在多个表格数据集上优于传统校准基线，应将危险过置信视为族级发现问题而非单指标校准问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的校准评估通常在聚合层面上进行，但最危险的失败往往是局部的：预测虽错误却保持高度自信。作者旨在研究这种错误置信集中现象，并提出有效方法检测这种局部危险过置信。

Method: 提出FALCON-Discover框架，一种后验的、模型无关的方法。该框架利用置信度、局部支持度、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性产生的差异信号对预测进行排序，从而发现错误置信集中的区域。

Result: 在7个二元表格数据集、4个随机种子、5折交叉拟合以及XGBoost和CatBoost等强学习器上进行实验，发现错误置信集中现象具有重复性但依赖于具体场景。在主要置信阈值下，基于差异的排序在最强场景中显著优于最强的验证集选校准或信任评分基线，而原始置信度几乎无法捕获危险错误。最佳检测器因数据集而异：学习型差异在需要组合多个线索时最强，而稳定性中心排序在局部决策脆弱性主导时效果最好。

Conclusion: 危险过置信更适合作为一个族级发现（family-level discovery）问题来处理，而非单指标校准问题。这启示应设计明确针对置信度、支持度和稳定性出现分歧区域的校准策略。

Original Abstract: Calibration is usually evaluated in aggregate, but the most dangerous failures are often local: predictions that remain highly confident despite being wrong. We study this failure mode as false-confidence concentration, the extent to which confident errors occupy compact, discoverable regions of prediction space. We introduce FALCON-Discover, a post-hoc, model-agnostic framework that ranks predictions using discrepancy signals from confidence, local support, neighborhood agreement, and perturbation stability. Across seven binary tabular datasets, four seeds, five-fold cross-fitting, and strong learners including XGBoost and CatBoost, we find that false-confidence concentration is recurrent but regime-dependent. At the main confidence threshold, discrepancy-based ranking substantially outperforms the strongest validation-selected calibration or trust-scoring baseline in the strongest regimes, while raw confidence recovers little dangerous-error mass. The best detector varies across datasets: learned discrepancy is strongest when multiple cues must be combined, whereas stability-centered ranking works best when local decisional fragility dominates. These results show that dangerous overconfidence is better treated as a family-level discovery problem than as a single-score calibration problem, and motivate calibration strategies that explicitly target regions where confidence, support, and stability diverge.

中文摘要翻译: 校准通常在聚合层面上进行评估，但最危险的失败往往是局部的：预测虽然错误却仍保持高度自信。我们将这种失败模式作为错误置信集中（false-confidence concentration）现象来研究，即自信的错误占据预测空间中紧凑且可发现的区域的程度。我们提出了FALCON-Discover，一个后验的、模型无关的框架，该框架利用来自置信度、局部支持度、邻域一致性和扰动稳定性的差异信号对预测进行排序。在七个二元表格数据集、四个随机种子、五折交叉拟合以及包括XGBoost和CatBoost在内的强学习器上，我们发现错误置信集中现象具有重复性但依赖于具体场景。在主要置信阈值下，基于差异的排序在最强场景中显著优于最强的验证集选校准或信任评分基线，而原始置信度几乎无法捕获危险错误的质量。最佳检测器因数据集而异：当需要组合多个线索时，学习型差异表现最强；而当局部决策脆弱性占主导时，稳定性中心排序效果最好。这些结果表明，危险过置信更适合作为一个族级发现问题而非单指标校准问题来处理，并启示我们设计明确针对置信度、支持度和稳定性出现分歧区域的校准策略。

</details>


### [100] [Beyond Output-Space Calibration: Spectral Evidence Bundling for Selective Reliability Estimation in Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18279)
*Filippo Cenacchi,Longbing Cao,Runze Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对时间序列分类的后验校准中存在的三个可靠性缺口，提出了一种验证门控固定标签可靠性策略。该方法将输出侧线索与全样本频谱描述符（包括频带能量、熵、峰值主导度、周期支持度和相位稳定性）结合，生成标量可靠性估计和诊断级频带证据。通过验证门控机制，仅在正确性排序改进且不违反特定容差时启用频谱条件化，否则回退到输出空间基线。在8个UCR/UEA数据集、8种时间序列骨干网络和标准重校准器上的实验表明，该方法显著提升了选择性可靠性指标（Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.779/0.786），并降低了FalseConf@0.9至0.094。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有时间序列分类的后验校准方法通常重新映射输出分数，但在部署决策（如信任、弃权、审查）中存在三个关键缺口：(1) 相同的置信度值可能隐藏不同的时间序列支撑证据；(2) 平均校准指标可能遗漏高置信度的错误预测；(3) 输出空间的重校准方法缺乏与输入相关联的可审计性。这三个缺口促使作者提出一种能结合输出置信度与时间序列频谱证据的可靠性估计方法。

Method: 提出验证门控固定标签可靠性策略：保持骨干网络预测不变，仅估计是否需要信任该预测。方法结合输出侧线索（置信度）与全样本频谱描述符（频带能量、熵、峰值主导度、周期支持度、相位稳定性），形成标量可靠性估计和诊断级频带证据。引入验证门控机制，仅在正确性排序改进且不违反FalseConf@0.9或AURC容差时使用频谱条件化，否则回退到更安全的输出空间基线。

Result: 在8个异构UCR/UEA数据集、8种时间序列骨干网络和标准重校准器上的实验表明：无约束方法在匹配评估子集上改进固定标签选择性可靠性指标，将Corr-AURC从0.693提升至0.779；验证门控策略进一步将Corr-AURC提升至0.786，并将FalseConf@0.9降至0.094。

Conclusion: 时间序列分类器的可靠性估计受益于将输出置信度与频谱证据相结合，而验证门控机制可以防止无充分支持的频谱条件化，确保方法的稳健性和安全性。

Original Abstract: Post-hoc calibration for time-series classification usually remaps output scores, but deployment decisions such as trust, abstention, and review depend on whether a confident prediction is supported by the current temporal signal. We address three time-series reliability gaps: identical confidence values can hide different temporal support, average calibration can miss false high-confidence errors, and output-space recalibration offers limited input-linked auditability. We introduce a validation-gated fixed-label reliability policy that keeps the backbone prediction unchanged while estimating whether it should be trusted. The method combines output-side cues with whole-sample spectral descriptors, including band energy, entropy, peak dominance, period support, and phase stability, to form a scalar reliability estimate and diagnostic band-level evidence. A validation gate enables spectral conditioning only when correctness ranking improves without breaching FalseConf@0.9 or AURC tolerances; otherwise it reverts to the safer output-space baseline. Across eight heterogeneous UCR/UEA datasets, eight time-series backbone families, and standard recalibrators, the unconstrained method improves fixed-label selective-reliability metrics on the matched evaluation subset, raising Corr-AURC from 0.693 to 0.779. The validation-gated policy further improves Corr-AURC to 0.786 and reduces FalseConf@0.9 to 0.094. These results suggest that reliability estimation for time-series classifiers benefits from bundling output confidence with spectral evidence, while validation gating prevents unsupported spectral conditioning.

中文摘要翻译: 时间序列分类的事后校准通常重新映射输出分数，但诸如信任、弃权和审查等部署决策取决于一个置信的预测是否得到当前时间信号的支持。我们解决了三个时间序列可靠性缺口：相同的置信度值可能隐藏不同的时间支撑、平均校准可能遗漏高置信度的错误、以及输出空间重校准提供的与输入关联的可审计性有限。我们引入了一种验证门控固定标签可靠性策略，该策略保持骨干网络预测不变，同时估计是否应信任该预测。该方法将输出侧线索与全样本频谱描述符（包括频带能量、熵、峰值主导度、周期支持度和相位稳定性）相结合，形成标量可靠性估计和诊断级频带证据。验证门控机制仅在正确性排名改进且不违反FalseConf@0.9或AURC容差时启用频谱条件化；否则回退到更安全的输出空间基线。在八个异构UCR/UEA数据集、八种时间序列骨干网络家族和标准重校准器上，无约束方法在匹配评估子集上改进了固定标签选择性可靠性指标，将Corr-AURC从0.693提高到0.779。验证门控策略进一步将Corr-AURC提高到0.786，并将FalseConf@0.9降至0.094。这些结果表明，时间序列分类器的可靠性估计受益于将输出置信度与频谱证据相结合，同时验证门控机制可防止无充分支持的频谱条件化。

</details>


### [101] [Beyond Single-Dimensional Compression: The Compound Sparsity Frontier of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18280)
*Chao Han,Haozhe Hu,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合低秩近似、通道剪枝（静态）和逐token动态层跳过（动态）的复合稀疏框架，证明在相同总稀疏度下，复合压缩优于单一机制，并揭示了参数剪枝与token跳过之间存在跨维度干扰，平衡分配效果最佳。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型在静态参数剪枝或动态token级计算压缩中，一旦稀疏度超过必要边界，性能会急剧下降。本文探索将两种机制结合，通过分摊压缩负担来延缓性能退化。

Method: 首先通过低秩近似和通道剪枝获得静态压缩骨架，然后引入轻量级路由器实现逐token动态层跳过，从而独立控制参数稀疏度和token级计算稀疏度。

Result: 在语言理解和建模基准上，复合稀疏在相同总稀疏度下始终优于单一机制压缩，延迟了理解任务的性能衰减点并保持更强的建模性能；分析表明参数剪枝与token跳过之间存在跨维度干扰，且在固定稀疏度预算下近似平衡分配最有效。

Conclusion: 复合压缩为提高大语言模型压缩效果提供了实用途径，同时揭示了更广泛的跨维度稀疏度边界限制了进一步压缩的可能性。

Original Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often compressed through static parameter pruning or dynamic token-level computation, yet aggressive sparsification can trigger rapid performance degradation beyond an essential sparsity boundary. This work asks \emph{whether combining these two mechanisms can delay such degradation by distributing the compression burden}. We study a minimalist compound sparsity framework that first applies low-rank approximation and channel pruning to obtain a statically compressed backbone, and then introduces lightweight routers for per-token dynamic layer skipping. This design enables independent control of parameter sparsity and token-level computation sparsity. Experiments across language understanding and modeling benchmarks show that compound sparsity consistently outperforms single-mechanism compression under the same total sparsity, delaying the decay point on understanding tasks and preserving stronger modeling performance. Further analysis reveals cross-dimensional interference between parameter pruning and token skipping, and shows that near-balanced allocation is most effective under a fixed sparsity budget. These results demonstrate that compound compression provides a practical way to improve LLM compression, while revealing a broader cross-dimensional sparsity boundary that ultimately limits further compression. Code will be available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning.

中文摘要翻译: 大语言模型通常通过静态参数剪枝或动态token级计算进行压缩，然而在超过必要稀疏度边界后，激进的稀疏化会引发快速性能退化。本文探讨了将这两种机制结合起来能否通过分摊压缩负担来延缓这种退化。我们研究了一个极简的复合稀疏框架：首先应用低秩近似和通道剪枝获得静态压缩骨架，然后引入轻量级路由器实现逐token动态层跳过。该设计能够独立控制参数稀疏度和token级计算稀疏度。在语言理解和建模基准上的实验表明，在相同的总稀疏度下，复合稀疏始终优于单一机制压缩，延迟了理解任务的性能衰减点，并保持了更强的建模性能。进一步分析揭示了参数剪枝与token跳过之间存在跨维度干扰，并表明在固定稀疏度预算下，近似平衡的分配最为有效。这些结果表明，复合压缩为提高大语言模型压缩效果提供了一种实用途径，同时揭示了更广泛的跨维度稀疏度边界最终限制了进一步压缩。代码将发布于 https://github.com/EIT-NLP/LLM-Pruning。

</details>


### [102] [ALAS: Additive Learnable Alpha-Stable Kernels for Flexible Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18282)
*Weibo Huang,Cheng Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于对称α-稳定谱分量的柔性高斯过程核族ALAS，通过学习稳定性参数α自适应调整光滑度，在多种优化任务上表现出稳健性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 贝叶斯优化的成功依赖于选择与目标函数未知结构匹配的核函数，而现有方法的核选择不够灵活，难以同时捕捉光滑趋势和尖锐不规则性。

Method: 提出了ALAS核族，基于对称α-稳定谱分量构建高斯过程核；通过学习稳定性参数α，自适应调整有效光滑度；给出两种参数化形式：ALAS（单一平稳分量，联合谱调制）和ALAS-Sep（可分离变体，学习逐维尾行为以提升近似可分解目标的鲁棒性）。

Result: 在标准基准测试和真实世界代理模型上的实验表明，ALAS在多种不同设置下均表现出强劲且稳健的性能。

Conclusion: ALAS核族通过自适应学习光滑度，有效平衡了光滑趋势与尖锐不规则性的建模，提升了贝叶斯优化在未知结构目标上的鲁棒性与通用性。

Original Abstract: Bayesian Optimization is widely used for expensive black-box optimization, yet its success often depends on choosing a kernel that matches the objective's unknown structure. In this work, we propose ALAS, a flexible Gaussian Process kernel family built from symmetric $α$-stable spectral components. By learning the stability parameter $α$, ALAS adapts its effective smoothness from data, capturing both smooth trends and sharp irregularities. We present two parameterizations: ALAS, a single stationary component with joint spectral modulation, and ALAS-Sep, a separable variant that learns dimension-wise tail behavior to improve robustness on approximately decomposable objectives. Experiments on standard benchmarks and real-world surrogates demonstrate strong and robust performance across diverse settings.

中文摘要翻译: 贝叶斯优化被广泛用于昂贵的黑箱优化问题，然而其成功往往依赖于选择与目标函数未知结构相匹配的核函数。在这项工作中，我们提出了ALAS，一个从对称α-稳定谱分量构建的灵活高斯过程核族。通过学习稳定性参数α，ALAS能够根据数据自适应调整其有效光滑度，同时捕捉光滑趋势和尖锐的不规则性。我们给出了两种参数化形式：ALAS，一个具有联合谱调制的单一平稳分量，以及ALAS-Sep，一个可分离变体，通过学习逐维尾行为来提升在近似可分解目标上的鲁棒性。在标准基准测试和真实世界代理模型上的实验表明，ALAS在多种不同设置下均展现出强劲且稳健的性能。

</details>


### [103] [FedCC: A Low-Resource Federated Adaptation of Foundation Models for Robust Corpus Callosum localization in Fetal Ultrasound Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18283)
*Alessandro Di Matteo,Sara Moccia,Giuseppe Rizzo,Gianpaolo Grisolia,Ricciarda Raffaelli,Lorenzo Vasciaveo,Francesco D'Antonio,Maria Chiara Fiorentino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedCC，一个基于联邦学习的胎儿超声图像胼胝体定位框架，通过冻结DINOv2骨干网络、结合轻量YOLO检测头和LoRA微调，在无需数据共享的多中心低资源场景下，以仅2.9M可训练参数（约为全微调的1/8.5）实现了mAP@50达0.857和F1-score达0.803的优异性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 胎儿超声图像中胼胝体的精确定位对于早期识别神经发育异常至关重要，但由于超声成像固有的低对比度、散斑噪声以及胼胝体显著的解剖变异性，该任务极具挑战。现有方法通常需要集中式数据训练，但医疗数据因隐私法规难以共享，且临床环境资源受限，亟需一种无需数据共享、通信和计算开销低的隐私保护解决方案。

Method: FedCC框架基于联邦学习设计，采用冻结的DINOv2视觉Transformer作为骨干网络提取通用特征，结合轻量级YOLO检测头实现目标定位。通过引入低秩适配（LoRA）模块进行参数高效微调，仅优化和交换少量可训练参数，大幅降低计算与通信开销。采用FedAvg策略进行客户端间模型聚合，无需共享原始数据。

Result: 在包含10,970帧超声图像、来自58名孕妇、3个临床中心的多中心数据集上，FedCC在联邦设置下表现优异：DINOv2+LoRA+FedAvg组合在mAP@50上达0.857，F1-score达0.803，优于全微调和编码器冻结基线。可训练参数从全微调的24.4M降至2.9M，通信成本降低约8.5倍。

Conclusion: FedCC展示了联邦学习结合自监督视觉基础模型（DINOv2）与参数高效微调（LoRA）在胎儿神经超声检查中的可行性，为可扩展、隐私保护且临床可部署的AI系统迈出了有希望的一步。

Original Abstract: Accurate localization of the corpus callosum (CC) in fetal ultrasound (US) images is crucial for the early identification of neurodevelopmental abnormalities. However, this task remains highly challenging due to the intrinsic limitations of US imaging, including low contrast, speckle noise, and the considerable anatomical variability of the CC. We propose FedCC, a federated learning (FL)-based framework for CC localization in fetal US images, specifically designed for realistic multi-center and resource-constrained clinical settings without requiring data sharing. The framework integrates a frozen DINOv2 backbone with a lightweight YOLO-based detection head. To enable parameter-efficient adaptation, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules are incorporated, allowing only a small subset of parameters to be optimized and exchanged among clients. This strategy substantially reduces both computational and communication overhead, making the framework suitable for low-resource environments. The proposed approach was evaluated on a multi-center dataset comprising 10,970 ultrasound frames acquired from 58 pregnant women during routine neurosonographic examinations across three clinical sites using heterogeneous imaging devices. The proposed framework achieved strong performance in the federated setting. In particular, the combination of DINOv2 and LoRA under the FedAvg strategy achieved an average mAP@50 of 0.857 and an F1-score of 0.803, outperforming both full fine-tuning and encoder-freezing baselines. Notably, the proposed approach reduced the number of trainable parameters to 2.9M compared with 24.4M in full fine-tuning, corresponding to an approximately 8.5$\times$ reduction in communication cost. These findings represent a promising step toward scalable, privacy-preserving, and clinically deployable AI systems for fetal neurosonography.

中文摘要翻译: 在胎儿超声图像中精确定位胼胝体对于早期识别神经发育异常至关重要。然而，由于超声成像固有的局限性，包括低对比度、散斑噪声以及胼胝体显著的解剖变异性，该任务极具挑战性。本文提出FedCC，一个基于联邦学习的胎儿超声图像胼胝体定位框架，专门设计用于无需数据共享的现实多中心和资源受限的临床场景。该框架集成了冻结的DINOv2骨干网络与轻量级基于YOLO的检测头。为了实现参数高效的自适应，引入了低秩适配模块，仅优化和交换一小部分参数。这一策略大幅降低了计算和通信开销，使框架适用于低资源环境。该方法在包含10,970帧超声图像的多中心数据集上进行了评估，该数据集来自三个临床中心使用异构成像设备对58名孕妇进行的常规神经超声检查。所提框架在联邦设置下取得了强性能。特别地，在FedAvg策略下，DINOv2与LoRA的组合实现了平均mAP@50为0.857、F1分数为0.803，优于全微调和编码器冻结基线。值得注意的是，所提方法将可训练参数减少至2.9M，而全微调为24.4M，通信成本降低了约8.5倍。这些发现代表了向可扩展、保护隐私且可临床部署的胎儿神经超声检查AI系统迈出的有希望一步。

</details>


### [104] [Compressing What Matters: Neuron Importance Meets Data-Aware Low Rank Approximation for Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18284)
*Athanasios Ntovas,Alexandros Doumanoglou,Petros Drakoulis,Dimitris Zarpalas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合参数重要性和逐层功能等价性的混合低秩压缩方法，并设计了一种计算高效的动态压缩率分配算法，在保持模型性能的同时显著提升压缩效果，尤其在高压缩比下优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大语言模型参数规模庞大导致内存需求巨大，限制了其在资源受限环境下的应用。现有SVD压缩方法要么从参数重要性角度出发，要么从逐层功能等价性角度出发，两者各自孤立研究；同时，压缩率的分配大多是均匀分配或依赖昂贵的启发式搜索。本文旨在结合两种视角并设计更高效的压缩率分配策略。

Method: 提出一种将参数重要性和逐层功能等价性两种视角融合到单一目标函数中的低秩近似方法；并在此基础上设计了一种增强的计算高效的动态压缩率分配算法，替代传统的均匀分配或昂贵启发式搜索。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法在性能上与现有最优方法持平或显著优于它们，尤其在高压缩比下优势明显。

Conclusion: 结合参数重要性和功能等价性的混合压缩策略，配合动态压缩率分配，能更有效地压缩大语言模型，降低内存占用，同时保持下游任务性能。

Original Abstract: To excel at their domain large language models are comprised of billions of parameters. Yet this comes at the cost of huge memory requirements restricting their applicability in resource-constrained environments. To address the problem of neural network (NN) compression Singular Value Decomposition (SVD) has played a key role as a fundamental component for matrix compression through decomposition. To minimize compression error and to maximize the efficacy of the compressed model on the downstream tasks previous works focused on low-rank approximation of the NN's weight matrices either from the perspective of parameter importance or per-layer functional equivalence. While previous works studied the aforementioned perspectives in isolation in this work we are investigating the effectiveness of an approach that combines ideas from these two perspectives in a single objective. In parallel to this an important aspect that affects the compression quality is the distribution of the compression rate across layers and NN parameters. Earlier works mostly considered distributing the compression rate uniformly across layers and network weights or relied on computationally expensive heuristic search. Contrary to them in this work we propose an enhanced and computationally efficient algorithm for dynamic compression rate allocation. Experimental results support the efficacy of the proposed approach which performs on par or substantially better than the previous state-of-the-art especially under high compression ratios.

中文摘要翻译: 为了在其领域表现出色，大语言模型由数十亿个参数组成。然而，这以巨大的内存需求为代价，限制了它们在资源受限环境中的适用性。为了解决神经网络（NN）压缩问题，奇异值分解（SVD）作为通过分解实现矩阵压缩的基本组件发挥了关键作用。为了最小化压缩误差并最大化压缩模型在下游任务上的有效性，先前的工作从参数重要性或逐层功能等价性的角度专注于NN权重矩阵的低秩近似。虽然先前的工作孤立地研究了上述视角，但在这项工作中，我们正在研究将这两种视角的思想结合到单一目标中的方法的有效性。与此并行的是，影响压缩质量的一个重要方面是压缩率在各层和NN参数之间的分布。早期的工作大多考虑在层和网络权重之间均匀分配压缩率，或依赖于计算昂贵的启发式搜索。与它们不同，在这项工作中，我们提出了一种增强且计算高效的动态压缩率分配算法。实验结果支持了所提出方法的有效性，尤其在高压縮比下，其性能与先前的最优方法持平或显著优于后者。

</details>


### [105] [Conditioned Direct Feedback Alignment via Activity and Error Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18574)
*Houman Safaai,Varun Reddy,Bernardo L. Sabatini*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了直接反馈对齐（DFA）训练中由外积局部权重更新导致的各向异性失败模式，发现活动预条件和误差预条件可分别提升性能，并提出了归一化DFA（nDFA）系列方法，但指出这并非BP的通用替代方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: DFA虽避免了BP的转置权重回传，但其局部权重更新基于外积计算，因此各向异性可通过突触前活动因子或局部误差因子引入。本文旨在识别并独立分析这两种不同的失败模式，并探索通过条件化（preconditioning）来缓解问题。

Method: 通过受控合成实验分离活动条件化和误差条件化的效果；提出归一化DFA（nDFA）系列，包括活动nDFA（右乘逆活动二阶矩）、误差nDFA（左乘逆局部误差二阶矩）以及K-nDFA（结合两者并分别调节阻尼）；利用线性化对齐后计算获得精确的输入侧谱恒等式和Kronecker因子动机；通过范数匹配排除标量步长解释。

Result: 活动条件化在任务无关噪声方向具有高方差时带来约40个百分点的提升；误差条件化提升原始DFA 1.77-7.53个百分点，两者结合在活动条件化基础上额外提升0.40-0.90个百分点。该发现在tanh/one-vs-rest MNIST、预注册Fashion-MNIST和ReLU/softmax MNIST上得到验证。

Conclusion: 将条件化DFA定位为因子层面的研究，揭示局部外积规则何时失效，而非作为BP的通用替代方案或全层卷积信用分配的完整解决方案。误差因子欠阻尼时脆弱，BatchNorm可作为活动侧的强替代，卷积网络的收益仍不完整。

Original Abstract: Direct feedback alignment (DFA) trains hidden layers with fixed random projections of the output error, avoiding the transposed-weight backward pass of backpropagation (BP). We study a failure mode of DFA training that is distinct from feedback quality: the local weight update is calculated by an outer product, so anisotropy can enter through either its presynaptic-activity factor or its local-error factor. Our analyses with controlled synthetic regimes isolate the first failure mode and show an approximately 40-percentage-point activity-conditioning gain when high-variance directions contain task-irrelevant nuisance. Three clean confirmations isolate a different regime: error conditioning improves raw DFA by 1.77--7.53 percentage points, and combining independently selected activity and error factors adds 0.40--0.90 points over activity conditioning. The signs hold for tanh/one-vs-rest MNIST and preregistered Fashion-MNIST, and replicate on eight fresh seeds in a ReLU/softmax MNIST model. This factorization yields a symmetric block-local family of normalized DFA (nDFA): activity nDFA right-preconditions by an inverse activity second moment, error nDFA left-preconditions by an inverse local-error second moment, and K-nDFA applies both factors with separately tuned damping. A linearized post-alignment calculation gives an exact input-side spectral identity and a Kronecker-factor motivation for the two-sided rule, whereas norm matching rules out a scalar step-size explanation. The error factor is fragile when under-damped, BatchNorm is a strong activity-side alternative, and convnet gains remain partial. We therefore frame conditioned DFA as a factor-level study of when local outer-product rules fail, not as a general replacement for BP or a solution to all-layer convolutional credit assignment.

中文摘要翻译: 直接反馈对齐（DFA）通过使用输出误差的固定随机投影来训练隐藏层，从而避免了反向传播（BP）的转置权重回传。我们研究了DFA训练中一种与反馈质量截然不同的失败模式：局部权重更新由外积计算，因此各向异性可通过其突触前活动因子或局部误差因子引入。我们利用受控合成机制的分析隔离了第一种失败模式，并显示当高方差方向包含任务无关噪声时，活动条件化带来约40个百分点的增益。三项清晰的验证隔离了不同的机制：误差条件化将原始DFA提升了1.77-7.53个百分点，而独立选择的活动因子和误差因子相结合在活动条件化基础上额外增加0.40-0.90个百分点。这些结论在tanh/one-vs-rest MNIST和预注册的Fashion-MNIST上成立，并在ReLU/softmax MNIST模型上用八个新种子进行了复现。这种分解产生了一个对称的块局部归一化DFA（nDFA）族：活动nDFA通过逆活动二阶矩进行右预条件，误差nDFA通过逆局部误差二阶矩进行左预条件，K-nDFA通过分别调节阻尼的两个因子进行应用。线性化对齐后计算给出了精确的输入侧谱恒等式和对双侧规则的Kronecker因子动机，而范数匹配则排除了标量步长的解释。误差因子在欠阻尼时较为脆弱，BatchNorm是一个强大的活动侧替代方案，而卷积网络的收益仍然有限。因此，我们将条件化DFA定位为局部外积规则何时失效的因子层面研究，而非BP的通用替代方案或全层卷积信用分配的解决方案。

</details>


### [106] [Edge-Efficient Transformer for End-to-End RF Spectrum Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18285)
*Zhifan Song,Haralampos-G. Stratigopoulos,Hassan Aboushady*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出E-SpecFormer，一种用于自动调制识别和隐蔽信道识别的端到端边缘智能Transformer模型，通过引入无Softmax和LayerNorm的线性Tanh注意力机制LiTAN，在极低参数量（<10k）和极快推理速度（92μs/帧）下达到超越现有边缘模型的准确率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习模型在频谱智能任务中计算开销大，难以部署在资源受限的物联网边缘设备上，且传统注意力机制（Softmax+LayerNorm）在射频任务中效率不足。因此需要设计一种兼顾高精度、低参数量和低延迟的边缘友好型模型。

Method: 提出E-SpecFormer模型，其核心是LiTAN（线性Tanh注意力网络），一种去除Softmax和LayerNorm的轻量级注意力机制。模型提供Nano、Small、Medium、Large四种参数化变体以适应不同硬件约束，并在FPGA/CPU协同架构上实现高效推理。

Result: 在RadioML2018调制识别数据集上，Nano变体在SNR>0 dB时平均准确率86.5%；在基于硬件木马的隐蔽信道数据集上准确率达94.2%。参数量少于10k，FPGA/CPU协同推理速度达92μs/帧，以极低成本超越现有边缘模型。

Conclusion: E-SpecFormer被证明是一种边缘高效的解决方案，适用于物联网设备上的实时频谱智能任务，在精度、速度和资源消耗之间取得了优异平衡。

Original Abstract: We present E-SpecFormer (Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer) for end-to-end automatic modulation and covert channel (CC) recognition. We introduce LiTAN (Linear Tanh Attention Network), a Softmax- and LayerNorm-free attention mechanism that reduces complexity while increasing accuracy in RF tasks. E-SpecFormer is parameterized in four scalable variants (Nano, Small, Medium, Large) to accommodate diverse hardware constraints. Using the RadioML2018 dataset for modulation recognition, the Nano variant achieves 86.5% average accuracy for Signal-to-Noise Ratios (SNRs)>0 dB, and on the hardware Trojan (HT)-based CC dataset it reaches 94.2% accuracy, both with fewer than 10k parameters and up to speed of 92 μs per frame on FPGA/CPU co-execution, surpassing state-of-the-art edge models at a fraction of their cost. These results establish E-SpecFormer as an edge-efficient solution for real-time spectrum intelligence on Internet of Things (IoT) devices. GitHub link to the repository: https://github.com/zsniko/E-SpecFormer.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了E-SpecFormer（Edge Spectrum monitoring Transformer，边缘频谱监测Transformer），用于端到端的自动调制识别和隐蔽信道识别。我们引入了LiTAN（线性Tanh注意力网络），一种去除了Softmax和LayerNorm的注意力机制，它在降低计算复杂度的同时提升了射频任务的准确率。E-SpecFormer提供了四种可扩展的参数量变体（Nano、Small、Medium、Large），以适应不同的硬件约束。在RadioML2018数据集上进行调制识别任务时，Nano变体在信噪比大于0 dB的条件下平均准确率达到86.5%；在基于硬件木马的隐蔽信道数据集上，其准确率达到94.2%。两者均在参数量少于10k、FPGA/CPU协同执行速度高达每帧92微秒的条件下实现，以极低的成本超越了最先进的边缘模型。这些结果表明，E-SpecFormer是物联网设备上实时频谱智能的一种边缘高效解决方案。GitHub仓库链接：https://github.com/zsniko/E-SpecFormer。

</details>


### [107] [Is EEG-to-Text Feasible in Real-World Scenarios? An In-Depth Analysis Using a Neuropsychology-Inspired Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18749)
*Zihan Zhang,Yu Bao,Xiao Ding,Tianyi Jiang,Kai Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现现有EEG到文本解码（EEG2Text）模型因忽视EEG信号的不稳定性而依赖教师强制（teacher-forcing）评估，无法在实际中应用。作者通过神经心理学实验证实了无需教师强制即可解码的可行性，并构建了COFETT基准数据集（128通道高密度EEG），实现了无需教师强制的稳健评估，为实际应用开辟了道路。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EEG2Text模型依赖teacher-forcing评估，离开该机制无法生成有意义的解码结果，导致该方法无法应用于真实场景，并引发学界对EEG是否包含可解码语言信息的广泛争议。本文旨在澄清这一争议并推动EEG2Text的实际应用。

Method: 采用神经心理学启发的实验范式，发现现有基准忽视了EEG信号的不稳定性这一混淆因素；基于此发现，使用128通道高密度EEG帽采集数据，构建COFETT基准数据集，支持无需teacher-forcing的稳健评估。

Result: 现有EEG2Text基准因忽视EEG不稳定性而导致推断混淆；实验证据表明无需teacher-forcing的EEG2Text解码是可行的；COFETT在区分不同模型性能方面达到SOTA水平，并支持无需teacher-forcing的稳健评估。

Conclusion: EEG2Text是一个有意义的、可行的研究方向，通过解决EEG不稳定性的问题并构建合适的基准，可以实现无需teacher-forcing的稳健解码，为EEG2Text的实际应用铺平道路。

Original Abstract: Translating brain signals into text could restore communication for people with severe paralysis, yet practically usable systems to date rely on invasive electrocorticography (ECoG). Electroencephalography (EEG) offers a non-invasive alternative, and EEG-to-text (EEG2Text) has been widely explored. Interestingly, however, EEG2Text models generally rely on teacher-forcing evaluation; without it, they fail to generate meaningful decoding.
