中文摘要翻译: 我们研究双因子低精度量化乘积C=AB的计算。针对独立、零均值、已知方差场的逐元素误差，我们推导了期望平方乘积误差的精确有限维恒等式；该恒等式对非过载减性抖动和独立随机舍入严格成立，并对确定性四舍五入（RTN）进行了经验评估。利用乘积保持等价性AB=(AT)(T^{-1}B)，我们提出了缩放假面预处理方法：联合选择因子表示及其在量化前的共享模式。预处理器可降低乘积误差，但可能需要额外的变换后量化副本；共享变换需要一个副本，块特定变换最多每块一个副本。在正对角放缩（放缩因子）的有界族内，几何规划可计算全局最优共享放缩因子，线性规划可判断恒等放缩是否已最优。对其他族我们推导了可计算的选取统计量——放缩的尾指数、剖面的色散度、旋转的相干性和加权Gram能量、层次深度的切片能量协方差——以及用于候选排序的上界。在一个训练好的三块图像分类器的12个线性乘积上，抖动模型预测与确定性RTN误差的中位秩相关系数在8位时为0.937，4位时为0.918。几何规划最优放缩相比恒等放缩使保留的乘积误差降低18.0%（8位）和20.5%（4位）的几何均值，在两种精度及12个乘积中的10个上均优于SmoothQuant风格网格基线，并使组合logit MSE降低15.4%和26.4%。因此，我们提供了精确的随机乘积误差核算、对角族内的认证选择，以及一个在RTN下评估可复用变换候选的统一目标函数。

</details>


### [164] [ConceptCF: Concept-based Counterfactuals for the Explainability of Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18748)
*Annemarie Jutte,Faizan Ahmed,Jeroen Linssen,Maurice van Keulen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ConceptCF 是一种基于可解释概念进行反事实生成的方法，通过时间序列分解构建概念（如幅度、频带），并利用遗传算法优化概念突变，在有效性、置信度、邻近性、稀疏性和合理性等指标上达到一流性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在高风险领域（如医疗、预测性维护）中，AI模型的可解释性至关重要，现有时间序列反事实方法在单个点或子序列上操作，无法保证变异的可解释性。因此需要一种在人类可理解的概念层面进行反事实解释的方法。

Method: 通过时间序列分解构建可解释概念（如幅度、频带等），并利用遗传算法优化概念层面的突变，生成反事实样本。

Result: 在五个最先进方法的对比评估中，ConceptCF 在有效性、置信度、邻近性、稀疏性和合理性指标上持续达到顶级性能。

Conclusion: ConceptCF 通过操作可解释概念生成反事实解释，比现有基于点或子序列的方法更具可解释性，且性能优越。

Original Abstract: This paper proposes ConceptCF, a method for counterfactual generation that operates on human-interpretable concepts. In high-stakes domains such as healthcare and predictive maintenance, artificial intelligence models can increase efficiency and safety. Explainability is key to ensure these models rely on causal relationships rather than spurious correlations. Counterfactual explanations identify minimal modifications that would change a model's predictions. Existing methods for time series operate on individual points or subsequences without ensuring interpretability of the mutations. ConceptCF instead modifies meaningful concepts. As a result we can provide explanations in terms of these concepts, for example ``the model's prediction would be `Sit' instead of `Walk' if you increase the scale of the movement''. In this paper, the concepts are constructed through time series decomposition, resulting in concepts such as scale, and frequency bands. Counterfactuals are generated using a genetic algorithm that optimizes the concept mutations. Evaluation against five state-of-the-art approaches demonstrates that ConceptCF consistently achieves top-tier performance across validity, confidence, proximity, sparsity and plausibility metrics.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出ConceptCF，一种在人类可解释概念上进行反事实生成的方法。在医疗和预测性维护等高风险领域，人工智能模型可以提高效率和安全性。可解释性是确保这些模型依赖因果关系而非虚假相关性的关键。反事实解释识别能够改变模型预测的最小修改。现有的时间序列方法在单个点或子序列上操作，无法保证变异的可解释性。ConceptCF则修改有意义的语义概念。因此，我们可以用这些概念提供解释，例如"如果增加运动的幅度，模型的预测将从'走路'变为'坐着'"。在本文中，概念通过时间序列分解构建，得到诸如幅度和频带等概念。反事实样本通过遗传算法优化概念突变生成。与五种最先进方法的评估对比表明，ConceptCF在有效性、置信度、邻近性、稀疏性和合理性指标上始终达到顶级性能。

</details>


### [165] [Decafs: Disentangled Conditional adversarial Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18755)
*Anirudh jain,Sakshi Varshney,Samuel Kaski,Vikas Garg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于李群的條件生成模型，通过对抗损失对齐解耦的潜空间与流空间，实现可解释的条件生成，无需扩展流空间维度，在图像和分子生成任务上表现优异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于流的生成模型虽然性能优越，但其潜空间中的生成因子高度纠缠，导致难以进行可解释的条件生成和受控生成。

Method: 利用李群构造解耦的替代潜空间作为条件生成器，通过对抗损失将其与潜流空间紧密对齐，从而在无需扩展流空间维度的前提下实现可解释的条件生成。

Result: 在条件图像生成任务中（MNIST、dSprites）超越StyleGAN；在分子生成任务（QM9、ZINC、MOSES）上也表现出强劲性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的模型能够在不增加流空间维度的情况下实现可解释的条件生成，并在图像和分子生成任务中取得了领先性能。

Original Abstract: Flow-based models have established state-of-the-art performance in generative modeling across domains, but are hard to interpret due to their complex latent embeddings. In particular, the entanglement of generative factors in the latent space hinders controlled generation. We circumvent this issue by appealing to a novel conditional generator based on Lie groups that disentangles an alternative latent space, which is aligned closely with the latent flow space using an adversarial loss. Our approach facilitates interpretable conditional generation while obviating the need to expand the dimensionality of the flow space (owing to its invertibility requirements). The proposed model demonstrates strong performance across conditional image (including, outperforming StyleGAN on MNIST, dSprites) and molecule (using standard QM9, ZINC and MOSES) generation tasks

中文摘要翻译: 基于流的模型在跨领域的生成建模中已取得最先进的性能，但由于其复杂的潜嵌入而难以解释。特别是，潜空间中生成因子的纠缠阻碍了受控生成。我们通过引入一种新颖的基于李群的条件生成器来规避这一问题，该生成器解耦了一个替代潜空间，并通过对抗损失使其与潜流空间紧密对齐。我们的方法在无需扩展流空间维度（由于其可逆性要求）的前提下，实现了可解释的条件生成。所提出的模型在条件图像生成（包括在MNIST、dSprites上超越StyleGAN）和分子生成（使用标准QM9、ZINC和MOSES数据集）任务中均表现出强劲性能。

</details>


### [166] [Relative Positions Generalize, Absolute Positions Memorize: An Implicit-Bias Account of Length Generalization in Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18759)
*Subham Singh,Ashutosh Mishra,Subha Raut*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化隐偏好的角度解释了Transformer中相对位置编码（如RoPE）比绝对位置编码在序列长度外推上表现更好的原因：相对编码使注意力logit仅依赖于相对偏移，具有精确等变性，从而训练时学到的选择规则能直接复制到更长序列；而绝对编码在训练范围外无约束，导致注意力头固定在训练范围内的绝对位置上。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有解释主要从表达性角度出发（是否存在长度泛化解），缺乏从优化过程视角的理解。作者希望从梯度下降的隐偏好角度解释相对位置编码与绝对位置编码在序列长度外推能力上的显著差异。

Method: 在最小固定偏移检索任务上理论分析注意力头的隐偏好；证明旋转位置编码（RoPE）使注意力logit仅依赖于相对偏移，具有精确等变性；而绝对位置编码在训练范围外无约束，学习到的规则固定在训练范围内的绝对位置上。进一步刻画了RoPE学到的低秩"载体"核与目标偏移对齐，并推导出注意力稀释定律。

Result: RoPE学到的选择规则在训练长度外精确复制，而绝对编码则固定在训练范围内的绝对位置；RoPE学到的规则可表征为与目标偏移对齐的低秩"载体"核，其精度衰减服从注意力稀释定律；线性注意力控制实验表明该机制是softmax特有的；上述现象、等变性和载体机制可迁移到多层多头Transformer的全序列长度泛化任务。

Conclusion: 相对位置编码的外推优势源于优化过程中的隐偏好，而非仅仅表达性优势；该发现连接了注意力的隐偏好、循环模型中外推的隐偏好以及RASP-L猜想的机器学习层面。

Original Abstract: Transformers with relative positional encodings often extrapolate to sequences longer than those seen during training, whereas transformers with learned absolute encodings typically do not. This is a robust empirical regularity, and the explanations offered for it so far are chiefly about expressivity, that is, about whether a length-generalizing solution exists. We give an optimization explanation. On a minimal fixed-offset retrieval task that isolates positional selection, the gap is governed by the implicit bias of the trained attention head: among the many solutions that fit short sequences, which one gradient descent actually selects. We prove that rotary encodings make the attention logit a function of relative offset alone, an exact equivariance, so whatever selection rule is learned at training lengths is reproduced verbatim at every longer length. Learned absolute encodings instead leave out-of-range positions unconstrained, and the trained head pins to a fixed absolute position inside the training range. We characterize the learned rotary rule as a low-rank ``carrier'' kernel aligned with the target offset, and we derive the resulting graceful accuracy decay as an attention-dilution law; both predictions are confirmed across seeds and offsets. A linear-attention control shows the mechanism is specific to softmax: without normalization, training selects a min-norm interpolant that does not extrapolate. The phenomenon, the equivariance, and the carrier all transfer to a multi-layer, multi-head transformer trained on a full-sequence length-generalization task. The account connects the implicit bias of attention, implicit bias for extrapolation in recurrent models, and the learning side of the RASP-L conjecture.

中文摘要翻译: 具有相对位置编码的Transformer通常能够外推到比训练时更长的序列，而具有学习型绝对位置编码的Transformer通常不能。这是一个稳健的经验规律，目前的解释主要从表达性角度出发，即是否存在长度泛化的解。本文给出了一种优化层面的解释。在一个将位置选择孤立出来的最小固定偏移检索任务上，这种差距由训练后注意力头的隐偏好决定：在众多拟合短序列的解中，梯度下降实际选择了哪一个。我们证明旋转位置编码使注意力logit仅依赖于相对偏移，这是一种精确的等变性，因此在训练长度上学到的选择规则可以原封不动地复制到任何更长的长度上。学习型绝对位置编码则使超出范围的位置不受约束，训练后的注意力头会固定在训练范围内的某个绝对位置上。我们将学到的旋转规则刻画为一个与目标偏移对齐的低秩"载体"核，并推导出由此产生的精度优雅衰减规律——注意力稀释定律；这两个预测在不同随机种子和偏移量下均得到验证。线性注意力对照组实验表明该机制是softmax特有的：没有归一化时，训练会选择最小范数插值解，该解无法外推。这一现象、等变性和载体机制均可迁移到在完整序列长度泛化任务上训练的多层多头Transformer上。本文的解释将注意力的隐偏好、循环模型中外推的隐偏好以及RASP-L猜想的机器学习层面联系了起来。

</details>


### [167] [Formulation-Level Auto-Tuning for QUBO-Based Machine Learning: A Case Study Across Multiple Quantum-Inspired Annealers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18774)
*Naoya Mizuki,Takahiro Katagiri,Daichi Mukunoki,Tetsuya Hoshino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Optuna的公式级自动调优框架，用于在多种量子启发式退火器上实现支持向量机（SVM）。该框架联合优化表示参数（编码基数B和比特深度K）、RBF核参数γ和等式约束惩罚ξ，通过内外两层优化（内层退火器最小化QUBO，外层Optuna循环最大化验证精度）来提升分类性能。实验表明，该方法在0-20%标签噪声的线性和非线性分类任务上，平均比网格搜索分别高出约0.8和2.1个百分点。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在退火型SVM中，连续对偶变量被离散化并转换为QUBO模型，这一过程引入了三个耦合的参数类（表示参数、核参数、惩罚参数），这些参数的联合选择对模型性能至关重要，但传统网格搜索效率低下且无法充分处理参数间的耦合关系。

Method: 将参数联合选择建模为混合离散-连续黑箱优化问题，构建双层优化框架：内层退火器最小化生成的QUBO，外层Optuna循环在每次试验中重构公式并最大化验证精度。在Fixstars Amplify Annealing Engine、Toshiba SQBM+和Fujitsu Digital Annealer三种退火器上，分别使用TPE和高斯过程采样器进行实验，并与网格搜索对比。

Result: 在0-20%标签噪声的线性和非线性分类任务上，该方法相比网格搜索平均提升约0.8个百分点（线性任务）和2.1个百分点（非线性任务）。

Conclusion: 公式质量与后端求解能力必须联合评估，任务级反馈能够补偿离散化、惩罚不平衡及后端依赖的近似优化带来的负面影响。

Original Abstract: This paper presents an Optuna-based formulation-level auto-tuning framework for support vector machines (SVMs) implemented on multiple quantum-inspired annealers. In an annealing-based SVM, continuous dual variables are discretized and converted into a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) model. This transformation introduces three coupled classes of parameters: representation parameters-the encoding base B and bit depth K-which determine numerical range, resolution, and QUBO size; the RBF kernel parameter γ, which determines classifier geometry; and the equality-constraint penalty ξ, which controls feasibility and coefficient balance. We formulate their joint selection as a mixed discrete-continuous black-box optimization problem. The framework has two optimization levels: an inner annealer minimizes the generated QUBO, while an outer Optuna loop reconstructs the formulation in every trial and maximizes validation accuracy. The same solver-agnostic procedure is applied to Fixstars Amplify Annealing Engine, Toshiba SQBM+, and Fujitsu Digital Annealer using TPE and Gaussian-process samplers and is compared with conventional grid search. Experiments on linear and nonlinear classification tasks with 0-20% label noise show mean gains over grid search of approximately 0.8 and 2.1 percentage points, respectively. The results demonstrate that formulation quality and backend capability must be evaluated jointly and that task-level feedback can compensate for discretization, penalty imbalance, and backend-dependent approximate optimization.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种基于Optuna的公式级自动调优框架，用于在多种量子启发式退火器上实现支持向量机（SVM）。在退火型SVM中，连续对偶变量被离散化并转换为二次无约束二进制优化（QUBO）模型。这一转换引入了三类耦合参数：表示参数——编码基数B和比特深度K，它们决定数值范围、分辨率和QUBO规模；RBF核参数γ，决定分类器几何形状；以及等式约束惩罚ξ，控制可行性和系数平衡。我们将它们的联合选择建模为一个混合离散-连续黑箱优化问题。该框架包含两个优化层级：内层退火器最小化生成的QUBO，外层Optuna循环在每次试验中重构公式并最大化验证精度。相同的解算器无关流程被应用于Fixstars Amplify Annealing Engine、Toshiba SQBM+和Fujitsu Digital Annealer，使用TPE和高斯过程采样器，并与传统网格搜索进行比较。在0-20%标签噪声的线性和非线性分类任务上的实验表明，该方法相比网格搜索平均提升约0.8个百分点和2.1个百分点。结果表明，公式质量与后端求解能力必须联合评估，并且任务级反馈能够补偿离散化、惩罚不平衡以及后端依赖的近似优化所带来的影响。

</details>


### [168] [PertReason: A Knowledge-Grounded Benchmark and Framework for Cell-State-Conditioned Mechanistic Reasoning of Perturbation Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18777)
*Dongkwan Kim,Yiming Gao,Yining Yang,Yang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了PertReason基准和框架套件，用于评估机器学习模型在细胞状态条件下的扰动效应推理能力。该工作揭示了现有模型在预测准确性与机制推理之间存在系统性差距，并提出了PertReasonLM模型以缓解这一问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在科学领域的机器学习评估中，仅关注预测准确性是不够的，还需要检验模型是否在分布偏移下（如新细胞类型、未见过的扰动）具备正确的机制推理能力。现有基准无法识别模型通过错误逻辑得出正确答案等隐性失败模式。

Method: 构建了PertReasonQA基准，结合单细胞遗传和化学扰动数据与知识图谱，动态条件化细胞特异性基态通路以避免机械记忆；提出了PertReasonLM大语言模型，通过将推理依据锚定于上下文特异性通路并强化结果与机制之间的一致性来训练模型。

Result: 对现有最先进模型的评估显示，预测准确性与机制推理之间存在系统性差距；模型存在通过错误逻辑得出正确答案、忽略细胞上下文、生成方向不一致机制等失败模式，这些在标准基准中几乎不可见。

Conclusion: 提供了一个用于揭示和缓解数据密集型科学系统中可信推理失败的诊断框架。

Original Abstract: Evaluating machine learning in scientific domains requires separating correct predictions from correct reasons under realistic distribution shifts. We introduce PertReason, a knowledge-grounded benchmark and framework suite for cell-state--conditioned reasoning about perturbation effects. At its core, PertReasonQA is a benchmark that tests whether models can generate mechanistically faithful explanations while remaining robust to complex shifts, such as new cells and unseen perturbations. PertReasonQA combines single-cell genetic and chemical perturbation data across multiple cellular contexts with knowledge graphs, and dynamically conditions pathways on cell-specific basal states to avoid generic memorization. Evaluations on state-of-the-art models reveal systematic gaps between predictive accuracy and mechanistic reasoning. Specifically, these models exhibit failure modes largely invisible to standard benchmarks, such as deriving correct answers through flawed logic, ignoring cellular context, and generating directionally inconsistent mechanisms. As a reference probe of the benchmark, we present PertReasonLM, a large language model trained to align outcome predictions with context-specific mechanistic reasoning. Our model targets the identified failure modes by grounding rationales in context-specific pathways and tightening agreement between outcomes and mechanisms. Together, we provide a diagnostic framework for exposing and mitigating failures in faithful reasoning in data-rich scientific systems.

中文摘要翻译: 在科学领域中评估机器学习，需要在实际分布偏移下将正确的预测与正确的推理区分开来。我们提出了PertReason，一个基于知识的基准测试与框架套件，用于细胞状态条件下的扰动效应推理。其核心是PertReasonQA基准，测试模型能否在复杂偏移（如新细胞类型和未见过的扰动）下生成机制上可信的解释。PertReasonQA将跨多种细胞环境的单细胞遗传与化学扰动数据与知识图谱相结合，并动态地根据细胞特异性基态对通路进行条件化处理，以避免机械记忆。对最先进模型的评估揭示了预测准确性与机制推理之间存在系统性差距。具体而言，这些模型展现出在标准基准中几乎不可见的失败模式，例如通过有缺陷的逻辑推导出正确答案、忽略细胞上下文以及生成方向不一致的机制。作为该基准的一个参考探针，我们提出了PertReasonLM，这是一个经过训练的大语言模型，用于将结果预测与上下文特异性的机制推理对齐。该模型通过将推理依据锚定于上下文特异性通路并加强结果与机制之间的一致性，来针对已识别的失败模式。总之，我们提供了一个诊断框架，用于在数据密集型科学系统中揭示和缓解可信推理中的失败。

</details>


### [169] [QScheduler: Adaptive Gradient Sampling for Zeroth-Order On-Device Training on INT8 NPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18802)
*Victor Felipe Domingues Do Amaral,Pierre Demaj,Erwan Libessart,Laurent Folliot,Anthony Kolar,Philippe Bénabès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QScheduler，一种自适应调整梯度采样数q的算法，用于优化NPU微控制器上的设备端学习，在不增加超参数搜索成本的前提下匹配最优固定配置的性能，并在STM32N6上首次实现INT8量化设备端训练。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 零阶优化中梯度采样数q的选择至关重要：q太小导致梯度噪声大、收敛早停；q太大则浪费计算资源。但最优q通常需要昂贵的超参数搜索，亟需一种自适应调整q的方法。

Method: 提出QScheduler算法，根据训练进度动态调整梯度采样数q，无需提前搜索最优超参数。并在STM32N6的Neural-ART NPU上首次实现INT8量化的设备端训练概念验证。

Result: 在EuroSAT和STL-10数据集上，使用ResNet18和MobileNetV2，QScheduler能够匹配经过精心调优的固定q配置的性能，且无需预先进行q超参数优化。

Conclusion: QScheduler通过自适应调整梯度采样数，在保持性能的同时消除了超参数搜索开销，并且首次证明了在STM32N6 NPU上进行INT8量化设备端训练的可行性。

Original Abstract: Zeroth-Order (ZO) optimization enables On-Device Learning (ODL) on NPU-equipped microcontrollers by estimating gradients through forward passes alone, bypassing the need for backpropagation primitives and reducing memory requirements. The number of gradient samples q critically affects training: insufficient samples produce noisy gradients that plateau early, while excessive samples consume more computational resources. However, finding an optimal q typically requires costly hyperparameter searches. This work introduces QScheduler, an adaptive algorithm that adjusts q based on training progress, and provides the first proof-of-concept of INT8 quantized on-device training on the STM32N6's Neural-ART NPU. Experiments on EuroSAT and STL-10 show that QScheduler matches well-tuned fixed-q configurations for both ResNet18 and MobileNetV2, without requiring prior q hyperparameter optimization.

中文摘要翻译: 零阶优化通过仅依靠前向传播来估计梯度，绕过了对反向传播原语的需求并减少了内存占用，从而能在配备NPU的微控制器上实现设备端学习。梯度采样数q对训练效果有重要影响：采样不足会产生噪声梯度导致提前收敛停滞，而采样过多则会消耗更多计算资源。然而，寻找最优q通常需要进行昂贵的超参数搜索。本文提出QScheduler，一种根据训练进度自适应调整q的算法，并在STM32N6的Neural-ART NPU上首次实现了INT8量化设备端训练的概念验证。在EuroSAT和STL-10数据集上的实验表明，对于ResNet18和MobileNetV2，QScheduler无需预先进行q超参数优化即可匹敌经过良好调优的固定q配置。

</details>


### [170] [Elicitation without Backpropagation: Steering Model Behavior by Optimizing the Latent Posterior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18804)
*Garrett Baker,Vinayak Pathak,Daniel Murfet,Susan Wei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出后验前缀微调（PPT）方法，通过优化提示分布来引导贝叶斯滤波Transformer（BFT）生成高期望效用的续文，无需反向传播且支持多效用零边际成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 利用Transformer的潜在后验模型（在BFT中精确成立），实现无需梯度传播的、高效的提示优化，以引导模型生成符合特定效用函数的输出。

Method: PPT在BFT上优化硬提示分布参数：通过预测性蒙特卡洛（PMC）从先验中采样，利用重要性采样估计目标函数的梯度，无需前向传播或反向传播；单次采样可服务于多个不同效用函数。

Result: 在Beta-Bernoulli和强化瓮BFT上，针对反向交叉熵、频率匹配、Dyck有效性三类效用函数验证了PPT的有效性。

Conclusion: PPT提供了一种高效、可扩展的提示优化方法，避免了传统基于梯度的反向传播开销，并且单次采样可重复用于多种效用目标。

Original Abstract: In the \emph{latent posterior model} of transformer behavior, the next-token distribution arises from a posterior over latent predictive models conditioned on the context, mixed to generate continuations. We exploit this model in settings where it is exact, namely Bayes-filtered transformers (BFTs) meta-learned on sequences from a hierarchical prior, to introduce \textbf{Posterior Prefix Tuning (PPT)}, a new method for \emph{eliciting} behavior from a transformer: given a utility function on continuations, find a prompt under which the transformer generates continuations of high expected utility. For a BFT, the elicitation objective factors through the latent posterior, and the gradient of this objective can be estimated from samples of the prior alone. PPT optimizes the parameters of a distribution over hard prompts: it draws prior samples once from the BFT via predictive Monte Carlo (PMC), then estimates the gradient by importance sampling against them. The optimization performs no transformer forward passes and no backpropagation through the transformer, and the prior samples are utility-independent, so a single set of samples drives elicitation against any number of utilities at negligible marginal cost. We validate PPT on Beta--Bernoulli and reinforced urn BFTs across three utility families (reverse cross-entropy, frequency matching, Dyck validity).

中文摘要翻译: 在Transformer行为的潜在后验模型中，下一词元的分布源于对基于上下文条件化的潜在预测模型的后验，并通过混合生成续文。我们利用该模型在精确成立的情形——即从层次先验的序列上元学习的贝叶斯滤波Transformer（BFT）——引入后验前缀微调（PPT），这是一种从Transformer中引导行为的新方法：给定续文上的效用函数，寻找一个提示，使得Transformer生成的续文具有高期望效用。对于BFT，引导目标通过潜在后验分解，该目标的梯度可仅从先验样本中估计。PPT优化硬提示分布的参数：它通过预测性蒙特卡洛（PMC）从BFT中一次性采样先验，然后利用重要性采样估计梯度。该优化无需执行Transformer前向传播和反向传播，且先验样本与效用无关，因此单次采样即可驱动对任意数量效用的引导，边际成本可忽略不计。我们在Beta-Bernoulli和强化瓮BFT上，针对三类效用族（反向交叉熵、频率匹配、Dyck有效性）验证了PPT的有效性。

</details>


### [171] [Countercurrent Multiplier Networks: A Renal-Inspired Iterative Operator with Provably Bounded Fixed-Point Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18829)
*Snigdha Chandan Khilar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 受哺乳动物肾脏逆流倍增机制启发，提出一种可微分的逆流倍增器（CCM）序列算子，作为残差迭代精炼的替代方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前神经架构中缺乏与肾脏逆流倍增类似的高效梯度放大机制，因此希望将这一生物机制形式化为可微分的神经网络算子，为深度学习提供一种新的迭代精炼替代方案。

Method: 将哺乳动物肾脏的逆流倍增机制形式化为可微分的序列算子——逆流倍增器（CCM）层，利用两股反向流动在发夹弯处汇合，通过弱局部泵的循环放大实现大范围轴向梯度。

Result: 该机制通过一个在任何点都不超过200 mOsm的单效应梯度，实现了四倍的浓度提升，证明了弱局部泵可被循环放大为显著的轴向梯度。

Conclusion: CCM层可作为残差迭代精炼的一种有效替代方案，为神经网络架构设计提供了新的生物启发式方向。

Original Abstract: The mammalian kidney concentrates urine using a mechanism with no analogue in current neural architectures: the countercurrent multiplier. Two anti-parallel flows joined at a hairpin recirculate a weak magnitude-bounded local pump into a large axial gradient achieving a four-fold concentration increase from a single-effect gradient that never exceeds 200 mOsm at any point. We formalize this mechanism as a differentiable sequence operator the Countercurrent Multiplier (CCM) layer and study it as an alternative to residual iterative refinement.

中文摘要翻译: 哺乳动物肾脏利用一种在当前神经架构中没有类似物的机制来浓缩尿液：逆流倍增器。两股反向流动在发夹弯处汇合，将幅值受限的弱局部泵循环放大为巨大的轴向梯度，通过一个在任何点都不超过200 mOsm的单效应梯度实现了四倍的浓度提升。我们将这一机制形式化为一个可微分的序列算子——逆流倍增器（CCM）层，并将其作为残差迭代精炼的一种替代方案进行研究。

</details>


### [172] [From Trajectories to Instructions: Language-Conditioned Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18830)
*Garvit Singla,Uma Maheswari Natarajan,Raghuram Bharadwaj Diddigi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LA-MAML提出用语言指令嵌入替代MAML内循环中的轨迹采集和梯度更新，在降低计算成本的同时实现竞争性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MAML的内循环需要采集轨迹并执行梯度更新，计算成本高昂；而外循环才是驱动全局参数学习的关键，因此内循环不必局限于梯度方法。许多任务天然附带有语言指令，可利用其作为任务特定信号来简化内循环。

Method: 提出LA-MAML，在元强化学习的内循环中，将任务的语言指令通过嵌入学习转化为任务特定信号，用单步参数调整替代传统的内循环轨迹采集与梯度更新。

Result: 在BabyAI基准上，LA-MAML在达到与基线竞争或更优性能的同时，显著降低了每次迭代的实际训练时间（wall-clock time）。

Conclusion: 语言指令可以作为基于轨迹的内循环适应的有效且高效的替代方案，用于元强化学习中。

Original Abstract: Model-Agnostic Meta-Learning (MAML) is a widely used framework for reinforcement learning (RL) that enables efficient transfer by learning global policy parameters that can be rapidly adapted to new tasks. MAML training proceeds in two loops: an inner loop where the global parameters are adapted to task-specific parameters, and an outer loop where these task-specific parameters are evaluated and losses are back-propagated to improve the global parameters. Traditionally, the inner loop adaptation is performed by collecting trajectories from the task environment and applying gradient updates on the empirical expected return, which can be a costly operation. We note that it is the outer loop that drives the actual learning of global parameters, and therefore the inner loop adaptation mechanism need not be restricted to be gradient-based. This observation leads us to ask: Can we replace the inner loop trajectory collection and gradient update with a simpler, task-specific signal? In many practical settings, tasks are naturally accompanied by language instructions. Leveraging these instructions as a direct task-specific signal, we propose LA-MAML (Language Adapted MAML), which modifies the inner loop by adapting the global policy parameters in a single step through a learned embedding of the task instruction, replacing the inner loop trajectory collection and gradient-based updates. Experiments on the BabyAI benchmark demonstrate that LA-MAML achieves competitive or improved performance compared to baselines at a significantly lower per-iteration wall-clock training time. These results demonstrate that language instructions are an effective and efficient substitute for trajectory-based inner loop adaptation in meta RL.

中文摘要翻译: 模型无关元学习（MAML）是一种广泛用于强化学习（RL）的框架，通过学习全局策略参数实现高效迁移，这些参数可以快速适应新任务。MAML训练分为两个循环：内循环将全局参数适应为任务特定参数，外循环评估这些任务特定参数并通过反向传播损失来改进全局参数。传统上，内循环适应通过从任务环境中采集轨迹并对经验期望回报应用梯度更新来完成，这一操作成本高昂。我们注意到，真正驱动全局参数学习的是外循环，因此内循环适应机制不必局限于基于梯度的方法。这一观察引导我们提出疑问：能否将内循环的轨迹采集和梯度更新替换为更简单的任务特定信号？在许多实际场景中，任务天然伴随着语言指令。利用这些指令作为直接的任务特定信号，我们提出了LA-MAML（语言适应的MAML），该方法通过任务指令的习得嵌入，以单步方式调整全局策略参数，从而替代内循环的轨迹采集和基于梯度的更新。在BabyAI基准上的实验表明，与基线相比，LA-MAML在每次迭代的端到端训练时间显著降低的同时，达到了具有竞争力或更优的性能。这些结果证明，语言指令是元强化学习中基于轨迹的内循环适应的有效且高效的替代方案。

</details>


### [173] [ABOPD: Antibody CDR Design via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18835)
*Zhuo Yang,Jiaying He,Jiaqing Xie,Daolang Wang,Xipeng Qiu,Yuxin Wang,Tianfan Fu,Beilun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ABOPD是一种基于在线策略蒸馏的抗体设计框架，通过在训练中利用天然结构监督模型自身的去噪轨迹，显著提升了抗体CDR-H3环的结构恢复精度（RMSD从2.37Å降至1.95Å），优于监督微调和离线蒸馏方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有蛋白质生成模型在抗体CDR环（特别是CDR-H3）设计中，标准去噪训练使用扰动后的天然结构，而递归生成则经过模型生成的中间状态，这种不匹配导致骨架偏差沿去噪轨迹累积，损害抗原结合环的几何结构。

Method: 提出ABOPD框架，采用基于在线策略蒸馏（on-policy distillation）的方法，在训练过程中利用特权天然几何结构（privileged native geometry）来监督模型自身去噪轨迹上访问的中间状态，从而提供细粒度的结构监督。

Result: 在RAbD基准的CDR-H3生成任务上，ABOPD将RMSD从2.37Å降至1.95Å（降低0.42Å），在结构恢复精度上显著优于监督微调和离线蒸馏控制方法。

Conclusion: ABOPD通过在线策略蒸馏提供细粒度结构监督，有效解决了去噪训练与递归生成之间的状态不匹配问题，为高保真蛋白质设计提供了可行路径。

Original Abstract: Antibodies are essential therapeutic molecules, and their complementarity-determining regions (CDRs) form the primary antigen-recognition interface. Recent protein generative models have demonstrated broad capabilities in biomolecular design, yet post-training strategies for downstream objectives remain limited. Standard denoising training operates on noisy states obtained by perturbing native structures, whereas recursive generation proceeds through model-generated intermediate states. For flexible antibody CDR loops such as CDR-H3, this mismatch can allow backbone deviations to accumulate along the denoising trajectory and compromise antigen-facing loop geometry. We introduce ABOPD, an antibody design framework based on on-policy distillation that leverages privileged native geometry during training to supervise states visited along the model's own denoising trajectories. With this fine-grained structural supervision, ABOPD substantially improves structural recovery on RAbD CDR-H3 generation, reducing RMSD by 0.42 Å (from 2.37 Å to 1.95 Å) and outperforming supervised fine-tuning and offline distillation controls, offering a path to higher-fidelity protein design.

中文摘要翻译: 抗体是重要的治疗性分子，其互补决定区（CDRs）构成了主要的抗原识别界面。最近的蛋白质生成模型在生物分子设计方面展现出广泛能力，但针对下游目标的后训练策略仍然有限。标准去噪训练在扰动天然结构获得的噪声状态上进行操作，而递归生成则经过模型生成的中间状态。对于柔性抗体CDR环（如CDR-H3），这种不匹配可能导致骨架偏差沿去噪轨迹累积，从而损害抗原结合环的几何结构。我们提出了ABOPD，一种基于在线策略蒸馏的抗体设计框架，该框架在训练过程中利用特权天然几何结构来监督模型自身去噪轨迹上访问的状态。通过这种细粒度的结构监督，ABOPD在RAbD CDR-H3生成任务上显著提升了结构恢复精度，将RMSD从2.37Å降低至1.95Å（降低0.42Å），优于监督微调和离线蒸馏控制方法，为更高保真度的蛋白质设计提供了可行路径。

</details>


### [174] [Regime-Aware Physics-Guided Early Warning of Lithium-Ion Battery Thermal Runaway Using Thermo-Mechanical Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18860)
*Syed Sajid Ullah,Muhammad Zunair Zamir,Salman Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种融合温度、电压、力、变形和荷电状态的物理引导框架，通过轻量卷积分类器识别安全/警告/危险状态，并利用因果时序卷积网络实现锂离子电池热失控的早期预警，在30组机械滥用测试中F1达0.89，预警提前时间优于最强基线69.6%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有热失控早期预警方法主要依赖温度信号，可能漏掉快速升温前已经出现的力学前兆，导致预警不及时或不可靠。

Method: 提出一个状态感知的物理引导框架，集成温度、电压、力、变形和荷电状态等多模态测量。首先通过轻量卷积分类器从力学信号推断安全/警告/危险状态，然后利用这些状态估计通过特征级线性调制、物理偏置注意力及状态相关门控来条件化因果时序卷积骨干网络。联合学习统一了状态识别、热失控检测和灾难时间估计三个任务。

Result: 在30组机械滥用测试（跨越10%、50%、90%三种荷电状态和两种加载协议）上，采用留一实验交叉验证。方法实现F1分数0.89，高温预测RMSE为12.3°C，平均预警提前时间15.6秒，检测成功率0.92，实验级误报率2.7%。预警提前时间超过最强基线69.6%。去除力信号后预警提前时间降低60.3%。

Conclusion: 状态感知的热-力学融合方法是在受控滥用条件下实现更早、更可靠热失控预警的有效策略，力学信号（尤其是力测量）对提升预警提前时间具有关键价值。

Original Abstract: Thermal runaway in lithium-ion batteries poses a major safety risk to electric vehicles and energy storage systems. Current early-warning methods depend mainly on temperature and may therefore miss mechanical precursors that emerge before rapid heating. We introduce a regime-aware, physics-guided framework that integrates temperature, voltage, force, deformation, and state-of-charge measurements for early warning under controlled mechanical abuse. A lightweight convolutional classifier first infers safe, warning, or danger regimes from mechanical signals. These regime estimates then condition a causal temporal convolutional backbone through feature-wise linear modulation, physics-biased attention, and regime-dependent gating. Joint learning unifies regime identification, thermal-runaway detection, and time-to-disaster estimation. We evaluate the framework using leave-one-experiment-out cross-validation on 30 mechanical-abuse tests across state-of-charge levels of 10%, 50%, and 90% and two loading protocols. The method achieves an F1 score of 0.89, a high-temperature prediction root-mean-square error of 12.3 °C, a mean warning lead time of 15.6 s, a detection success rate of 0.92, and an experiment-level false alarm rate of 2.7%. Its lead time exceeds that of the strongest baseline by 69.6%. Removing force reduces the lead time by 60.3%, highlighting the value of mechanical precursors. These results support regime-aware thermo-mechanical fusion as a promising strategy for earlier and more reliable thermal-runaway warning under controlled abuse conditions.

中文摘要翻译: 锂离子电池的热失控对电动汽车和储能系统构成重大安全风险。当前的早期预警方法主要依赖温度信号，因此可能遗漏在快速升温之前就已出现的力学前兆。我们提出了一种状态感知的物理引导框架，该框架集成温度、电压、力、变形和荷电状态等测量信息，用于受控机械滥用条件下的早期预警。一个轻量卷积分类器首先从力学信号中推断安全、警告或危险状态。这些状态估计随后通过特征级线性调制、物理偏置注意力和状态相关门控来条件化一个因果时序卷积骨干网络。联合学习统一了状态识别、热失控检测和灾难时间估计三个任务。我们使用留一实验交叉验证方法，在跨越10%、50%和90%三种荷电状态及两种加载协议的30组机械滥用测试上评估该框架。该方法实现了0.89的F1分数、12.3°C的高温预测均方根误差、15.6秒的平均预警提前时间、0.92的检测成功率和2.7%的实验级误报率。其预警提前时间超过最强基线69.6%。去除力信号后预警提前时间降低60.3%，凸显了力学前兆的价值。这些结果支持将状态感知的热-力学融合作为在受控滥用条件下实现更早、更可靠热失控预警的一种有前景的策略。

</details>


### [175] [HindsightBench: A Black-Box Behavioral Audit Protocol for Parametric Hindsight in Time-Indexed LLM Decision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18867)
*Haozhe Jia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HindsightBench，一种黑盒行为审计协议，用于检测大型语言模型在历史金融决策任务中是否泄露了参数化的后见之明（即模型知道事后结果）。该协议通过四臂日期操作矩阵、双内存探针和六项指标，以探针级成本实现审计，无需回测、logprobs或语料库访问。在15个模型上应用发现：日期触发反射跟踪训练代际而非规模；有效知识截止日期比厂商报告早最多8个月；审计结果对服务方式敏感，因此协议附带操作要求。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大型语言模型存在参数化后见之明（将事后结果泄露到历史决策任务中）的问题，但用户缺乏一种低成本的方法来审计特定模型是否存在此问题。现有方法成本高昂（需要回测、logprobs或语料库访问），因此需要一种廉价的、黑盒的审计协议。

Method: HindsightBench协议包括：(1) 四臂日期操作矩阵（revealed/date-only/masked/transplanted四种日期处理方式）；(2) 双内存探针（日期恢复和结果回忆）；(3) 六项逐模型指标（触发强度、移植效应、截止日期后安慰剂、可恢复性、行为有效知识截止日期、回忆-准确性解耦系数）；(4) 数据依赖时的显式门控。在258个节点的老式校正宏观面板上对7个供应商的15个模型进行审计。

Result: 三个主要发现：(i) 日期触发反射跟踪训练代际而非规模——2024开源代际模型（1B-70B）中不存在，在2026代际模型中全部存在，且在Qwen3→Qwen3.6同一供应商系谱中在固定MoE架构和3B激活参数下切换开启；(ii) 有效截止日期跨供应商跨度22个月，比供应商报告日期早最多8个月，使日历窗口安慰剂设计无效；(iii) 审计结果对服务方式不具不变性——BF16服务破坏触发估计稳定性而AWQ-INT4保持稳定，供应商锁定推理模式导致一个探针不收敛。

Conclusion: HindsightBench能够以低成本有效审计LLM的参数化后见之明。协议附带操作要求（固定量化方式和思考模式，披露解析器和采样策略）。研究团队发布了面板数据、预注册、逐模型审计行及成本、转录和单命令复现脚本。

Original Abstract: Large language models leak parametric knowledge of realized outcomes into historical financial decision tasks. Existence is settled; what users lack is a cheap way to audit a given model for it. We present HindsightBench, a black-box behavioral audit protocol that profiles parametric hindsight in any time-indexed LLM decision task at probe-level cost (no backtests, no logprobs, no corpus access). The protocol chains a four-arm date-manipulation matrix (revealed/date-only/masked/transplanted), dual memory probes (date recovery; outcome recall), and six per-model metrics -- trigger strength, transplant effect, post-cutoff placebo, recoverability, behaviorally effective knowledge cutoff, and a recall-accuracy dissociation coefficient -- with explicit gates where identifiability is data-dependent. Applying it to 15 models from seven vendors on a 258-node vintage-correct macro panel yields three headline patterns: (i) the date-trigger reflex tracks training generation, not scale -- absent across the 2024 open-weight generation from 1B to 70B, present in every tested 2026-generation model, and switching on within one vendor lineage (Qwen3 -> Qwen3.6) at fixed MoE architecture and 3B active parameters; (ii) effective cutoffs span 22 months across vendors and precede vendor-reported dates by up to eight months, invalidating calendar-window placebo designs; (iii) audit results are not invariant to serving -- BF16 serving of an FP8-referenced model breaks the trigger estimate's stability while AWQ-INT4 preserves it, and a provider-locked reasoning regime makes one probe non-convergent -- so the protocol ships with operational requirements (pin quantization and thinking regime; disclose parser and sampling policy). We release the panel, frozen preregistrations, per-model audit rows with measured dollar costs, transcripts, and one-command regeneration.

中文摘要翻译: 大型语言模型会将已实现结果的参数化知识泄露到历史金融决策任务中。这一现象的存在性已基本得到确认，但用户缺乏一种低成本的方式来审计特定模型是否存在此问题。本文提出HindsightBench，一种黑盒行为审计协议，能够以探针级成本（无需回测、无需对数概率、无需语料库访问）在任何时间索引的LLM决策任务中刻画参数化后见之明。该协议串联了一个四臂日期操作矩阵（揭示/仅日期/遮蔽/移植）、双内存探针（日期恢复；结果回忆）以及六项逐模型指标——触发强度、移植效应、截止日期后安慰剂、可恢复性、行为有效知识截止日期和回忆-准确性解耦系数——并设置了数据依赖时可识别性问题的显式门控。将该协议应用于来自七家供应商的15个模型，在包含258个节点的老式校正宏观面板上进行测试，得出了三个主要发现：(i) 日期触发反射跟踪训练代际而非规模——在2024年开源代际（1B到70B）中完全不存在，在所有测试的2026代际模型中均存在，且在一个供应商系谱内（Qwen3→Qwen3.6）于固定MoE架构和3B激活参数下切换开启；(ii) 有效截止日期跨供应商跨度达22个月，比供应商报告的日期最多早8个月，使日历窗口安慰剂设计失效；(iii) 审计结果对服务方式不具有不变性——BF16服务破坏了FP8参考模型的触发估计稳定性，而AWQ-INT4保持了稳定性，且供应商锁定的推理模式导致一个探针不收敛——因此该协议附带操作要求（固定量化和思考模式；披露解析器和采样策略）。我们发布了该面板数据、冻结预注册、逐模型审计行及其实际美元成本、转录文本以及单命令复现脚本。

</details>


### [176] [Reinforcement Learning for Delivery Drone-Based Participatory Sensing in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18874)
*Xin Ouyang,Songxin Lei,Xusen Guo,Yutian Jiang,Sijie Ruan,Yuxuan Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向无人机城市感知任务的双时间尺度强化学习框架TSRL，通过宏观任务调度与微观风速感知速度控制的分层决策，在动态环境下实现了系统收益的显著提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于无人机的城市感知方法忽视了风等环境扰动对无人机速度和能效的严重影响，导致在动态环境中面临可扩展性瓶颈（机群规模扩大时性能下降）和多时间尺度决策异质性（宏观任务调度与微观速度控制之间的协调困难）两大挑战。

Method: 提出TSRL双时间尺度强化学习框架，将决策分为两个协作层：（1）宏观层面，任务嵌入感知调度器通过分别编码不同任务特征并顺序评估无人机适配性来实现可扩展的任务分配；（2）微观层面，风速感知速度控制器学习细粒度的速度调度以适应动态环境变化。

Result: 在真实数据集上的实验表明，TSRL显著优于基线方法，在杭州和上海分别实现了平均系统收益20.1%和46.6%的提升。

Conclusion: TSRL通过分层决策框架有效解决了无人机城市感知中可扩展性和多时间尺度协调两大挑战，为动态环境下的联合配送与感知任务提供了高效解决方案。

Original Abstract: Using Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) for urban sensing has emerged as a powerful paradigm to monitor the status of the city, e.g., air quality and noise levels, through agile aerial crowdsourcing. Despite this potential, existing UAV-based sensing approaches overlook environmental disturbances like wind that drastically impact drone velocity and energy efficiency. Consequently, directly applying existing methods to this joint delivery and sensing paradigm in dynamic environments faces two severe challenges: (1) scalability bottlenecks as fleet sizes expand; and (2) multi-timescale decision heterogeneity between macro task dispatching and micro velocity control. To tackle these, we formalize the problem as SensUAV and propose a Two TimeScale Reinforcement Learning framework (TSRL). Specifically, TSRL separates decision-making into two cooperative layers. At the macro level, a task-embedding sensing dispatcher handles scalability by separately encoding distinct task features and sequentially evaluating UAV suitability before task selection. At the micro level, a wind-aware velocity controller learns fine-grained velocity scheduling to adapt to dynamic environmental variations. Extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate that TSRL significantly outperforms baselines, achieving average system profit improvements of 20.1% in Hangzhou and 46.6% in Shanghai.

中文摘要翻译: 使用无人机进行城市感知已成为一种强大的范式，通过敏捷的空中众包来监测城市状态（如空气质量和噪音水平）。尽管具有这种潜力，现有基于无人机的感知方法忽视了风等环境扰动对无人机速度和能效的严重影响。因此，将现有方法直接应用于动态环境中的这种联合配送与感知范式面临两大严峻挑战：（1）机群规模扩大时的可扩展性瓶颈；（2）宏观任务调度与微观速度控制之间的多时间尺度决策异质性。为解决这些问题，我们将该问题形式化为SensUAV，并提出了一种双时间尺度强化学习框架（TSRL）。具体而言，TSRL将决策分离为两个协作层。在宏观层面，任务嵌入感知调度器通过分别编码不同任务特征并在任务选择前顺序评估无人机适配性来处理可扩展性问题。在微观层面，风速感知速度控制器学习细粒度的速度调度以适应动态环境变化。在真实数据集上的广泛实验表明，TSRL显著优于基线方法，在杭州和上海分别实现了平均系统收益20.1%和46.6%的提升。

</details>


### [177] [Physics-Informed Super-Resolution of Atmospheric Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18877)
*Chang Xu,Gencer Sumbul,Hugo Porta,Manon Béchaz,Sebastian Schemm,Devis Tuia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出物理信息超分辨率（PISR）方法，通过将静水原始方程约束融入大气数据超分辨率模型，提升重建数据的物理一致性和极端事件检测能力，并提出了归一化物理一致性（NPC）指标来评估超分辨率结果的物理合理性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 全球变暖背景下极端事件频发，大气观测空间分辨率不足，现有超分辨率方法虽高效但无法保证重建数据满足地球系统的基本物理规律，影响其在气候应用中的可信度。

Method: 提出物理信息超分辨率（PISR）方法，将基于原始方程的多尺度物理信息目标函数融入超分辨率模型训练，约束输出遵守静水原始方程，从而自然编码变量间的物理关系；同时提出归一化物理一致性（NPC）指标衡量超分辨率数据的物理一致性。

Result: 在ERA5、CERRA和COSMO数据集上的实验表明，PISR在物理一致性、超分辨率精度以及极端事件（热浪和极端风）下游检测方面均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 将静水原始方程物理约束引入超分辨率模型可有效提升大气重建数据的物理可信度，改善极端事件检测性能，为气候相关应用提供更可靠的超分辨率方案。

Original Abstract: In the context of global warming, extreme events have become more frequent and intense, making their trustworthy detection and forecasting more important than ever. Yet, atmospheric observations lack sufficient spatial resolution, motivating atmospheric data downscaling as a way to reconstruct high-resolution data from coarse observations. This task is now being formulated as a super-resolution (SR) problem with machine learning methods featuring high efficiency. Nevertheless, it remains unclear whether the super-resolved atmospheric data still satisfies fundamental physics governing the Earth system, raising concerns about their trustworthiness in climate-related applications. In this work, we address this challenge by constraining SR models to respect hydrostatic primitive equations that represent multivariate atmospheric physics. First, we propose a Physics-Informed Super-Resolution (PISR) method involving multi-scale physics-informed objectives based on primitive equations. PISR favors the SR outputs to respect these equations and therefore naturally encodes inter-variable relationships. In addition, we propose a metric called Normalized Physical Consistency (NPC) derived from said primitive equations to measure the physical consistency of super-resolved data. Experiments on ERA5, CERRA, and COSMO demonstrate that PISR enhances the reconstruction fidelity by improving physical consistency, SR accuracy, and downstream detection of extreme events, as demonstrated by case studies in heatwaves and extreme winds.

中文摘要翻译: 在全球变暖的背景下，极端事件变得愈发频繁和剧烈，这使得对它们的可靠检测与预测比以往任何时候都更加重要。然而，大气观测缺乏足够的空间分辨率，从而推动了大气数据降尺度作为一种从粗糙观测中重建高分辨率数据的方法。该任务现已被形式化为超分辨率（SR）问题，并采用了高效率的机器学习方法。然而，超分辨率后的大气数据是否仍然满足控制地球系统的基本物理规律尚不明确，这引发了人们对其在气候相关应用中可信度的担忧。在本工作中，我们通过约束超分辨率模型尊重代表多变量大气物理的静水原始方程来应对这一挑战。首先，我们提出了一种物理信息超分辨率（PISR）方法，该方法引入了基于原始方程的多尺度物理信息目标函数。PISR倾向于让超分辨率输出遵守这些方程，从而自然地编码变量间的关系。此外，我们提出了一种基于上述原始方程的归一化物理一致性（NPC）指标，用于衡量超分辨率数据的物理一致性。在ERA5、CERRA和COSMO上的实验表明，PISR通过提升物理一致性、超分辨率精度以及极端事件（如热浪和极端风案例研究所示）的下游检测能力，增强了重建保真度。

</details>


### [178] [RAMP: Recognition parametrisation by Amortised Message Passing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18883)
*Lior Fox,Kai Biegun,James Heald,Samo Hromadka,Arielle Rosinski,Maneesh Sahani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RAMP是一种基于识别参数化建模的非线性摊销消息传递框架，能够高效地从复杂高维数据中恢复潜变量分布。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有无监督学习方法在揭示观测数据间依赖关系的潜在因素时，要么依赖可处理信念传播的分布选择，要么依赖随模型规模和复杂度扩展性差的近似方法，亟需一种高效且可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 基于识别参数化建模范式，提出RAMP方法，通过学习一个灵活的非线性摊销消息传递框架来隐式定义潜在结构。

Result: RAMP能够在表达性非线性模型中以基于似然的方式高效恢复潜变量分布，适用于复杂高维数据。

Conclusion: RAMP为无监督学习中的潜在因素发现提供了一种高效、可扩展的新途径，克服了传统方法在分布选择和近似推理上的局限。

Original Abstract: A central aim of unsupervised learning is to uncover latent factors that explain dependencies among observations. Probabilistic models typically achieve this by introducing multiple latent variables linked through a graph of conditional relationships, with distributional parameters and their dependence learnt from data. Learning relies either on distributional choices that allow tractable belief propagation, or on approximations that scale poorly with model size and complexity. We build on the recently developed recognition-parametrised modelling paradigm to propose an alternative approach: RAMP, a method that implicitly defines latent structure by learning a flexible, nonlinear, amortised message-passing framework. We show that RAMP enables efficient likelihood-based recovery of latent-variable distributions within expressive nonlinear models acting on complex high-dimensional data.

中文摘要翻译: 无监督学习的一个核心目标是揭示解释观测之间依赖关系的潜在因素。概率模型通常通过引入多个通过条件关系图连接的潜在变量来实现这一点，其分布参数和依赖关系从数据中学习。学习过程要么依赖于允许可处理信念传播的分布选择，要么依赖于随模型规模和复杂度扩展性差的近似方法。我们基于最近开发的识别参数化建模范式，提出了一种替代方法：RAMP，一种通过学习灵活的非线性摊销消息传递框架来隐式定义潜在结构的方法。我们证明，RAMP能够在作用于复杂高维数据的表达性非线性模型内，高效地以基于似然的方式恢复潜变量分布。

</details>


### [179] [KALE: Kernel Alignment with Loss Equilibration for Stable CLIP-DINOv2 Alignment at Web Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18885)
*Michał Pawłowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KALE（损失均衡控制器），解决KUEA方法在噪声网络规模数据（如CC12M）上对齐项梯度失效的问题。KALE通过自适应调整对齐权重（可增大四个数量级），无需逐数据集调参，在3.3M图像子集上保持图文检索能力，SVHN线性探测提升，零样本11数据集平均提升+2.00，超过KUEA的+1.29。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于核的CLIP对齐方法（KUEA）在ImageNet-1K上有效，但当迁移到噪声、网络规模数据（CC12M）时，对齐项的加权贡献降至约0.2%，梯度在固定权重下失效。需要一种无需逐数据集调参的自动均衡机制。

Method: 提出KALE（损失均衡控制器），跟踪两个损失项并自适应地重新缩放对齐权重，使其朝目标比率恢复信号。控制器需要有界高学习率和带适度衰减的退火调度才能稳定，且控制器会均衡而非发散。

Result: 在CC12M的330万图像子集上：对齐模型保持图文检索；SVHN线性探测可重复提升；零样本在11个数据集平均提升+2.00（CLIP基线），超过KUEA的+1.29；需将对齐权重增大约四个数量级；报告了完整的运行间方差。

Conclusion: KALE通过在噪声网络数据上自适应恢复对齐信号，无需逐数据集调参，在多个指标上优于固定权重的KUEA方法，证明了损失均衡控制的有效性。

Original Abstract: Kernel-based alignment of CLIP toward a vision centric teacher such as DINOv2 (KUEA) improves CLIP's visual representations while preserving text-encoder compatibility, using a fixed trade-off weight tuned on curated ImageNet-1K. We ask whether this transfers to noisy, web-scale data (CC12M) and find that it does not: the alignment term's weighted contribution falls to about 0.2% of the clean term, so under any fixed weight its gradient is effectively inert. We introduce KALE, a loss-equilibration controller that tracks both losses and adaptively rescales the alignment weight toward a target ratio, restoring the signal with no per-dataset tuning; reaching balance requires increasing the weight by roughly four orders of magnitude, and the required value is configuration-dependent, so no fixed scalar suffices. We characterize the resulting regime: a bounded high learning rate and a decaying schedule with a moderate floor are needed for stability, and the controller equilibrates rather than diverging. On a 3.3M-image CC12M subset, the aligned model preserves image-text retrieval and reproducibly improves SVHN linear probing; zero-shot improves by +2.00 over CLIP on the standard 11-dataset average, exceeding KUEA's +1.29. We report all results with explicit run-to-run variance and base our conclusions on the metrics that are stable across runs.

中文摘要翻译: 基于核的CLIP对齐方法（KUEA）借助DINOv2等视觉中心教师模型，在固定权衡权重下（基于ImageNet-1K精心调优）改进了CLIP的视觉表示，同时保持文本编码器的兼容性。我们探究该方法是否能够迁移到含噪的网络规模数据（CC12M），结果发现不能：对齐项的加权贡献降至干净项的约0.2%，因此在任何固定权重下其梯度实际上都是惰性的。我们引入KALE，一种损失均衡控制器，它跟踪两个损失并自适应地重新缩放对齐权重，使其朝目标比率恢复信号，无需逐数据集调参；达到平衡需要将权重增加大约四个数量级，且所需值依赖于具体配置，因此没有固定的标量能够胜任。我们刻画了由此产生的运行机制：需要有界的高学习率和带适度衰减的退火调度才能保持稳定，且控制器会均衡而非发散。在330万图像的CC12M子集上，对齐模型保持了图文检索能力，并可重复地提升SVHN线性探测性能；零样本在标准11数据集平均上比CLIP提升+2.00，超过KUEA的+1.29。我们报告了所有结果及其显式的运行间方差，并基于跨运行稳定的指标得出结论。

</details>


### [180] [Breaking Feedback-Blindness: Utility-Augmented Transformer for Sequential Decision Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18910)
*Yuyang Shen,Shan Dai,Daimin Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对非平稳和部分可观测环境下Transformer决策模型存在的"反馈盲检索"结构瓶颈，提出Utility-Augmented Transformer (UAT)，通过紧凑的效用状态调节注意力查询、键、值投影，使动作-奖励历史能直接改变上下文检索，理论证明UAT严格扩展了纯观测Transformer类并能一致逼近反馈依赖的决策映射，在四个非平稳基准任务上显著优于基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有Transformer决策模型在检索机制上存在结构瓶颈：即使奖励被用于训练或作为输入token暴露，注意力检索仍主要由观测相似性驱动。作者将这一限制形式化为"反馈盲检索"（feedback-blind retrieval），并正式证明在反馈信息性任务中，观测等价但具有不同动作-奖励结果的历史无法被任何纯观测注意力区分，导致次优选择。因此需要一种新的检索机制，使动作-奖励反馈能够直接干预上下文检索过程。

Method: 提出Utility-Augmented Transformer (UAT)，一种新的反馈条件化检索注意力架构。核心思想是引入一个紧凑的效用状态（utility state），该状态对查询、键和值投影进行调节，使得动作-奖励历史能够在前向传播过程中直接改变上下文检索。UAT还具有精确的零门退化特性（exact zero-gate degradation property），当反馈无信息时能恢复为Vanilla Transformer。

Result: 在四个非平稳基准任务上验证：合成导航（隐藏目标偏移）、非平稳脓毒症治疗、跨市场投资组合分配、延迟反馈推荐。UAT在所有任务上一致优于纯观测、测试时适应和输入级反馈基线方法，在需要更强适应的噪声更严重的场景中增益尤为显著。理论上证明了UAT严格扩展了纯观测Transformer类，并能一致逼近反馈依赖的决策映射。

Conclusion: 通过引入效用状态对注意力机制进行反馈条件化，UAT有效克服了现有Transformer决策模型在非平稳部分可观测环境中的反馈盲检索问题，在理论和实验两方面均证明了其优越性，为序列决策中的反馈感知建模提供了新的架构设计思路。

Original Abstract: Sequential decision making in non-stationary and partially observable environments requires rapid adaptation to latent regime changes. However, existing Transformer decision models face a structural bottleneck in the retrieval mechanism: even when reward is used for training or exposed as an input token, attention retrieval remains primarily driven by observation-derived similarity. We formalize this limitation as feedback-blind retrieval, and formally show that, on feedback-informative tasks, observation-equivalent histories with different action-reward outcomes cannot be distinguished by any observation-only attention, resulting in suboptimal choice. To address this mismatch, we propose the Utility-Augmented Transformer (UAT), a new feedback-conditioned retrieval attention architecture in which a compact utility state modulates the query, key, and value projections, allowing action-reward history to directly alter context retrieval during the forward pass. UAT also enjoys an exact zero-gate degradation property that recovers the Vanilla Transformer when feedback is uninformative. Under finite-horizon compactness and Lipschitz assumptions, we prove that UAT strictly enlarges the observation-only Transformer class and can uniformly approximate feedback-dependent decision maps. Across four non-stationary benchmarks: synthetic navigation with hidden goal shifts, non-stationary sepsis treatment, cross-market portfolio allocation, and delayed-feedback recommendation, UAT consistently improves performance over observation-only, test-time adaptation, and input-level feedback baselines, with particularly large gains in noisier regimes that require stronger adaptation.

中文摘要翻译: 非平稳和部分可观测环境中的序列决策需要快速适应潜在的状态变化。然而，现有的Transformer决策模型在检索机制上存在一个结构性瓶颈：即使奖励被用于训练或作为输入token暴露，注意力检索仍然主要由观测驱动的相似性主导。我们将这一限制形式化为反馈盲检索，并正式证明，在反馈信息性任务中，观测等价但具有不同动作-奖励结果的历史无法被任何纯观测注意力区分，从而导致次优选择。为解决这一不匹配问题，我们提出了Utility-Augmented Transformer (UAT)，一种新的反馈条件化检索注意力架构，其中紧凑的效用状态对查询、键和值投影进行调节，使得动作-奖励历史能够在前向传播过程中直接改变上下文检索。UAT还具有精确的零门退化特性，当反馈无信息时可恢复为Vanilla Transformer。在有限时域紧致性和Lipschitz假设下，我们证明UAT严格扩展了纯观测Transformer类，并能一致逼近反馈依赖的决策映射。在四个非平稳基准任务上：合成导航（隐藏目标偏移）、非平稳脓毒症治疗、跨市场投资组合分配和延迟反馈推荐，UAT在纯观测、测试时适应和输入级反馈基线方法上均取得一致性改进，在需要更强适应的噪声更大的场景中增益尤为显著。

</details>


### [181] [Circuit Claims Depend on What Is Extracted and How It Is Compared](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18921)
*Yang Sheng,Jie Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过合成性Lean战术预测基准实验，论证了电路提取研究中“保行为即机制”的解读存在欠定性：提取结果高度依赖于报告哪个电路对象、如何比较电路以及剪枝阈值等选择，导致组件级边重叠度低至随机基线，仅粗粒度摘要（注意力头集合、电路大小排序）保持稳定。提出电路研究需明确报告对象、剪枝阈值和比较层级。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有电路提取研究默认将能保留目标行为的电路解读为背后的机制，但这一解读是欠定的：保留行为并不能唯一确定一个电路，因为所支持的论断取决于报告哪个电路以及如何比较两个电路。本文旨在揭示并形式化这一问题。

Method: 在合成性Lean策略预测基准（预测证明下一步）上，利用固定证明规则与随机化表面形式，使提取电路的差异可归因于分析选择而非任务本身。在密集和权重稀疏检查点上，针对原子性和组合性证明，变化：(1) 报告对象（紧凑预测保电路、保留读写路由结构的更宽图、或满足后剪枝损失阈值的最小子图）；(2) 注意力头查询和键的表示方式（联合或分离）。分析组件级边重叠度及粗粒度摘要的稳定性。

Result: (1) 精确的组件到组件边重叠度很低，有时降至随机基线水平；(2) 两种粗粒度摘要保持稳定：所选注意力头集合，以及监督检查点初始化强化学习（RL）时不同条件下的电路大小排序；(3) RL在组合性证明上的最大准确率提升来自于超越原子电路的最多结构。

Conclusion: 电路级论断只有在明确了报告哪个电路、提取所用剪枝阈值以及比较层级时才是有明确定义的。本文将这些要求提炼为电路提取研究的报告规范。

Original Abstract: Circuit extraction identifies a small set of model components whose presence preserves a target behavior under ablation, and the resulting circuit is often read as the mechanism behind that behavior. We argue that this reading is under-determined: preserving behavior does not single out one circuit, because the claim it supports depends on which circuit is reported and how two circuits are compared. We make this concrete in a synthetic Lean tactic-prediction benchmark -- predicting the next step of a proof -- where fixed proof rules with randomized surface form let differences between extracted circuits be attributed to these choices rather than to the task. Across dense and weight-sparse checkpoints (most weights constrained to zero) of the same transformer, evaluated on atomic (single-rule) and compositional (multi-rule) proofs, we vary which extracted object is reported (a compact prediction-preserving circuit, a broader graph that also keeps surrounding read, write, and routing structure, or the smallest subgraph meeting a post-ablation loss threshold), and whether each attention head's query and key are represented jointly or separately. Exact component-to-component edge overlap is low and sensitive to these choices, at times dropping to a random baseline, while two coarser summaries stay stable: the set of selected attention heads, and the circuit-size ranking of conditions that differ in which supervised checkpoint initializes reinforcement learning (RL). The largest accuracy gains from RL on compositional proofs come with the most structure beyond the atomic circuits. A circuit-level claim is therefore well defined only once one states which circuit is reported, the pruning threshold used to extract it, and the level at which circuits are compared. We distill these requirements into a reporting practice for circuit-extraction studies.

中文摘要翻译: 电路提取可以识别出一小部分模型组件，这些组件的存在能在消融下保留目标行为，提取出的电路通常被解读为该行为背后的机制。我们认为这种解读是欠定的：保留行为并不能唯一确定一个电路，因为其所支持的论断取决于报告哪个电路以及如何比较两个电路。我们在一个合成性Lean策略预测基准（预测证明的下一步）上具体化了这一问题——该基准中固定的证明规则与随机化的表面形式使得提取电路的差异可归因于这些选择而非任务本身。在同一个transformer的密集和权重稀疏检查点（大多数权重被约束为零）上，针对原子性（单规则）和组合性（多规则）证明进行评估，我们变化了报告哪个提取对象（紧凑的预测保留电路、同时保留周围读取/写入及路由结构的更宽图、或满足后消融损失阈值的最小子图），以及每个注意力头的查询和键是联合表示还是分别表示。精确的组件到组件边重叠度很低且对这些选择敏感，有时降至随机基线水平，而两种粗粒度摘要保持稳定：所选注意力头的集合，以及不同条件（监督检查点初始化强化学习）下电路大小的排序。强化学习在组合性证明上最大的准确率提升来自原子电路之外的最多结构。因此，只有明确了报告哪个电路、提取所用的剪枝阈值以及电路比较的层级，电路级论断才是有明确定义的。我们将这些要求提炼为电路提取研究的报告规范。

</details>


### [182] [Functional Equivalence and Geometric Diversity in Neural Network Approximations: An Empirical Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18930)
*Anuragine S A,Prem Jagadeesan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了单隐藏层神经网络在逼近基本数学函数时的功能等价性与几何多样性，揭示了存在大量功能等价但几何结构不同的网络，并提出了一种基于简约性和估计效率的模型选择标准。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管万能逼近定理保证了单隐藏层神经网络可以任意逼近连续函数，但网络表示的唯一性无法保证，这引发了关于实际可识别性的问题。本文旨在深入分析这一担忧。

Method: 通过分析单层神经网络和多层感知机在有无噪声条件下对基本数学函数逼近的功能等价性与几何多样性，利用代价函数Hessian矩阵的特征谱和有效秩来量化参数空间的维度，从“松散性”（sloppiness）视角研究几何特性。

Result: 发现了大量功能不可区分但几何结构多样的网络等价类，这些网络一致地表现出低有效秩和结构冗余性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于简约性、估计简便性和推理效率的模型选择标准，用于识别最优模型。

Original Abstract: The Universal Approximation Theorem states that a neural network with a single hidden layer is sufficient to approximate any continuous univariate function on a compact domain to arbitrary error. However, the uniqueness of such neural network representations is not guaranteed, raising questions about practical identifiability. In this work, we address this concern by analyzing functional equivalence and geometric diversity of neural network approximations to a few elementary mathematical functions. The analysis includes an extensive study of single-layer neural networks and multilayer perceptrons under noisy and noise-free conditions. Beyond just network capacity, we study the geometric properties through the lens of sloppiness, characterized by the eigen spectrum of the Hessian of the cost function and the effective rank to quantify the dimensionality of parameter space. The study reveals large equivalence classes of functionally indistinguishable yet geometrically diverse networks that consistently exhibit low effective rank and structural redundancy. Finally, a model select criterion is proposed for identifying optimal models based on parsimony, ease of estimation, and inference efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 万能逼近定理指出，具有单隐藏层的神经网络足以在紧致定义域上以任意误差逼近任何连续单变量函数。然而，此类神经网络表示的唯一性无法保证，这引发了对实际可识别性的担忧。在本工作中，我们通过分析神经网络逼近若干基本数学函数时的功能等价性和几何多样性来应对这一问题。分析包括对单层神经网络和多层感知机在含噪和无噪声条件下的广泛研究。超越网络容量本身，我们通过松散性（sloppiness）的视角研究几何特性，利用代价函数Hessian矩阵的特征谱和有效秩来量化参数空间的维度。研究揭示了存在大量功能不可区分但几何结构多样的网络等价类，这些网络一致地表现出低有效秩和结构冗余性。最后，提出了一个基于简约性、估计简便性和推理效率的模型选择标准，用于识别最优模型。

</details>


### [183] [ROMS-IMLE: A Minimalist Approach to Competitive Single-Step Generative Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19332)
*Chirag Vashist,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文质疑了“通过多步小变换逐步将噪声分布转换为数据分布”这一扩散模型的核心信念，采用极简主义方法设计生成模型。仅使用隐式最大似然估计（IMLE）作为训练目标和一个中等规模的卷积网络作为模型，构建了一个单步、参数高效的生成模型，在ImageNet 256上取得了2.56的FID。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 挑战扩散模型和流匹配中普遍存在的“需要通过多次小变换逐步将噪声分布转换为数据分布”这一主流信念，探索极简设计下能否构建具有竞争力的生成模型。

Method: 采用极简主义方法：训练目标选择隐式最大似然估计（IMLE），避免变分推断、对抗训练和数值积分等复杂方案；模型选择中等规模的卷积网络而非Transformer；仅添加真正必要的元素，不包含迭代去噪过程，最终实现单步生成。

Result: 在ImageNet 256上实现了2.56的FID，同时具有良好的精确率（precision）和召回率（recall），且模型参数效率高、生成速度快。

Conclusion: 迭代去噪并非生成模型取得优异性能的必要条件，通过极简设计（IMLE + 卷积网络）可以在单步生成中达到与扩散模型竞争的性能，挑战了领域内的主流认知。

Original Abstract: Generative models have undergone many generations of evolution, from VAEs/GANs to diffusion/flow matching. Along the way, the underlying techniques have become more complicated and various beliefs about what drives strong empirical performance have taken hold. Due to the success of diffusion models and flow matching, one of the more common beliefs is the importance of transforming the noise distribution to the data distribution gradually through many small transformations. We ask whether this is truly necessary, and take a minimalist approach to designing a competitive generative model. We start with the bare-bones essentials, namely just a training objective and a model. We purposefully make both simple. For the training objective, we choose Implicit Maximum Likelihood Estimation (IMLE), and eschew more complicated alternatives such as variational inference, adversarial training and numerical integration. For the model, we eschew transformers and instead choose a moderately sized convolutional network. Then we judiciously added elements that are truly essential, which surprisingly do not include iterative denoising. The result is a single-step parameter-efficient generative model that produces high quality samples at fast speed: it achieves an FID of 2.56 on ImageNet 256 and simultaneously attains good precision and recall.

中文摘要翻译: 生成模型已经经历了多代进化，从VAE/GAN到扩散/流匹配。在此过程中，底层技术变得愈发复杂，并且关于什么驱动了强大实证性能的各种信念也逐渐固化。由于扩散模型和流匹配的成功，一个较为普遍的信念是通过多次小变换逐步将噪声分布转换为数据分布的重要性。我们质疑这是否真的必要，并采用极简主义的方法来设计有竞争力的生成模型。我们从最基本的要素出发，即仅包含一个训练目标和一个模型。我们有意将两者都设计得简单。对于训练目标，我们选择隐式最大似然估计（IMLE），摒弃了变分推断、对抗训练和数值积分等更复杂的替代方案。对于模型，我们摒弃了Transformer，转而选择中等规模的卷积网络。然后我们审慎地添加了真正必要的元素，令人惊讶的是这些元素并不包括迭代去噪。最终得到的是一个单步、参数高效的生成模型，能够以快速生成高质量的样本：在ImageNet 256上实现了2.56的FID，同时兼顾了良好的精确率和召回率。

</details>


### [184] [H$^2$SD: Hybrid Hindsight Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18955)
*Qiye Cai,Yichuan Ma,Linyang Li,Peiji Li,Yongkang Chen,Qipeng Guo,Yicheng Zou,Tao Gui,Xiaocheng Feng,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H²SD是一种混合事后自我蒸馏框架，根据轨迹正确性采用不同的教师策略：对成功轨迹用学生正确响应调节更新幅度，对失败轨迹用参考提示进行反向KL散度最小化，在数学推理和代码生成任务上优于RLVR、OPSD和RLSD等基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有RLVR方法使用稀疏的标量结果奖励，导致令牌级信用分配受限；OPD需要额外教师模型且假设共享词汇表；OPSD存在信息泄露和不稳定优化问题；RLSD无法在采样推理失败时提供显式纠正方向。H²SD旨在解决这些方法的权衡问题。

Method: 提出H²SD（Hybrid Hindsight Self-Distillation）框架，根据轨迹正确性区分处理：对于成功轨迹，将学生确认正确的响应与改写指令一起输入教师，用教师对学生原始响应令牌的概率调节更新幅度但不改变奖励确定的方向；对于失败轨迹，将教师条件设置为包含关键推理步骤和验证答案的参考提示，最小化学生到教师的逆向KL散度。

Result: 在多个具有挑战性的推理基准上，H²SD一致优于代表性的RLVR、OPSD和RLSD基线方法，同时保持稳定的优化和良好的生成效率。

Conclusion: H²SD通过混合事后自我蒸馏策略，成功解决了现有方法在监督密度与优化稳定性之间的权衡问题，为提升大语言模型推理能力提供了一种更优的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has substantially improved the reasoning capabilities of large language models on tasks such as mathematical reasoning and code generation. However, most RLVR methods assign a scalar outcome reward to an entire trajectory, resulting in sparse supervision and limited token-level credit assignment. On-policy distillation (OPD) provides denser supervision by distilling token-level distributions from a stronger teacher model, but requires an additional teacher and typically assumes a shared vocabulary. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) removes this dependency by conditioning the same model on privileged information to construct a teacher policy. However, directly matching the teacher distribution may cause information leakage and unstable optimization. RLSD avoids direct matching by using the teacher signal only to modulate update magnitudes, but it cannot provide an explicit correction direction when the sampled reasoning fails. To address this tradeoff, we introduce $\mathrm{H}^{2}\mathrm{SD}$, a hybrid hindsight self distillation framework that uses the teacher differently according to trajectory correctness. For successful trajectories, the teacher receives the student response confirmed as correct together with a rephrasing instruction, and its probabilities on the original response tokens are used to modulate update magnitudes without changing the direction determined by the reward. For failed trajectories, we condition the teacher on a reference hint containing key reasoning steps and a verified answer, and minimize the reverse KL divergence from the student to the teacher. Experiments on multiple challenging reasoning benchmarks show that H$^2$SD consistently outperforms representative RLVR, OPSD, and RLSD baselines while maintaining stable optimization and favorable generation efficiency.

中文摘要翻译: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）已显著提升了大语言模型在数学推理和代码生成等任务上的推理能力。然而，大多数RLVR方法将标量结果奖励分配给整个轨迹，导致监督稀疏且令牌级信用分配有限。在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过从更强的教师模型中蒸馏令牌级分布来提供更密集的监督，但需要额外的教师模型并且通常假设共享词汇表。在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）通过将同一模型基于特权信息进行条件化来构建教师策略，从而消除了这一依赖。然而，直接匹配教师分布可能导致信息泄露和不稳定优化。RLSD通过仅使用教师信号来调节更新幅度来避免直接匹配，但当采样推理失败时无法提供显式的纠正方向。为了解决这一权衡问题，我们引入了H²SD，一种混合事后自我蒸馏框架，它根据轨迹正确性以不同方式使用教师。对于成功轨迹，教师接收被确认为正确的学生响应以及改写指令，其对原始响应令牌的概率被用于调节更新幅度，而不改变由奖励决定的方向。对于失败轨迹，我们将教师条件设置为包含关键推理步骤和验证答案的参考提示，并最小化从学生到教师的逆向KL散度。在多个具有挑战性的推理基准上的实验表明，H²SD在保持稳定优化和良好生成效率的同时，一致优于代表性的RLVR、OPSD和RLSD基线方法。

</details>


### [185] [SFGA: A Statistics-First Gating Architecture with Adjudicative Escalation for Trustworthy SFT Data Procurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18960)
*Arther Tian,Alex Ding,Simon Wu,Aaron Chan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种统计优先的门控架构 ProcureLLM，将监督微调数据采购视为基于多样性、实用性和冗余性三个质量维度的成本感知路由问题，通过廉价盲测与置信区间判断，在不确定时触发辩论机制，以低预算实现接近最优的数据采购决策。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 监督微调（SFT）数据采购面临决策困境：买方在下游训练前必须决定候选语料库是否值得获取，而传统方法要么盲目接受、要么全部验证，成本与准确性难以兼顾。因此需要一种在有限预算下高效、可靠地评估数据质量的采购架构。

Method: 提出 ProcureLLM 门控架构：（1）对候选数据沿多样性（diversity）、实用性（utility）、冗余性（redundancy）三个轴进行廉价盲测，汇总成带置信区间的估计；（2）门控单元仅在置信区间紧凑、样本量充足且三个轴一致时才做出决策，否则将案例升级至"购买-辩护"与"拒绝-辩护"双方的对抗辩论，由主审法官裁决。

Result: 在12个数据集（2×3×2网格覆盖三个轴）的受控基准上，门控架构以每单位$0.017的成本达到0.90准确率和0.83 F1分数，介于"始终验证"基线（0.75）和"理想上界"（0.98）之间，且成本低于"始终升级"基线（$0.020）。辩论路径分析显示：反方胜率0.80（p≈3×10⁻⁶），立场翻转率52%，暴露了朴素LLM裁判难以发现的消极性与立场偏见。

Conclusion: ProcureLLM 在可控的合成基准上展示了良好的测量保真度和路由校准能力，以较低预算实现了接近最优的采购决策质量；同时揭示了辩论机制中存在的消极偏差和立场偏见，证明了透明诊断的必要性；外部有效性留待未来工作验证。

Original Abstract: Procuring supervised fine-tuning (SFT) data forces a buyer to decide, before any downstream training, whether a candidate corpus is worth acquiring. We present \sys{}, a statistics-first gating architecture that treats procurement as a cost-aware routing problem over three intrinsic quality axes -- diversity, utility, and redundancy. Cheap blind measurements are summarised into per-axis estimates with confidence intervals; a gate accepts a decision only when intervals are tight, sample sizes are adequate, and the axes agree, otherwise it escalates the case to an adjudicative debate between a buy-advocate and a reject-advocate judge, resolved by a presiding verdict. On a controlled benchmark of 12 datasets ($2{\times}3{\times}2$ grid over the three axes) with 5 seeds, the gate reaches 0.90 accuracy and 0.83 $F_1$ at \$0.017 per unit, sitting between an always-verify baseline (0.75) and an oracle upper bound (0.98) while spending less than always-escalate (\$0.020). We further report honest negative diagnostics of the debate path: a con-side win rate of 0.80 ($p\approx3{\times}10^{-6}$) and a 52\% position-flip rate under advocate swapping expose negativity and positional biases that a naive LLM-judge would hide. We frame the injected-knob evaluation explicitly as a controlled synthetic benchmark for measurement fidelity and routing calibration, and delimit external validity as future work.

中文摘要翻译: 获取监督微调（SFT）数据要求买方在下游训练开始之前就决定候选语料库是否值得获取。本文提出 ProcureLLM——一种统计优先的门控架构，将采购问题视为一个在三个内在质量轴（多样性、实用性和冗余性）上的成本感知路由问题。廉价的盲测被汇总为带置信区间的逐轴估计；只有当置信区间紧凑、样本量充足且三个轴的意见一致时，门控才接受决策，否则将案件升级至"购买-辩护"与"拒绝-辩护"两位裁判之间的对抗辩论，由主审法官裁决。在覆盖三个轴的12个数据集（2×3×2网格）的受控基准上，以5种随机种子进行实验，门控以每单位0.017美元的成本达到0.90准确率和0.83 F1分数，介于"始终验证"基线（0.75）与"理想上界"（0.98）之间，且成本低于"始终升级"基线（0.020美元）。我们进一步报告了辩论路径的诚实负面诊断：反方胜率0.80（p≈3×10⁻⁶），以及在裁判立场互换下52%的立场翻转率，这些暴露了朴素LLM裁判会隐藏的消极性与立场偏见。我们明确地将注入旋钮式评估构建为用于测量保真度和路由校准的可控合成基准，并将外部有效性界定为未来工作。

</details>


### [186] [Variational meta-learning inference for low dimensional neural system identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.18965)
*Matteo Rufolo,Dario Piga,Marco Forgione*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于摊销变分推断的完全概率化流形元学习框架，将低维参数流形上的生成先验与最大后验估计及拉普拉斯近似相结合，在极低数据条件下实现非线性系统辨识的校准不确定性量化。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流形元学习框架虽然通过将模型参数约束在低维流形上解决了数据效率问题，但该方法是纯确定性的，缺乏可靠的不确定性量化能力，在极低数据场景下容易过拟合。

Method: 提出流形元学习框架的完全概率化扩展：基于摊销变分推断学习低维参数流形上的生成先验；在任务适配阶段，结合最大后验估计与拉普拉斯近似，得到有数学基础的后验近似。

Result: 在静态回归任务和Bouc-Wen动力系统基准测试上，所提方法在预测精度上与确定性版本相当，同时在极低数据场景下成功提供了校准的不确定性界限。

Conclusion: 该概率化流形元学习方法有效地将不确定性量化能力引入低数据体制下的非线性系统辨识，兼顾了预测精度与可靠性。

Original Abstract: Deep learning has proven highly effective for nonlinear system identification, but heavily parameterized neural networks are prone to overfitting in low-data regimes and lack reliable uncertainty quantification. The recently developed manifold meta-learning framework addresses the data efficiency problem by restricting the model parameters to a meta-learned low-dimensional manifold. However, that method is purely deterministic. We propose a fully probabilistic extension of the manifold meta-learning framework, based on amortized Variational Inference, where a generative prior over the low-dimensional parameter manifold is learned. During task-specific adaptation, we combine Maximum A Posteriori estimation with the Laplace approximation to yield a mathematically grounded posterior approximation. Evaluated on a static regression task and the Bouc--Wen dynamical system benchmark, the proposed approach achieves predictive accuracy comparable to its deterministic counterpart while successfully providing calibrated uncertainty bounds in severely low-data regimes.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习已被证明在非线性系统辨识中非常有效，但高度参数化的神经网络在低数据场景下容易过拟合，且缺乏可靠的不确定性量化。最近发展的流形元学习框架通过将模型参数约束在元学习的低维流形上，解决了数据效率问题。然而，该方法是纯确定性的。我们提出了一种基于摊销变分推断的流形元学习框架的完全概率化扩展，其中学习了低维参数流形上的生成先验。在任务特定适配阶段，我们将最大后验估计与拉普拉斯近似相结合，得到有数学基础的后验近似。在静态回归任务和Bouc-Wen动力系统基准测试上的评估表明，所提方法在预测精度上与其确定性版本相当，同时在极低数据场景下成功提供了校准的不确定性界限。

</details>


### [187] [Subject-Conditioned Glucose Forecasting in Type-1 Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19006)
*Giorgia Rigamonti,Mirko Paolo Barbato,Davide Marelli,Paolo Napoletano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为SCGP的多模态深度学习架构，通过显式的个体条件建模实现个性化血糖预测，在多个预测时间窗口上显著优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有血糖预测方法多依赖群体层面表征或隐式个性化策略，无法提供有效个体化预测，亟需一种能显式捕捉个体间差异的预测方法。

Method: 提出SCGP（Subject-Conditioned Glucose Prediction）框架，将个体特征表征与血糖动态建模显式分离，通过个体特定表征对血糖预测进行条件约束，避免早期融合异构输入，从而有效捕捉个体间变异性并保持稳健的时间建模能力。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的实验表明，SCGP在多个预测时间窗口上持续提升预测性能，并能可靠检测不良血糖事件。

Conclusion: 显式的个体条件建模对于个性化糖尿病管理具有显著优势，SCGP框架能够有效提升血糖预测的个体化水平。

Original Abstract: Accurate forecasting of blood glucose concentration is key in the management of Type 1 Diabetes, facilitating early detection of adverse glycemic events and supporting timely therapeutic interventions. Despite recent advances in glucose prediction, most existing approaches rely on population-level representations or implicit personalization strategies that fail to deliver effective subject-specific forecasts. In this work, we propose Subject-Conditioned Glucose Prediction (SCGP), a novel multimodal deep learning architecture conceived for personalized blood glucose prediction. SCGP conditions glucose predictions based on observed glucose data and a compact subject-specific representation learned from contextual information. By explicitly separating subject characterization from glucose dynamics modeling and avoiding early fusion of heterogeneous inputs, the proposed framework effectively captures inter-subject variability while preserving robust and reliable temporal modeling. Experiments on two state-of-the-art benchmark datasets demonstrate that SCGP consistently improves forecasting performance, enabling reliable detection of adverse glycemic events across multiple prediction horizons, highlighting the benefits of explicit subject conditioning for personalized diabetes management.

中文摘要翻译: 准确的血糖浓度预测是1型糖尿病管理的关键，有助于及早发现不良血糖事件并支持及时的治疗干预。尽管近年血糖预测取得了进展，但大多数现有方法依赖群体层面的表征或隐式个性化策略，无法提供有效的个体化预测。本文提出Subject-Conditioned Glucose Prediction（SCGP），一种新颖的多模态深度学习架构，专为个性化血糖预测设计。SCGP基于观测到的血糖数据以及从上下文信息中学习的紧凑型个体表征来条件约束血糖预测。通过将个体特征表征与血糖动态建模显式分离，并避免异构输入的早期融合，该框架有效捕捉了个体间变异性，同时保持了稳健可靠的时间建模。在两个先进的基准数据集上的实验表明，SCGP在多个预测时间窗口上持续提升了预测性能，能够可靠检测不良血糖事件，突显了显式个体条件建模在个性化糖尿病管理中的优势。

</details>


### [188] [Biological Amnesia in ICU Time-Series Prediction: A Drift-Adaptive Two-Stream Architecture with Temporal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19020)
*Fatema Ferdous Tamanna,K. M. Merajul Arefin,Md. Abdul Masud*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结构解耦的自适应临床智能架构，将参数更新限制在治疗表征流上，结合双重触发机制和归因驱动的时序RAG模块，使临床AI能随实践演变而不扭曲已学习的患者生理学知识。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 临床决策支持系统随着治疗方案的演变会无声地退化，但标准的适配方法将模型视为整体模块，无法区分稳定的患者生理学与变化的机构实践。

Method: 提出一种自适应的临床智能架构，结构性地将生理表征与治疗表征解耦，通过双重分布和精度触发将参数更新限制在治疗流上；自动审计日志记录每个适配事件的治疗特征驱动力及其重要性变化；推理时通过归因驱动的时序RAG模块，将每个预测锚定到患者主导生理特征对应的时代匹配PubMed证据上。

Result: 漂移完全定位在治疗流上；选择性适配在血管升压药和脓毒性休克判别及校准上优于静态源模型；完整重训练的基线在总体判别上略高但漏掉了框架正确识别的26例脓毒性休克病例且无反例；框架保留了与适配前源模型的检索一致性，而重训练基线严重退化。

Conclusion: 将适配结构性约束在漂移组件上同时保留稳定的生理表征，能使临床AI随实践演变而不扭曲已学习的患者生物学特征；该架构为高风险临床环境中可治理、可解释的自适应模型部署提供了模板。

Original Abstract: Background: Clinical decision support systems degrade silently as treatment protocols evolve, yet standard adaptation methods treat models as monolithic blocks, unable to distinguish stable patient physiology from shifting institutional practice. Methods: We propose an adaptive clinical intelligence architecture for ICU intervention prediction that structurally decouples physiological from treatment representations, confining parameter updates to the treatment stream upon a dual distributional and accuracy trigger. Automated audit logs record which treatment features drove each adaptation event and how their importance shifted. At inference, an attribution-driven Temporal RAG module grounds each prediction in patient-specific, era-matched PubMed evidence anchored to the patient's dominant physiological features. Experiments used 84,792 MIMIC-IV stays (2008-2022) under strict chronological split. Results: Drift localised entirely to the treatment stream, validating the structural prior. Selective adaptation improved vasopressor and septic shock discrimination and calibration over the static source model. A fully retrained baseline yielded marginally higher aggregate discrimination but missed 26 septic shock cases the framework correctly identified, with none in the reverse direction; retrieval consistency with the pre-adaptation source model was preserved by the framework but degraded substantially in the retrained baseline. Conclusions: Structurally constraining adaptation to drifting components while preserving stable physiological representations enables clinical AI to evolve with practice without distorting learned patient biology. This architecture offers a template for governable, interpretable deployment of adaptive models in high-stakes clinical environments.

中文摘要翻译: 背景：临床决策支持系统随着治疗方案的演变而无声退化，然而标准的适配方法将模型视为整体模块，无法区分稳定的患者生理学与变化的机构实践。方法：我们提出一种面向ICU干预预测的自适应临床智能架构，该架构从结构上将生理表征与治疗表征解耦，并通过双重分布性和准确性触发机制将参数更新限制在治疗流上。自动审计日志记录每个适配事件由哪些治疗特征驱动以及这些特征的重要性如何变化。在推理时，归因驱动的时序RAG模块将每个预测锚定到患者特定、时代匹配的PubMed证据，该证据与患者的主导生理特征相关联。实验使用84,792例MIMIC-IV住院记录（2008-2022年），采用严格的时间顺序划分。结果：漂移完全定位在治疗流上，验证了结构先验的有效性。选择性适配在血管升压药和脓毒性休克判别及校准上优于静态源模型。完整重训练的基线在总体判别上略高，但遗漏了框架正确识别的26例脓毒性休克病例，且无反例；框架保持了与适配前源模型的检索一致性，而重训练基线严重退化。结论：将适配结构性约束在漂移组件上同时保留稳定的生理表征，能使临床AI随实践演变而不扭曲已学习的患者生物学特征。该架构为高风险临床环境中可治理、可解释的自适应模型部署提供了模板。

</details>


### [189] [Unsupervised Multi-kernel Learning for Automated Algorithm Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19031)
*Yihang Lu,Tome Eftimov,Carola Doerr*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的多核聚类方法用于黑箱优化中的算法选择，该方法在不使用性能标签的情况下对问题实例进行聚类，并通过三阶段评估协议映射到求解器推荐。在DE和PSO选择器任务上，多核聚类取得了有竞争力的结果，并揭示了可解释的核权重分配。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统的监督式算法选择模型训练成本高、依赖基准数据集、且难以泛化到未见问题类。本文旨在探索一种无需性能标签的无监督替代方案，降低对标注数据的依赖，同时提升跨问题类的泛化能力。

Method: 采用多核k-means聚类框架，联合学习聚类分配和四种异构景观表示（ELA、DeepELA、DoE2Vec、TransOptAS）上的核权重。聚类阶段不使用性能标签，随后通过严格分离的三阶段评估协议将聚类结果后验地映射为求解器推荐。

Result: 在固定评估预算下，针对差分进化（DE）和粒子群优化（PSO）的仿射BBOB选择器任务，多核聚类在DE组合上获得了最强平均选择器性能，在PSO组合上与领先基线方法持平或略优。可视化显示，学到的核权重保留了ELA和TransOptAS，而将DeepELA和DoE2Vec的权重置零。

Conclusion: 多核聚类是一种有效的无监督算法选择方法，能够在无需性能标签的情况下实现有竞争力的选择器性能，同时通过核权重学习提供任务特定的可解释性，揭示了不同景观表示对特定优化任务的贡献差异。

Original Abstract: Automated algorithm selection in black-box optimization typically relies on supervised models that map landscape features to algorithm performance labels. Such models are costly to train, benchmark-dependent, and often fail to generalize to unseen problem classes. We study an unsupervised alternative: multi-kernel clustering over heterogeneous landscape representations, in which problem instances are grouped without using performance labels in the clustering stage, and the resulting clusters are mapped post hoc to solver recommendations through a strictly separated three-stage evaluation protocol. Drawing on two decades of advances in multiple kernel learning, we adopt a multi-kernel k-means formulation that jointly learns cluster assignments and kernel weights over four heterogeneous landscape views: ELA, DeepELA, DoE2Vec, and TransOptAS. On affine BBOB-derived selector tasks for Differential Evolution (DE) and Particle Swarm Optimization (PSO) at a fixed evaluation budget, we report mean plus or minus standard deviation selector profiles over 50 independent random seeds for stochastic configurations. Multi-kernel clustering obtains the strongest mean profile on the DE portfolio and remains competitive with, and nominally ahead of, the leading baselines on the more compressed PSO portfolio, where differences among the best methods are small relative to stochastic variation. In representative median-seed runs used for visualization, the learned kernel weights retain ELA and TransOptAS while assigning zero weight to DeepELA and DoE2Vec, providing a task-specific interpretation of which representations are retained by the multi-kernel model for selector-oriented grouping.

中文摘要翻译: 黑箱优化中的自动化算法选择通常依赖于监督模型，这些模型将景观特征映射到算法性能标签上。这类模型训练成本高、依赖基准数据集，且通常难以泛化到未见问题类。本文研究了一种无监督替代方案：基于异构景观表示的多核聚类，在聚类阶段不使用性能标签对问题实例进行分组，随后通过严格分离的三阶段评估协议将所得聚类后验地映射为求解器推荐。借鉴过去二十多年多核学习的进展，我们采用多核k-means公式，在四种异构景观视图（ELA、DeepELA、DoE2Vec和TransOptAS）上联合学习聚类分配和核权重。在固定评估预算下，针对差分进化（DE）和粒子群优化（PSO）的仿射BBOB衍生选择器任务，我们报告了随机配置下50次独立随机种子的均值±标准差选择器轮廓。多核聚类在DE组合上获得了最强的平均轮廓，并在更为压缩的PSO组合上与领先基线方法保持竞争且名义上领先，其中最佳方法之间的差异相对于随机波动较小。在用于可视化的代表性中位数种子运行中，学习到的核权重保留了ELA和TransOptAS，而将DeepELA和DoE2Vec的权重置为零，提供了多核模型为面向选择器的分组保留了哪些表示的任务特定解释。

</details>


### [190] [Spectral Higher-Order Neural Networks Have Sharp Expressivity Bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19042)
*Gianluca Peri,Diego Febbe,Duccio Fanelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于频谱属性的神经超图参数化方法（SHONNs），通过权重共享机制减少参数爆炸，并在N位奇偶校验任务上验证了其性能与可解释性优势。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统神经超图因加权超边数量过多导致参数爆炸，计算成本高昂，难以实际部署。

Method: 利用频谱属性进行参数化，通过权重共享机制回收参数，从而显著降低计算成本。

Result: 在N位奇偶校验任务上，SHONNs展现了性能与可解释性的显著提升，假设空间具有高度灵活性和可调性。

Conclusion: 频谱高阶神经网络（SHONNs）拥有一个多用途且高度可调的假设空间，是一种高效的神经超图架构。

Original Abstract: Neural hypergraphs are a natural generalization of neural networks, the reference models in modern machine learning. Yet, their deployment has proven demanding: the number of weighted hyperedges required leads to an intractable parameter explosion. However, a novel parametrization that leverages spectral attributes for neural hypergraphs has been recently proposed, that enables to recycle parameters via a weight sharing scheme and consequently yields a significant reduction of the associated computational cost. Preliminary tests carried out on spectral higher-order architectures pointed to meaningful improvements in both performance and interpretability. Building on these results, we advance the benchmarking efforts by evaluating the spectral higher order framework on N-bit parity tasks, a well-established testbed known to be particularly challenging. As we will convincingly argue, Spectral Higher-Order Neural Networks (SHONNs) possess a versatile and highly tunable hypothesis space.

中文摘要翻译: 神经超图是现代机器学习中参考模型——神经网络的自然推广。然而，其部署已被证明具有挑战性：加权超边的数量导致难以处理的参数爆炸。但最近提出了一种利用频谱属性进行神经超图参数化的新方法，该方法通过权重共享机制实现参数回收，从而显著降低了相关计算成本。在频谱高阶架构上进行的初步测试表明，其在性能和可解释性方面均有显著提升。基于这些结果，我们通过在N位奇偶校验任务（一个公认具有挑战性的测试基准）上评估频谱高阶框架，推进了基准测试工作。令人信服的是，我们将证明频谱高阶神经网络（SHONNs）拥有一个多用途且高度可调的假设空间。

</details>


### [191] [Adopting Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards for Molecular Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19044)
*Mingxuan Ouyang,Hao Lan,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LLMol框架，将分子生成任务建模为目标条件序列预测，采用监督微调+基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR+GRPO）两阶段训练范式，在单属性目标优化和结构约束优化任务上优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于LLM的分子生成方法主要依赖有限数据集的监督训练或微调，难以捕获复杂分子设计目标；部分方法虽尝试引导生成，但缺乏直接优化机制，生成分子难以对齐期望属性。

Method: 两阶段训练框架：(1) 监督微调阶段，让LLM学习化学语法和分子分布；(2) 基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）阶段，采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) 稳定策略优化算法，直接整合属性奖励信号引导分子生成。

Result: 在多种分子设计基准上（包括penalized logP、QED单属性优化和结构约束优化），LLMol一致优于现有方法，实现更高的成功率和更好的效率。

Conclusion: 通过将可验证奖励直接融入强化学习框架，LLMol有效解决了分子生成中属性对齐的难题，为基于LLM的靶向分子设计提供了一种稳健且高效的解决方案。

Original Abstract: Leveraging large language models (LLMs) for molecular generation has shown remarkable potential in chemical and drug design. Current methods primarily rely on supervised training or fine-tuning with limited datasets, which are insufficient to capture complex molecular design objectives. While some approaches attempt to guide generation toward specific goals, they often lack direct optimization mechanisms, making it difficult to align generated molecules with desired properties. To tackle these challenges, we propose \textbf{LLMol}, a principled reinforcement learning framework that directly incorporates verifiable rewards for targeted molecule generation. The key insight is to formulate molecular design as a goal-conditioned sequence prediction task, where verifiable rewards serve as explicit supervision to drive generation toward desired objectives. LLMol follows a two-stage training paradigm combining supervised learning and reinforcement learning. In the first stage, large language models are supervised fine-tuned to capture chemical syntax and molecular distributions. In the second stage, we introduce Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR), which directly integrates property-based reward signals to guide molecular generation toward task-specific objectives. To address the high variance and instability common in discrete sequence optimization, we adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), a stable on-policy algorithm that smooths reward signals and improves training robustness. This framework enables LLMol to effectively handle a range of molecular design tasks, including single-property targeting (e.g., penalized logP, QED) and structure-constrained optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that LLMol consistently outperforms existing methods, achieving higher success rates and improved efficiency across diverse molecular benchmarks.

中文摘要翻译: 利用大型语言模型（LLMs）进行分子生成在化学和药物设计中展现出显著潜力。当前方法主要依赖于有限数据集上的监督训练或微调，难以捕获复杂的分子设计目标。虽然部分方法尝试引导生成朝向特定目标，但它们通常缺乏直接的优化机制，导致生成的分子难以与期望属性对齐。为应对这些挑战，我们提出\textbf{LLMol}——一个将可验证奖励直接融入靶向分子生成的原则性强化学习框架。核心思想是将分子设计建模为目标条件序列预测任务，其中可验证奖励作为显式监督信号驱动生成朝向期望目标。LLMol采用监督学习与强化学习相结合的两阶段训练范式：第一阶段对大型语言模型进行监督微调，以捕获化学语法和分子分布；第二阶段引入基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），直接整合基于属性的奖励信号，引导分子生成朝向任务特定目标。为解决离散序列优化中常见的高方差和不稳定问题，我们采用组相对策略优化（GRPO），一种平滑奖励信号并提升训练鲁棒性的稳定在策略算法。该框架使LLMol能够有效处理多种分子设计任务，包括单属性目标优化（如penalized logP、QED）和结构约束优化。实验结果表明，LLMol在多种分子基准任务上一致优于现有方法，实现了更高成功率和更优效率。

</details>


### [192] [Probabilistic Physics-Aware Machine Learning Predictions of Electric Truck Energy Consumption with Field Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19054)
*Hannes Nilsson,Rafael Basso,Balázs Kulcsár,Morteza Haghir Chehreghani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 将第一性原理物理模型融入数据驱动方法，基于物理感知模型进行贝叶斯线性回归和复杂机器学习（神经网络、梯度提升回归树）的能耗预测，结果表明该方法相比标准模型显著提升了预测精度和不确定性估计的可靠性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有数据驱动的能耗预测方法缺乏物理机理的约束，导致预测可靠性和泛化能力不足，亟需将物理先验知识融入模型以提高预测的准确性和可信度。

Method: 提出一种物理感知模型，该模型考虑了车辆运行过程中不同能量损失来源；基于此模型分别采用贝叶斯线性回归、神经网络和梯度提升回归树进行能耗预测，并开发了预测标准差形式的不确定性估计框架。

Result: 物理感知的贝叶斯线性回归相比标准线性回归提高了能耗预测可靠性；基于同一物理模型的神经网络和梯度提升回归树在预测精度上显著优于其标准版本；所有模型在不确定性估计方面均表现良好。

Conclusion: 将第一性原理物理模型融入机器学习方法能够有效提升能耗预测的精度和可靠性，同时模型能够合理估计预测的不确定性。

Original Abstract: In this work, we incorporate first principle physics into the construction of data-driven methods by considering a model that accounts for the different sources of energy losses during vehicle operations. Our results show that Bayesian linear regression based on this physics-aware model can improve the reliability of the expected energy consumption, as compared with standard linear regression. Further, it is shown that more complex machine learning models such as neural networks and gradient boosted regression trees, based on the same physical model, can further improve the accuracy in energy forecasting and significantly outperform standard versions of the same machine learning models. In addition to point predictions of the energy consumption, we develop a framework for estimating the corresponding uncertainty in the form of predicted standard deviation. Our results show that all of the models learn to estimate the uncertainty reasonably well.

中文摘要翻译: 在本工作中，我们将第一性原理物理模型融入数据驱动方法的构建中，通过考虑一个能够解释车辆运行过程中不同能量损失源的模型来进行分析。结果表明，基于该物理感知模型的贝叶斯线性回归相比标准线性回归能够提高能耗期望预测的可靠性。进一步，基于同一物理模型的更复杂机器学习模型（如神经网络和梯度提升回归树）能够进一步提升能耗预测的精度，并显著优于相应标准机器学习模型。除了能耗的点预测外，我们还开发了一个以预测标准差形式估计相应不确定性的框架。结果表明，所有模型均能较好地学习估计不确定性。

</details>


### [193] [Where Should Optimizer State Live? Tiered State Allocation for Memory-Efficient Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.19058)
*Nuemaan Malik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SkewAdam为MoE训练提出了一种分参数种群、差异化分配优化器状态的方案：对稠密主干使用float32动量+分解二阶矩，对专家仅使用分解二阶矩，对路由器使用精确二阶矩。相比AdamW，优化器状态从50.6GB降至1.29GB（2.6%），峰值训练内存从81.4GB降至31.3GB，且在验证困惑度上超越AdamW、Muon、Lion等基线。研究表明优化器状态的存放位置与存放总量同等重要。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: MoE训练中优化器状态（AdamW的一阶和二阶矩）占据最大内存开销（6.78B参数模型上50.6GB vs 12.6GB权重），而MoE的三个参数种群（稠密主干、专家、路由器）在规模和梯度统计上差异巨大，不应共享相同的优化器状态设置。

Method: 提出SkewAdam优化器，为MoE的不同参数种群分配差异化状态：稠密主干（5%参数）保留float32动量+分解二阶矩；专家（95%参数）仅保留分解二阶矩；路由器（<0.01%）保留精确二阶矩。通过这种非对称分配大幅压缩优化器状态内存。

Result: 在6.78B MoE模型上，SkewAdam的优化器状态仅1.29GB（AdamW的2.6%），峰值训练内存从81.4GB降至31.3GB（可放入40GB加速器）。在82M tokens的受控对比中，验证困惑度108.4，优于AdamW（126.8）、Muon（120.2）、Lion（393.7）；路由器负载均衡度在均匀下限1%以内。消融实验表明分层分配省内存但不损精度，精度来源于动量机制。

Conclusion: 优化器状态在MoE不同参数种群中的存放位置（location）至少与其总量（quantity）同等重要。SkewAdam通过精准分配状态实现了数量级的内存节省，同时保持甚至提升模型性能。

Original Abstract: Optimizer state is the largest single line item in the memory budget of mixture-of-experts (MoE) training: on a 6.78B-parameter MoE language model, AdamW keeps 50.6 GB of first and second moments to update 12.6 GB of bfloat16 weights. We study SkewAdam, an optimizer built on the observation that the three parameter populations of an MoE - the dense backbone, the experts, and the router - differ enough in size and gradient statistics that they should not receive the same state. SkewAdam keeps float32 momentum plus a factored second moment for the backbone (5% of parameters), a factored second moment alone for the experts (95%), and an exact second moment for the router (<0.01%). The resulting state occupies 1.29 GB, 2.6% of AdamW's, and peak training memory falls from 81.4 GB to 31.3 GB, within the budget of a 40 GB accelerator. In a controlled comparison from identical initializations over 82M tokens, SkewAdam reaches validation perplexity 108.4, ahead of AdamW (126.8), Muon (120.2), and Lion (393.7), and settles router load balance to within 1% of its uniform floor. The allocation is not what earns that perplexity: a tier ablation matches it with twenty times the state, and Adafactor, which shares the factored estimator but drops momentum, plateaus 40 points behind. The tiers buy memory at no cost to accuracy; the accuracy comes from keeping momentum, which a uniform optimizer shares too. Sweeping the baselines' learning rates narrows but does not close the gap: the best tuned AdamW reaches 118.5, tuned Adafactor 139.7. Where optimizer state lives, these results suggest, matters at least as much as how much of it there is.
